## `checklists` — 4 extracted, showing 3 representatives

Sources: APF Odisha, Animedh Charitable Trust DNH, Ashwini uat, JNPCT

### `Ashwini uat` · `form[Child Enrolment]`  (606 bytes)

```javascript
//SAMPLE RULE EXAMPLE
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
  const programEnrolment = params.entity;
  const checklistDetails = params.checklistDetails;
  
  const vaccinationDetails = checklistDetails.find(cd => cd.name === 'Vaccination');
    if (vaccinationDetails === undefined) return [];
        const vaccinationList = {
            baseDate: programEnrolment.individual.dateOfBirth,
            detail: {uuid: vaccinationDetails.uuid},
            items: vaccinationDetails.items.map(vi => ({
                detail: {uuid: vi.uuid}
            }))
        };
        return [vaccinationList];
};
```

### `APF Odisha` · `form[Child Enrolment]`  (407 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
  let vaccination = params.checklistDetails.find(cd => cd.name === 'Vaccination');
  if (vaccination === undefined) return [];
  

  const vaccinationList = {
    baseDate: params.entity.individual.dateOfBirth,
    detail: {uuid: vaccination.uuid},
    items: vaccination.items.map(vi => ({
      detail: {uuid: vi.uuid}
    }))
  };

  return [vaccinationList];
  
};
```

```javascript
//SAMPLE RULE EXAMPLE
  "use strict";
({params, imports}) => {
    const programEncounter = params.entity;
    const decisions = params.decisions;    
    //create complicationBuilder using programEncounter and then push to encounterDecisions array.
     const lmpDate = programEncounter.programEnrolment.getObservationValue('Last menstrual period');
     const pregnancyPeriodInWeeks = imports.moment(programEncounter.encounterDateTime).diff(lmpDate,'weeks');
     
     const systolic = programEncounter.getObservationValue('Systolic');
     const diastolic = programEncounter.getObservationValue('Diastolic');
     const isBloodPressureHigh = (systolic >= 140) || (diastolic >= 90); 

     
let highRiskConditions = programEncounter.programEnrolment.getObservationReadableValueInEntireEnrolment('High Risk Conditions');
const isEssentialHypertensive = highRiskConditions && highRiskConditions.indexOf('Essential Hypertension') >= 0;

const obsHistory = programEncounter.programEnrolment.getObservationReadableValueInEntireEnrolment('Obstetrics history');
const obsHistoryOfPregnancyInducedHypertension = imports.common.contains(obsHistory, 'Pregnancy induced hypertension');
         

  const medicalHistory = ["Hypertension", "Heart-related Diseases", "Diabetes", "Sickle Cell", "Epilepsy", 
   "Renal Disease","HIV/AIDS", "Hepatitis B Positive"];

const pastComplications = ["Ante Partum Haemorrhage", "Intrauterine Growth Retardation", "Pre-term labour", 
    "Prolonged labour","Instrumental Delivery", "LSCS/C-section", "Intrauterine death", "Threatened abortion",
    "3 or more than 3 spontaneous abortions", "Still Birth", "Multiple Births", "Retained Placenta",
    "Post Partum Haemorrhage (Excessive bleeding after delivery)", "Intrapartum Death", "Neonatal death within first 28 days","Congenital anomaly", "Rh negative in the previous pregnancy"];
  
  const investigations = [["Paracheck", [1, 2, 3]], ["Hb", [1, 2, 3]], 
        ["VDRL", [1]], ["HIV/AIDS Test", [1]], ["HbsAg", [1]], ["Sickling Test", [1]], 
        ["Hb Electrophoresis", [1]],["Urine Albumin", [1, 2, 3]], ["Urine Sugar", [1, 2, 3]]];
        //["Blood Sugar", [1, 2, 3]],
        
 const pregnancyComplications = ["Excessive vomiting and inability to consume anything orally in last 24 hours", "Severe Abdominal Pain", "Blurred vision","Decreased Foetal movements", "Per vaginal bleeding", "PV leaking", "Morning Sickness", "Difficulty breathing", "Severe headache"];
   
   if (imports.motherCalculations.isBelowNormalWeightGain) {
        const isBelowNormalWeightGain = imports.motherCalculations
            .isBelowNormalWeightGain(programEncounter.programEnrolment, programEncounter);
        console.log('isBelowNormalWeightGain', isBelowNormalWeightGain);
    }
  
     

const isNormalWeightGain = imports.motherCalculations
            .isNormalWeightGain(programEncounter.programEnrolment, programEncounter);
  console.log('isNormalWeightGain',isNormalWeightGain);
            
               
    const complicationsBuilder = new imports.rulesConfig.complicationsBuilder({
        programEncounter: programEncounter,
        complicationsConcept: "High Risk Conditions"
    });
        complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Underweight")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Weight").is.lessThanOrEqualTo(35);  
        
        complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Essential Hypertension")
        .whenItem(pregnancyPeriodInWeeks).is.lessThanOrEqualTo(20)
        .and.whenItem(isBloodPressureHigh).is.truthy;
        
        complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Superimposed Pre-Eclampsia")
      .when.valueInEncounter("Urine Albumin")
      .containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Trace","+1","+2","+3","+4")
      .and.whenItem(pregnancyPeriodInWeeks).is.lessThanOrEqualTo(20)
      .and.whenItem(isBloodPressureHigh).is.truthy;
      
      complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Pregnancy induced hypertension")
      .whenItem(isBloodPressureHigh).is.truthy
      .and.whenItem(obsHistoryOfPregnancyInducedHypertension).is.not.truthy
      .and.whenItem(pregnancyPeriodInWeeks).greaterThan(20)
      .and.whenItem(isEssentialHypertensive).is.not.truthy;       
     
     complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Eclampsia")
     .when.valueInEncounter("Has she been having convulsions?").containsAnswerConceptName('Present')
      .and.when.valueInEncounter("Urine Albumin").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Trace","+1","+2","+3","+4") 
      .and.whenItem(isBloodPressureHigh).is.truthy  
      .and.whenItem(obsHistoryOfPregnancyInducedHypertension).is.not.truthy
      .and.whenItem(pregnancyPeriodInWeeks).greaterThan(20)
      .and.whenItem(isEssentialHypertensive).is.not.truthy;       
     
     complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Mild Pre-Eclampsia")
      .whenItem(isBloodPressureHigh).is.truthy
    .and.when.valueInEncounter("Has she been having convulsions?").containsAnswerConceptNameOtherThan('Present')
      .and.when.valueInEncounter("Urine Albumin")
      .containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Trace","+1","+2")      
      .and.whenItem(obsHistoryOfPregnancyInducedHypertension).is.not.truthy
      .and.whenItem(pregnancyPeriodInWeeks).greaterThan(20)
      .and.whenItem(isEssentialHypertensive).is.not.truthy;       
     
     complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Severe Pre-Eclampsia")
      .whenItem(isBloodPressureHigh).is.truthy
      .and.when.valueInEncounter("Has she been having convulsions?").containsAnswerConceptNameOtherThan('Present')  
      .and.when.valueInEncounter("Urine Albumin")
      .containsAnyAnswerConceptName("+3","+4")   
      .and.whenItem(obsHistoryOfPregnancyInducedHypertension).is.not.truthy
      .and.whenItem(pregnancyPeriodInWeeks).greaterThan(20)
      .and.whenItem(isEssentialHypertensive).is.not.truthy;       
     
  
       
       complicationsBuilder.addComplication('Miscarriage')
       .when.valueInEncounter("Pregnancy complications")
       .containsAnswerConceptName('Per vaginal bleeding')
       .and.whenItem(pregnancyPeriodInWeeks).is.lessThanOrEqualTo(20);
       
       complicationsBuilder.addComplication('Ante Partum hemorrhage (APH)')
       .when.valueInEncounter("Pregnancy complications")
       .containsAnswerConceptName('Per vaginal bleeding')
       .and.whenItem(pregnancyPeriodInWeeks).is.greaterThan(20);       
       
       complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Severe Anemia")
       .when.valueInEncounter("Hb").is.lessThan(7);       
       
       complicationsBuilder.addComplication('Moderate Anemia')
       .when.valueInEncounter("Hb").is.lessThanOrEqualTo(11)
       .and.when.valueInEncounter("Hb").is.greaterThanOrEqualTo(7);
       
       complicationsBuilder.addComplication('HIV/AIDS Positive')
       .when.valueInEncounter("HIV/AIDS Test").containsAnswerConceptName('Positive');
       
       complicationsBuilder.addComplication('VDRL Positive')
       .when.valueInEncounter("VDRL").containsAnswerConceptName('Positive');
       
       complicationsBuilder.addComplication('Sickling Positive')
       .when.valueInEncounter("Sickling Test").containsAnswerConceptName('Positive');
              
       complicationsBuilder.addComplication('Sickle cell disease SS')
       .when.valueInEncounter("Hb Electrophoresis").containsAnswerConceptName('SS');
       
       complicationsBuilder.addComplication('Malaria')
       .when.valueInEncounter("Paracheck").containsAnswerConceptName('Positive for PF')
       .or.when.valueInEncounter("Paracheck").containsAnswerConceptName('Positive for PV')
       .or.when.valueInEncounter("Paracheck").containsAnswerConceptName('Positive for PF and PV');
       
       complicationsBuilder.addComplication('Jaundice Present')
       .when.valueInEncounter("Jaundice (Icterus)").containsAnswerConceptName('Present');
       
       
       complicationsBuilder.addComplication('Convulsions Present')
       .when.valueInEncounter("Has she been having convulsions?").containsAnswerConceptName('Present');
       
       
       pregnancyComplications.forEach((complication) => {
        complicationsBuilder.addComplication(complication)

    });
    
    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Pedal Edema Present")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Pedal Edema").containsAnswerConceptName("Present");

    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Pallor Present")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Pallor").containsAnswerConceptName("Present");

    if (isBelowNormalWeightGain) {
        referralAdvice.addComplication("Irregular weight gain")
            .whenItem(isBelowNormalWeightGain).is.truthy;
    }
        
     complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Foetal heart sound irregular")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Foetal Heart Sound").containsAnswerConceptName("Present and Irregular");

    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Foetal heart sound absent")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Foetal Heart Sound").containsAnswerConceptName("Absent");

    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Foetal heart rate irregular")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Foetal Heart Rate").is.lessThan(120)
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Foetal Heart Rate").is.greaterThan(160);

    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Low Pulse")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Pulse").is.lessThan(60);

    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("High Pulse")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Pulse").is.greaterThan(100);

    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Low Respiratory Rate")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Respiratory Rate").is.lessThan(12);

    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("High Respiratory Rate")

    //complicationsBuilder.addComplication("High blood sugar")

    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Abnormal Urine Sugar")

    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Multiple foetuses")
        .when.valueInEncounter("USG Scanning Report - Number of foetus").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Two", "Three", "More than three");

    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Abnormal Liquour")
        .when.valueInEncounter("USG Scanning Report - Liquour").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Increased", "Decreased");

    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Placenta Previa Present")
        .when.valueInEncounter("USG Scanning Report - Placenta Previa").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Previa");
    
    complicationsBuilder.addComplication('Low BMI')
        .when.valueInEncounter('BMI').lessThan(18.5);

    complicationsBuilder.addComplication('High BMI')
        .when.valueInEncounter('BMI').greaterThan(24.9);

    ["Flat", "Retracted"].forEach((nippleState) => {
        complicationsBuilder.addComplication(`${nippleState} Nipples`)
            .when.valueInEncounter("Breast Examination - Nipple").containsAnswerConceptName(nippleState);

    });
       
    decisions.encounterDecisions.push(complicationsBuilder.getComplications());   
    
    const recommendationBuilder = new imports.rulesConfig.complicationsBuilder({
        programEncounter: programEncounter,
        complicationsConcept: "Recommendations"
    });
    
    recommendationBuilder.addComplication("Institutional Delivery")
        .when.age.is.lessThan(18)
        .or.when.age.is.greaterThan(30)

        .or.when.valueInRegistration("Medical history").containsAnyAnswerConceptName(medicalHistory)
        .or.when.valueInEnrolment("Height").is.lessThan(145)

        .or.when.valueInEnrolment("Age of youngest child").asAge.is.lessThan(1)
        .or.when.valueInEnrolment("Obstetrics history").containsAnyAnswerConceptName(pastComplications)
        .or.when.valueInEnrolment("Infertility treatment").is.yes
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Systolic").greaterThanOrEqualTo(140)

        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Urine Albumin").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Trace","+1","+2","+3","+4")

        .containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Increased", "Decreased")
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("USG Scanning Report - Placenta Previa")
        .containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Previa")
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Foetal presentation").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Transverse", "Breech")
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("VDRL").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Positive")

        .or.when.valueInEncounter("HbsAg").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Positive")
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Sickling Test").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Positive")
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Hb Electrophoresis").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("SS");

    recommendationBuilder.addComplication("Institutional ANC")
        .when.valueInRegistration("Medical history").containsAnyAnswerConceptName(medicalHistory)
        .or.when.valueInEnrolment("Age of youngest child").asAge.is.lessThan(1)
        .or.when.valueInEnrolment("Obstetrics history")
        .containsAnyAnswerConceptName("3 or more than 3 spontaneous abortions")
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Systolic").greaterThanOrEqualTo(140)
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Diastolic").greaterThanOrEqualTo(90)
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Urine Albumin").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Trace", "+1", "+2", "+3", "+4")
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Urine Sugar").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Trace", "+1", "+2", "+3", "+4")
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("USG Scanning Report - Number of foetus")
        .containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Two", "Three", "More than three")
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("USG Scanning Report - Liquour")
        .containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Increased", "Decreased")
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("USG Scanning Report - Placenta Previa")
        .containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Previa")
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Foetal presentation").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Transverse", "Breech");
    
    decisions.encounterDecisions.push(recommendationBuilder.getComplications());   
    
    
    const reasonsBuilder = new imports.rulesConfig.complicationsBuilder({
        programEncounter: programEncounter,
        complicationsConcept: "Reason(s) for Institutional Delivery"
    });
    
    reasonsBuilder.addComplication("Under age pregnancy").when.age.is.lessThan(18);
    reasonsBuilder.addComplication("Old age pregnancy").when.age.is.greaterThan(30);

         ["Excessive vomiting and inability to consume anything orally in last 24 hours",
          "Severe Abdominal Pain", "Blurred vision",
        "Decreased Foetal movements", "Per vaginal bleeding", "PV leaking"].forEach((complication) => {
            referralAdvice1.addComplication(complication)
            .when.valueInEncounter("Pregnancy complications")
            .containsAnswerConceptName(complication);
    });

const isAbsoluteMaxWeightGain = imports.motherCalculations
            .isAbsoluteMaxWeightGain(programEncounter.programEnrolment, programEncounter);

   referralAdvice1.addComplication("Irregular weight gain")
        .whenItem(isAbsoluteMaxWeightGain).is.truthy;

    referralAdvice1.addComplication("Convulsions")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Has she been having convulsions?").containsAnswerConceptName("Present");

    referralAdvice1.addComplication("Jaundice")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Jaundice (Icterus)").containsAnswerConceptName("Present");

    referralAdvice1.addComplication("Foetal movements absent")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Foetal movements").containsAnswerConceptName("Absent");

    referralAdvice1.addComplication("Foetal movements reduced")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Foetal movements").containsAnswerConceptName("Reduced");

    referralAdvice1.addComplication("Foetal heart sound irregular")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Foetal Heart Sound").containsAnswerConceptName("Present and Irregular");

    referralAdvice1.addComplication("Foetal heart sound absent")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Foetal Heart Sound").containsAnswerConceptName("Absent");

    referralAdvice1.addComplication("Foetal heart rate irregular")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Foetal Heart Rate").is.lessThan(120)
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Foetal Heart Rate").is.greaterThan(160);

    referralAdvice1.addComplication("Hypertension")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Systolic").greaterThanOrEqualTo(140)
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Diastolic").greaterThanOrEqualTo(90);

    referralAdvice1.addComplication("Fever")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Temperature").greaterThan(99);

    referralAdvice1.addComplication("Abnormal Paracheck")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Paracheck").
        containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Positive for PF", "Positive for PF and PV", "Positive for PV");

    referralAdvice1.addComplication("Abnormal Hb")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Hb").lessThan(8);

    //referralAdvice1.addComplication("High blood sugar")
      //  .when.valueInEncounter("Blood Sugar").greaterThanOrEqualTo(140);

    referralAdvice1.addComplication("Abnormal Hb Electrophoresis")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Hb Electrophoresis").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("SS");

    referralAdvice1.addComplication("Abnormal Urine Albumin")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Urine Albumin").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Trace", "+1", "+2", "+3", "+4");

    referralAdvice1.addComplication("Abnormal Urine Sugar")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Urine Sugar").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Trace", "+1", "+2", "+3", "+4");

    
    decisions.encounterDecisions.push(referralAdvice1.getComplications());  
  
  const adviceBuilder = new imports.rulesConfig.complicationsBuilder({
        programEncounter: programEncounter,
        complicationsConcept: "Missing tests"
    });
    
     investigations.forEach(([investigation, applicableTrimesters]) => {
      adviceBuilder.addComplication(investigation)
         .when.valueInEncounter(investigation).is.notDefined
         .and.whenItem(applicableTrimesters.indexOf(trimester)).is.greaterThan(-1);
    });
    
    decisions.encounterDecisions.push(adviceBuilder.getComplications());  
  
    
    return decisions;
};
```

### `MLD Trust` · `form[Birth Form]`  (10368 bytes)

```javascript
"use strict";
({params, imports}) => {
    const programEncounter = params.entity;
    const decisions = params.decisions;
    const enrolmentDecisions = [];
    const encounterDecisions = [];
    const registrationDecisions = [];
    const moment = imports.moment;
    const _ = imports.lodash;
    const individualService = params.services.individualService;
    let mother = null;
    let ancEncounters = [];
    let latestAnc = null;

    function getMatchingConditions(conditionList) { 
        return conditionList
            .filter(condition => condition.conditionRule)
            .map(condition => condition.value);
    }
    const individual = programEncounter.programEnrolment.individual;
    const lowestAddressLevel = individual.lowestAddressLevel;

    const motherUUID = individual.getObservationValue("670f4d67-851d-4c6e-acd4-7c537715c908");
    if(motherUUID){
        mother = individualService.getSubjectByUUID(motherUUID)
    }
    if(mother){
        let pregnancyEnrolments = mother.enrolments;
        let requiredEnrolment = null;
        for (const enrolment of pregnancyEnrolments) {
            let deliveryEncounter = enrolment.getEncountersOfType("Delivery");
            if(deliveryEncounter.length == 0){
                continue;
            } 
            else {
                deliveryEncounter = deliveryEncounter[0];
            }

            const deliveryDate = moment(deliveryEncounter.getObservationValue("76d5d509-ca1b-45b7-863f-536d5f65d06f")).startOf('day'); 
            const childDOB = moment(individual.dateOfBirth).startOf('day');
            const differenceInDays = deliveryDate.diff(childDOB, 'days');
            if(differenceInDays <= 2 &&  differenceInDays >= -2){
                requiredEnrolment = enrolment;
                break;
            }
        }
        

        if(requiredEnrolment){
            ancEncounters = requiredEnrolment.getEncountersOfType("ANC");
            if(ancEncounters.length > 0){
                latestAnc = ancEncounters[0];
            }
        }
    }
        
 
    const highRiskConditionList = [
        {
            value: "Head Size: Small or Big",
            conditionRule: new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter}).when.valueInEncounter("2fddc3fb-41b2-4030-abc2-01e29dac1b92").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("87beb69f-2dc7-4400-95ac-cfeb052bd18a","63aadedb-be59-4712-a0b2-0db28b47da22").matches()
        },
        {
            value: "Birth Weight less than 1.5 kg",
            conditionRule: new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter}).when.valueInEncounter("148fb61a-9d85-4876-b20f-50235c0d1d6e").lessThan(1.5).matches()
        },
        {
            value: "Gestation age less than 32 weeks",
            conditionRule: new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter}).when.valueInEncounter("2bb79ac5-463b-4940-aa70-5c1b5cfd2045").containsAnswerConceptName("066a76ab-bfdb-4d7f-a75c-e920f73390e9").matches()
        },
        {
            value: "Did not cry immediately after birth",
            conditionRule: new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter}).when.valueInEncounter("49a7857f-3cba-477c-be3a-b1b81c593a86").containsAnswerConceptName("09149611-0d88-4cf2-9031-118240d02f8c").matches()
        },
        {
            value: "Birth Defect: Ear",
            conditionRule: new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter}).when.valueInEncounter("e91ba5ed-83cd-4276-b884-4824e2de5b33").containsAnswerConceptName("2920d457-972f-4e9b-9509-c128f4b373b1").matches()
        },
        {
            value: "Birth Defect: Eyes",
            conditionRule: new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter}).when.valueInEncounter("7ff4a864-5f83-434c-8082-d5267657aa19").containsAnswerConceptName("2920d457-972f-4e9b-9509-c128f4b373b1").matches()
        },
        {
            value: "Birth Defect: Lips",
            conditionRule: new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter}).when.valueInEncounter("a0f68373-12ea-4fda-b26b-df7ba4cc1ff3").containsAnswerConceptName("0fd90bf0-b7d0-492f-ac32-083cd6d63217").matches()
        },
        {
            value: "Birth Defect: Nose",
            conditionRule: new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter}).when.valueInEncounter("1bb815e5-8fe7-4769-b989-fdebcd238686").containsAnswerConceptName("2920d457-972f-4e9b-9509-c128f4b373b1").matches()
        },
        {
            value: "Palate: Cleft",
            conditionRule: new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter}).when.valueInEncounter("4a29be5d-9239-4ed2-a89e-795299ee9510").containsAnswerConceptName("0fd90bf0-b7d0-492f-ac32-083cd6d63217").matches()
        },
        {
            value: "Anal Opening: Narrow or Absent",
            conditionRule: new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter}).when.valueInEncounter("41356ac9-ac8c-4c46-9ea2-25856dba14d8").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("214b69f1-4400-431f-89f2-b22b23e50960","a8c69141-9b5a-445e-be7b-9c7867703ace").matches()
        },
        {
            value: "Testicles Undescended",
            conditionRule: new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter}).when.valueInEncounter("8ab3b580-b5f3-4b1f-ab19-18daf046d8f9").containsAnswerConceptName("9f770b21-c02e-4ad6-ac21-07bb250d5a50").matches()
        },
        {
            value: "Curved backbone",
            conditionRule: new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter}).when.valueInEncounter("bf26d586-8672-4455-94f9-dcf1a1e5fbd2").containsAnswerConceptName("85bc34b1-574e-4e05-8333-e94f743cdd39").matches()
        },
        {
            value: "Short or Swollen Neck",
            conditionRule: new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter}).when.valueInEncounter("3396e72b-2b58-4ea1-aadf-3e121d71bd08").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("0661b1eb-c97d-45b7-b5d2-0e7d0672a03b","e7b32ebd-1eac-49a7-813e-0f2c68140ae0").matches()
        }
    ]

    let motherHighRiskConditionList = [
        {
            value:  "Mother's Chronic Disease: HIV",
            conditionRule: latestAnc && latestAnc.getObservationReadableValue("f0025d4d-9798-4b21-bcb6-d685e78ade4e") == 'Yes'
        },
        {
            value: "Mother's Chronic Disease: Tuberculosis",
            conditionRule: latestAnc && latestAnc.getObservationReadableValue("e54866ed-cac2-4109-b426-bb2e2737e289") == 'Yes'
        },
        {
            value: "Mother's Chronic Disease: Syphilis",
            conditionRule: latestAnc && latestAnc.getObservationReadableValue("358b7cb1-cafd-47c0-8e1b-7351b57269fc") == 'Yes'
        },
        {
            value: "Mother's Chronic Disease: Leprosy",
            conditionRule: latestAnc && latestAnc.getObservationReadableValue("aad0bfaa-a2bf-4d9b-aa99-84b8302b2dde") == 'Yes'
        },
        {
            value: "Mother's Chronic Disease: Hepatitis B",
            conditionRule: latestAnc && latestAnc.getObservationReadableValue("367db94f-1704-4df3-b15f-437217c5f881") == 'Yes'
        },
        {
            value: "Mother's Temperature is greater than or equal to 100 F",
            conditionRule: ancEncounters.some((anc)=> anc.getObservationValue("3f9dca2f-f1f5-4cec-a46a-9fdc2b39a442") >= 100)
        }
    ]
        
    const highRiskValues = getMatchingConditions(highRiskConditionList);
    const motherHighRiskValues = getMatchingConditions(motherHighRiskConditionList);
    
    const height = programEncounter.getObservationValue("Height");
    const weight = programEncounter.getObservationValue("Weight");
    const asOnDate = programEncounter.encounterDateTime;
    const individual = programEncounter.programEnrolment.individual;
    const age = programEncounter.programEnrolment.individual.getAgeInMonths();
    
    const getGradeforZscore = (zScore) => {
        let grade;
        if (zScore <= -3) {
            grade = 3;
        } else if (zScore > -3 && zScore < -2) {
            grade = 2;
        } else if (zScore >= -2) {
            grade = 1;
        }
        return grade;
    };
    
    const zScoresForChild = ruleServiceLibraryInterfaceForSharingModules.common.getZScore(individual, asOnDate, weight, height);
  
    /*Weight for age*/
    const zScoreGradeStatusMappingWeightForAge = {
      '1': 'Green',
      '2': 'Yellow',
      '3': 'Red'
    };
    
    const wfaGrade = getGradeforZscore(zScoresForChild.wfa);
    const wfaStatus = zScoreGradeStatusMappingWeightForAge[wfaGrade];
    const wfaZScore= zScoresForChild.wfa;
    
    encounterDecisions.push({name: "Z-Score Gradation Value (Weight for Age)", value: wfaZScore});
    encounterDecisions.push({name: "Z-Score Gradation Colour (Weight for Age)", value: wfaStatus});  
    
    /*Weight for age*/
    
    /*Height for age*/
    const zScoreGradeStatusMappingHeightForAge = {
      '1': 'Normal Height',
      '2': 'Stunted',
      '3': 'Severely Stunted'
    };

    const hfaGrade = getGradeforZscore(zScoresForChild.hfa);
    const hfaZScore = zScoresForChild.hfa;
    const hfaStatus = zScoreGradeStatusMappingHeightForAge[hfaGrade];
    
    encounterDecisions.push({name: "Z-Score Gradation Value (Height for Age)", value: hfaZScore});
    encounterDecisions.push({name: "Z-Score Gradation Category (Height for Age)", value: hfaStatus});  
    /*Height for age*/
    
    
    /*Weight for height*/
    const zScoreGradeStatusMappingWeightForHeight = {
      '1': 'Normal',
      '2': 'Moderate Wasting',
      '3': 'Severe Wasting'
    };
    
    const wfhGrade = getGradeforZscore(zScoresForChild.wfh);
    const wfhStatus = zScoreGradeStatusMappingWeightForHeight[wfhGrade];
    const wfhZScore = zScoresForChild.wfh;
    
    
    encounterDecisions.push({name: "Z-Score Gradation Value (Weight for Height)", value: wfhZScore});
    encounterDecisions.push({name: "Z-Score Gradation Category (Weight for Height)", value: wfhStatus});  
    /*Weight for height*/
    
    enrolmentDecisions.push({name: "High risk condition for child", value: highRiskValues}); 
    encounterDecisions.push({name: "High risk condition for child", value: highRiskValues}); 
    encounterDecisions.push({name: "Child at High Risk due to mother's high risk condition", value: motherHighRiskValues}); 

    decisions.enrolmentDecisions.push(...enrolmentDecisions);
    decisions.encounterDecisions.push(...encounterDecisions);
   
    return decisions;
};
```

### `APF Odisha` · `form[ANC]`  (9232 bytes)

```javascript
"use strict";
({params, imports}) => {
    const programEncounter = params.entity;
    const decisions = params.decisions;
    const moment = imports.moment;
    const _ = imports.lodash;

    function pregnancyInducedMorbidityConditionChecker(answerConceptUUID) {
        return new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter})
            .when.valueInEncounter("12d99265-c769-4236-aff5-fcba73976396")
            .containsAnswerConceptName(answerConceptUUID)
            .matches();
    }

    // Function to retrieve high-risk conditions based on specific rules
    function getHighRiskConditions(){

        // ancHighRiskValues contains the high-risk values that can be found in the ANC Encounter
        const ancHighRiskValues = ["Severe anemia", "Moderate anemia", "Mild anemia", "Inadequate Weight Gain during Pregnancy", "Hypertension", "Diabetes/ Gestational Diabetes", "Tuberculosis", "Asthma", "Pre-eclampsia", "Sickle cell anemia", "Thalassemia", "Heart conditions", "Hypothyroidism", "Syphillis", "Other pre-morbidity conditions"];

        const hbValue = programEncounter.getObservationReadableValue("68bc6e51-eb49-4816-b78b-2427bbab8d92");
        // Defining Rule Conditions for each high-risk condition
        const isSevereAnemia = hbValue && hbValue < 7;
        const isModerateAnemia = hbValue && hbValue >= 7 && hbValue < 10;
        const isMideAnemia = hbValue && hbValue >= 10 && hbValue < 11;
        const isHypertension = pregnancyInducedMorbidityConditionChecker("621462ed-23da-4b73-b590-4af8ccf34b45");
        const isDiabetes = pregnancyInducedMorbidityConditionChecker("9f6c206e-f04a-4a2a-b3ae-78edbffb0f62");
        const isTuberculosis = pregnancyInducedMorbidityConditionChecker("34f5f1a8-8a03-47d0-a91b-2a0fe959fc10");
        const isAsthma = pregnancyInducedMorbidityConditionChecker("11228d39-49f1-4a44-9bbf-6c673c208326");
        const isPreeclampsia = pregnancyInducedMorbidityConditionChecker("b95305a1-1831-49b7-a5a6-b4cb458a5d6f");
        const isSickleCellAnemia = pregnancyInducedMorbidityConditionChecker("4b0c2486-20ac-458b-b89a-28603715aeb2");
        const isThalassemia = pregnancyInducedMorbidityConditionChecker("83168c77-2a4d-4650-9ead-5c42f1c7f0c4");
        const isHeartConditions = pregnancyInducedMorbidityConditionChecker("dea0c584-32a3-493a-be95-66588e01181a");
        const isHypothyroidism = pregnancyInducedMorbidityConditionChecker("1e527e8b-76ef-426d-aee7-a9002dc031d0");
        const isSyphillis = pregnancyInducedMorbidityConditionChecker("d9414d88-e0cf-4ffe-9cba-151d6879b773");
        const isOtherPremorbidity = pregnancyInducedMorbidityConditionChecker("9f771423-6205-42db-8428-293ac0ad9b13");

        // Comparing the current ANC weight with the previous ANC weight. If the weight has not increased by 2 kg or more, it is considered high risk.
        let isInadequareWeightGain = false;
        const earliestDate = moment(programEncounter.earliestVisitDateTime);
        const ancPreviousEncounters = programEncounter.programEnrolment.getEncounters(true).filter((enc) => enc.encounterType.name === 'ANC' && enc.encounterDateTime && earliestDate.isAfter(moment(enc.earliestVisitDateTime)));
        if (ancPreviousEncounters.length > 0) {
            ancPreviousEncounters.sort((a, b) => moment(b.earliestVisitDateTime).diff(moment(a.earliestVisitDateTime)));
            const lastANCEncounter = ancPreviousEncounters[0];

            const currentWeight = programEncounter.getObservationReadableValue("3981ddb0-30a3-43d2-9564-16ae9cc0e25e");
            const lastMonthWeight = lastANCEncounter.getObservationReadableValue("3981ddb0-30a3-43d2-9564-16ae9cc0e25e");

            if (lastMonthWeight + 2 > currentWeight) {
                isInadequareWeightGain = true;
            }
        }

        // Array to hold true conditions and their corresponding values
        const highRiskConditions = [
            {isTrue: isSevereAnemia, value: "Severe anemia"},
            {isTrue: isModerateAnemia, value: "Moderate anemia"},
            {isTrue: isMideAnemia, value: "Mild anemia"},
            {isTrue: isInadequareWeightGain, value: "Inadequate Weight Gain during Pregnancy"},
            {isTrue: isHypertension, value: "Hypertension"},
            {isTrue: isDiabetes, value: "Diabetes/ Gestational Diabetes"},
            {isTrue: isTuberculosis, value: "Tuberculosis"},
            {isTrue: isAsthma, value: "Asthma"},
            {isTrue: isPreeclampsia, value: "Pre-eclampsia"},
            {isTrue: isSickleCellAnemia, value: "Sickle cell anemia"},
            {isTrue: isThalassemia, value: "Thalassemia"},
            {isTrue: isHeartConditions, value: "Heart conditions"},
            {isTrue: isHypothyroidism, value: "Hypothyroidism"},
            {isTrue: isSyphillis, value: "Syphillis"},
            {isTrue: isOtherPremorbidity, value: "Other pre-morbidity conditions"}
        ];

        // Array to store high-risk values found in this ANC Encounter
        let clinicalHighRiskValues = [];

        // Retrieve high-risk values found in this ANC Encounter
        highRiskConditions.forEach(condition => {
            if (condition.isTrue) {
                clinicalHighRiskValues.push(condition.value);
            }
        });

        // Retrieve the existing high-risk values.
        let existingHighRiskValues = programEncounter.programEnrolment.getObservationReadableValue('9a7f284b-251d-459b-97d9-929ed280b3d3') || [];

        // Remove the high-risk values found in Previous ANC encounters from existingHighRiskValues and updating it with the new high-risk values from the current ANC high-risk values.
        if (existingHighRiskValues) {
            existingHighRiskValues = existingHighRiskValues.filter(value => !ancHighRiskValues.includes(value));
            clinicalHighRiskValues = clinicalHighRiskValues.concat(existingHighRiskValues);
        }

        return clinicalHighRiskValues;
    }

    // Get latest high-risk conditions
    const clinicalHighRiskValues = getHighRiskConditions();

    decisions.enrolmentDecisions.push({name: "High risk condition", value: clinicalHighRiskValues});
    decisions.encounterDecisions.push({name: "High risk condition", value: clinicalHighRiskValues});

    // Get the latest ANC or Delivery encounter
    const latestEnc = programEncounter.programEnrolment.lastFulfilledEncounter("ANC", "Delivery");
    
    // Check if the current encounter is the latest one
    const isCurrentEncounterTheLatest = latestEnc === undefined || programEncounter.uuid === latestEnc.uuid || moment(programEncounter.encounterDateTime).isAfter(moment(latestEnc.encounterDateTime));

    if (isCurrentEncounterTheLatest) {

        const currentlyActiveInProgram = !programEncounter.programEnrolment.programExitDateTime;
        const isDeliveryEncounterIncomplete = !programEncounter.programEnrolment.hasCompletedEncounterOfType('Delivery');
        const requiresMedicalInterventionTreatment = new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter}).when.latestValueInEntireEnrolment("35f880ca-60b5-4240-97e1-813c0a7a78c4").containsAnswerConceptName("8ebbf088-f292-483e-9084-7de919ce67b7").matches();
        const anmRecommendedMedicalFacilityIntervention = new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter}).when.valueInEncounter("f7cc4fd5-5c66-4695-b5e3-1d83c3621f5d").containsAnswerConceptName("8ebbf088-f292-483e-9084-7de919ce67b7").matches();
        const isSevereAnemic = new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter}).when.valueInEncounter("68bc6e51-eb49-4816-b78b-2427bbab8d92").lessThan(7).matches();
        const isGeographicalHighRisk = new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter}).when.valueInEncounter("96b167e1-2d98-40b9-af04-8e4f64f9999a").containsAnswerConceptName("8ebbf088-f292-483e-9084-7de919ce67b7").matches();

        if(currentlyActiveInProgram && isDeliveryEncounterIncomplete && (requiresMedicalInterventionTreatment || anmRecommendedMedicalFacilityIntervention || isSevereAnemic)) {

            const toBeMonitoredByQRT = (isGeographicalHighRisk || anmRecommendedMedicalFacilityIntervention || isSevereAnemic);
            const value = toBeMonitoredByQRT ? "Yes" : "No";

            decisions.registrationDecisions.push({name: "To be monitored by QRT", value});
            decisions.enrolmentDecisions.push({name: "To be monitored by QRT", value});
            decisions.encounterDecisions.push({name: "To be monitored by QRT", value});
        }
        else{
            decisions.registrationDecisions.push({name: "To be monitored by QRT", value: "No"});
            decisions.enrolmentDecisions.push({name: "To be monitored by QRT", value: "No"});
            decisions.encounterDecisions.push({name: "To be monitored by QRT", value: "No"});
        }
        if (isGeographicalHighRisk) {
            decisions.enrolmentDecisions.push({name: "Pregnancy geographically high risk", value: "Yes"});
        }
        if (clinicalHighRiskValues.length > 0) {
            decisions.enrolmentDecisions.push({name: "Clinically high risk", value: "Yes"});
        }
    } else {
        decisions.encounterDecisions.push({name: "To be monitored by QRT", value: "No"});
    }

    return decisions;
};
```

```javascript
    if(locationUuid == undefined){
        locationUuid = villageUuid;
        locationName = "Village"
    }

    function getPath(uuid, parent) {
        const element = questionStructure[uuid];
        if (!element) {
            return encounter.getObservationReadableValue(uuid);
        }
        
        let identifier = element.name;
        let nextQuestionUUIDs = element.nextQuestionUUID;
        nextQuestionUUIDs.forEach((question) => {
            if (question.parent == parent) {
                let observedUUID = encounter.getObservationValue(question.uuid);
                
                let subPath = getPath(observedUUID, parent);
                if(subPath){
                  identifier += " > " + subPath;
                }
                else {
                  let observedValue = encounter.getObservationReadableValue(question.uuid);
                  identifier += " > " + observedValue;
                }
            }
        });
        
        return identifier;
    }
    
    let identifier = getPath(locationUuid, locationName);

    encounterDecisions.push({name: "Identifier", value: identifier});  
    
    const encounterDateForEditCase = encounter.getObservationValue("1f4bc226-9e3f-42c9-a332-2d81cd8e64f7");
    
    if (!encounterDateForEditCase) {
      let encDateTime = moment().format("YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss");
      
      if (encDateTime) {
        encounterDecisions.push({ name: "Encounter DateTime", value: encDateTime });
      }
    }
  
    decisions.enrolmentDecisions.push(...enrolmentDecisions);
    decisions.encounterDecisions.push(...encounterDecisions);
    decisions.registrationDecisions.push(...registrationDecisions);
    return decisions;
};
```

### `Animedh Charitable Trust DNH` · `form[Child Home Visit Anthropometry Encounter]`  (4928 bytes)

```javascript
"use strict";
({params, imports}) => {
    const programEncounter = params.entity;
    const decisions = params.decisions;
    const individual = programEncounter.programEnrolment.individual;
         
   const zScoreGradeStatusMappingWeightForAge = {
    '1': 'Normal',
    '2': 'Moderately Underweight',
    '3': 'Severely Underweight'
};

const zScoreGradeStatusMappingHeightForAge = {
    '1': 'Normal',
    '2': 'Stunted',
    '3': 'Severely stunted'
};

const zScoreGradeStatusMappingWeightForHeight = [
    ["Severely wasted", -3],
    ["Wasted", -2],
    ["Normal", 1],
    ["Possible risk of overweight", 2],
    ["Overweight", 3],
    ["Obese", Infinity],
];

const weightForHeightStatus = function (zScore) {
    let found = _.find(zScoreGradeStatusMappingWeightForHeight, function (currentStatus) {
        return zScore <= currentStatus[1];
    });
    return found && found[0];
}

const getGradeforZscore = (zScore) => {
    let grade;
    if (zScore <= -3) {
        grade = 3;
    }
    else if (zScore > -3 && zScore < -2) {
        grade = 2;
    }
    else if (zScore >= -2) {
        grade = 1;
    }
    return grade;
};

const addIfRequired = (decisions, name, value) => {
    if (value === -0) value = 0;
    if (value !== undefined) decisions.push({name: name, value: value});
};

const classifyGrowth = (zScores) => {
    let classifications = [];
    
    if (zScores.wfh < -3) classifications.push("WFH - SAM");
    else if (zScores.wfh >= -3 && zScores.wfh < -2) classifications.push("WFH - MAM");
    else if (zScores.wfh >= -2 && zScores.wfh <= 2) classifications.push("WFH - Normal");
    else if (zScores.wfh > 2) classifications.push("WFH - Overweight");
    
    if (zScores.wfa < -3) classifications.push("WFA - SUW");
    else if (zScores.wfa >= -3 && zScores.wfa < -2) classifications.push("WFA - MUW");
    else if (zScores.wfa >= -2 && zScores.wfa <= 2) classifications.push("WFA - Normal");
    else if (zScores.wfa > 2) classifications.push("WFA - Overweight");
    return classifications;
};

const currentEncounterDateTime = programEncounter.encounterDateTime;
const weight = programEncounter.findObservation("Weight") &&
               programEncounter.findObservation("Weight").getValue();
const height = programEncounter.findObservation("Height") &&     
               programEncounter.findObservation("Height").getValue();
   
const zScoresForChild = ruleServiceLibraryInterfaceForSharingModules.common.getZScore(individual, currentEncounterDateTime, weight, height);

var wfaGrade = getGradeforZscore(zScoresForChild.wfa);
var wfaStatus = zScoreGradeStatusMappingWeightForAge[wfaGrade];
var hfaGrade = getGradeforZscore(zScoresForChild.hfa);
var hfaStatus = zScoreGradeStatusMappingHeightForAge[hfaGrade];
var wfhStatus = weightForHeightStatus(zScoresForChild.wfh);
var growthClassifications = classifyGrowth(zScoresForChild);

addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Weight for age z-score", zScoresForChild.wfa);
addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Weight for age Grade", wfaGrade);
addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Weight for age Status", wfaStatus ? [wfaStatus] : []);
addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Height for age z-score", zScoresForChild.hfa);
addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Height for age Grade", hfaGrade);
addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Height for age Status", hfaStatus ? [hfaStatus] : []);
addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Weight for height z-score", zScoresForChild.wfh);
addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Weight for Height Status", wfhStatus ? [wfhStatus] : []);
addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Growth Classification", growthClassifications);

// Growth Faltering Status Logic
const sd2Neg = ruleServiceLibraryInterfaceForSharingModules.common
                .projectedSD2NegForWeight(individual, currentEncounterDateTime);
const observations = programEncounter.programEnrolment.getObservationsForConceptName("Weight");
const observationsSorted = _.orderBy(observations,
    encounter => imports.moment(encounter.encounterDateTime).unix(),
    ["desc"]
);

const pastObservation = _.find(observationsSorted, observation =>
    imports.moment(currentEncounterDateTime).diff(imports.moment(observation.encounterDateTime), "months", true) >= 3.0
);

if (pastObservation) {
    const pastSD2Neg = ruleServiceLibraryInterfaceForSharingModules.common
    .projectedSD2NegForWeight(individual, pastObservation.encounterDateTime);
    
    const pastWeight = pastObservation.obs;
    const falteringInPast = pastWeight < pastSD2Neg;
    const falteringStatus = falteringInPast && weight < sd2Neg ? "Yes" : "No";
    addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Growth Faltering Status", [falteringStatus]);
} else {
    const falteringStatus = "No";
    addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Growth Faltering Status", [falteringStatus]);
}

return decisions;
};
```

### `atul_uat` · `form[PNC Encounter Cancellation]`  (1660 bytes)

```javascript
"use strict";
({params, imports}) => {
    const programEncounter = params.entity;
    const moment = imports.moment;
    const decisions = params.decisions;
    const enrolmentDecisions = [];
    const encounterDecisions = [];
    const registrationDecisions = [];

    const temporaryMigratedOrDead = new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter}).when.valueInCancelEncounter("e19ded48-c845-4a50-91ab-0d559d570b39").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("1b6ce78f-181b-4b64-9ec9-7afc07fe9729","345d9312-166d-4162-8ed2-17a016323181").matches();
  
    if(temporaryMigratedOrDead ){
        encounterDecisions.push({name: "Lactating Mother", value: ["No"]});
        enrolmentDecisions.push({name: "Lactating Mother", value: ["No"]}); 
    }
    else {
        const allThePNCsCompleted = programEncounter.programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType("PNC")
        .some((enc)=> enc.name == 'PNC Followup 10' && enc.voided == false && (enc.encounterDateTime != null || enc.cancelDateTime != null))
    
        if(programEncounter.name == 'PNC Followup 10'){
            encounterDecisions.push({name: "Lactating Mother", value: ["No"]});
            enrolmentDecisions.push({name: "Lactating Mother", value: ["No"]}); 
        }
        else if(!allThePNCsCompleted){
            encounterDecisions.push({name: "Lactating Mother", value: ["Yes"]});
            enrolmentDecisions.push({name: "Lactating Mother", value: ["Yes"]}); 
        }
    }

    decisions.enrolmentDecisions.push(...enrolmentDecisions);
    decisions.encounterDecisions.push(...encounterDecisions);
    decisions.registrationDecisions.push(...registrationDecisions);
    return decisions;
};
```

### `Purna Clinic` · `form[Chronic Disease Follow up]`  (1655 bytes)

```javascript
"use strict";
({params, imports}) => {
    const programEncounter = params.entity;
    const decisions = params.decisions;
    const _ = imports.lodash;
    const moment = imports.moment;
    const encounterDateTime = programEncounter.encounterDateTime? moment(programEncounter.encounterDateTime): moment();
    const encounterType = programEncounter.encounterType.name;
    const programEnrolment = programEncounter.programEnrolment;

    const AFFECTED_DISEASE_OBSERVATION_UUID = "d56abf12-b1d7-4e00-8fe3-19d8aa5fea3c";
    const ANY_DISEASE_OBSERVATION_UUID = "7e2ab8eb-adaa-49f3-8f98-64acac49e539";
    const NO_DISEASE_NAME = 'No Disease';
    const AFFECTED_DISEASES_DECISION_NAME = 'Affected Diseases';

    const getObservationValue = (entity, observationUuid = AFFECTED_DISEASE_OBSERVATION_UUID) => 
        entity.getObservationReadableValue(observationUuid) || [];

    const previousEncounters = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType(encounterType)
        .filter(encounter => 
            encounter.uuid !== programEncounter.uuid && 
            !encounter.voided && 
            moment(encounter.encounterDateTime).isBefore(encounterDateTime)
        );

    const currentEncounterAffectedDiseases = getObservationValue(programEncounter, ANY_DISEASE_OBSERVATION_UUID);

    if (currentEncounterAffectedDiseases.length === 0) {
        currentEncounterAffectedDiseases = [NO_DISEASE_NAME];
    }

    if (currentEncounterAffectedDiseases.length > 0) {
        decisions.encounterDecisions.push({
            name: AFFECTED_DISEASES_DECISION_NAME,
            value: currentEncounterAffectedDiseases
        });
    }

    return decisions;
};
```

### `Goonj` · `form[Distribution Registration]`  (1339 bytes)

```javascript
"use strict";
({params, imports}) => {
    const individual = params.entity;
    const moment = imports.moment;
    const decisions = params.decisions;
    const enrolmentDecisions = [];
    const encounterDecisions = [];
    const registrationDecisions = [];
    
    function toStartCase(str) {
        return str
        .trim()
        .toLowerCase()
        .split(/[\s]+/)
        .map(word => word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.slice(1))
        .join(' ');
    }
          
    const tola = individual.getObservationReadableValue("5e259bfe-07a8-4c88-a712-d22b9a612429");
    const block = individual.getObservationReadableValue("e2d35dee-c34f-4f54-a68b-f32ee81835b6");
    const village = individual.getObservationReadableValue("16b4db7c-e0a8-41f1-ac67-07470a762d9f");
  
    if(tola){
        registrationDecisions.push({name: "Tola / Mohalla", value:toStartCase(tola) });  
    }
    if(block){
        registrationDecisions.push({name: "Other Block", value:toStartCase(block) });  
    }
    if(village){
        registrationDecisions.push({name: "Other Village", value:toStartCase(village) });  
    }
    
  
    decisions.enrolmentDecisions.push(...enrolmentDecisions);
    decisions.encounterDecisions.push(...encounterDecisions);
    decisions.registrationDecisions.push(...registrationDecisions);
    return decisions;
};
```

### `IPH Sickle Cell` · `form[Screening Form]`  (632 bytes)

```javascript
//SAMPLE RULE EXAMPLE
  "use strict";
({params, imports}) => {
    const encounter = params.entity;
    const decisions = params.decisions;
    let participantsConsent = encounter.getObservationReadableValue('63e85622-dc67-4872-a96a-2032e1ec3439');
    
    if (participantsConsent && participantsConsent == 'Yes') {
     let obs=encounter.getObservationReadableValue('Solubility Test for SCD');
    decisions.registrationDecisions.push({name : "Solubility Test for SCD", value :obs});
    decisions.registrationDecisions.push({name : "Solubility test date", value :encounter.encounterDateTime});
    }
    
    return decisions;
};
```

```javascript
        .when.valueInEncounter("Child Pulse").greaterThan(100);

        .when.valueInEncounter("Child Temperature").lessThan(97.5);

    highRiskBuilder.addComplication("High Temperature")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Child Temperature").greaterThan(99.5);

    highRiskBuilder.addComplication("Low Respiratory Rate")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Child Respiratory Rate").lessThan(30);

        .when.valueInEncounter("Child Respiratory Rate").greaterThan(60);

    highRiskBuilder.addComplication("Icterus present")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Jaundice (Icterus)").containsAnswerConceptName("Present");
    
    decisions.encounterDecisions.push(highRiskBuilder.getComplications());   
    
       const zScoreGradeStatusMappingWeightForAge = {
    '1': 'Normal',
    '2': 'Moderately Underweight',
    '3': 'Severely Underweight'
};

const zScoreGradeStatusMappingHeightForAge = {
    '1': 'Normal',
    '2': 'Stunted',
    '3': 'Severely stunted'
};

const zScoreGradeStatusMappingWeightForHeight = [
    ["Severely wasted", -3],
    ["Wasted", -2],
    ["Normal", 1],
    ["Possible risk of overweight", 2],
    ["Overweight", 3],
    ["Obese", Infinity],
];

const weightForHeightStatus = function (zScore) {
    let found = _.find(zScoreGradeStatusMappingWeightForHeight, function (currentStatus) {
        return zScore <= currentStatus[1];
    });
    return found && found[0];
}

const getGradeforZscore = (zScore) => {
    let grade;
    if (zScore <= -3) {
        grade = 3;
    }
    else if (zScore > -3 && zScore < -2) {
        grade = 2;
    }
    else if (zScore >= -2) {
        grade = 1;
    }

    return grade;

}

const addIfRequired = (decisions, name, value) => {
    if (value === -0) value = 0;
    if (value !== undefined) decisions.push({name: name, value: value});
};

   const currentEncounterDateTime = programEncounter.encounterDateTime;
   const individual = programEncounter.programEnrolment.individual;
   
   const weight = programEncounter.findObservation("Weight") &&
                  programEncounter.findObservation("Weight").getValue();
   const height = programEncounter.findObservation("Height") &&     
                  programEncounter.findObservation("Height").getValue();
   
      const zScoresForChild = ruleServiceLibraryInterfaceForSharingModules.common.getZScore(individual, currentEncounterDateTime, weight, height);
      console.log('zScoresForChild',zScoresForChild);

    var wfaGrade = getGradeforZscore(zScoresForChild.wfa);
    console.log('zScoresForChild',getGradeforZscore(zScoresForChild.wfa));

    var wfaStatus = zScoreGradeStatusMappingWeightForAge[wfaGrade];

    var hfaGrade = getGradeforZscore(zScoresForChild.hfa);
    var hfaStatus = zScoreGradeStatusMappingHeightForAge[hfaGrade];

    var wfhStatus = weightForHeightStatus(zScoresForChild.wfh);

    addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Weight for age z-score", zScoresForChild.wfa);
    addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Weight for age Grade", wfaGrade);
    addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Weight for age Status", wfaStatus ? [wfaStatus] : []);

    addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Height for age z-score", zScoresForChild.hfa);
    addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Height for age Grade", hfaGrade);
    addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Height for age Status", hfaStatus ? [hfaStatus] : []);

    addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Weight for height z-score", zScoresForChild.wfh);
    addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Weight for Height Status", wfhStatus ? [wfhStatus] : []);
   
   const sd2Neg = ruleServiceLibraryInterfaceForSharingModules.common
                    .projectedSD2NegForWeight(individual, currentEncounterDateTime);
    const observations = programEncounter.programEnrolment.getObservationsForConceptName("Weight");
    const observationsSorted = _.orderBy(observations,
        encounter => imports.moment(encounter.encounterDateTime).unix(),
        ["desc"]
    );

    const pastObservation = _.find(observationsSorted, observation =>
        imports.moment(currentEncounterDateTime).diff(imports.moment(observation.encounterDateTime), "months", true) >= 3.0
    );

    if (pastObservation) {
        const pastSD2Neg = ruleServiceLibraryInterfaceForSharingModules.common
        .projectedSD2NegForWeight(individual, pastObservation.encounterDateTime);
        
        const pastWeight = pastObservation.obs;
        const falteringInPast = pastWeight < pastSD2Neg;
        const falteringStatus = falteringInPast && weight < sd2Neg ? "Yes" : "No";
        addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Growth Faltering Status", [falteringStatus]);
    } else {
        const falteringStatus = "No";
        addIfRequired(decisions.encounterDecisions, "Growth Faltering Status", [falteringStatus]);
    }

   
    return decisions;
};
```

### `Ashwini uat` · `form[PNC]`  (7216 bytes)

```javascript
//SAMPLE RULE EXAMPLE
  "use strict";
({params, imports}) => {
    const programEncounter = params.entity;
    const decisions = params.decisions;
    const complicationsBuilder = new imports.rulesConfig.complicationsBuilder({
        programEncounter: programEncounter,
        complicationsConcept: "PNC Complications"
    });
    
    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Post-Partum Haemorrhage")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Any vaginal problems").containsAnswerConceptName("Bad-smelling lochia")
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Post-Partum Haemorrhage symptoms").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Difficulty breathing", "Bad headache", "Blurred vision")
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Systolic").is.lessThan(90)
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Diastolic").is.lessThan(60);

    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Urinary Tract Infection")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Any abdominal problems").containsAnswerConceptName("Abdominal pain")
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Any difficulties with urinating").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Difficulty passing urine", "Burning Urination");

    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Genital Tract Infection")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Any abdominal problems").containsAnswerConceptName("Uterus is soft or tender")
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Any vaginal problems").containsAnswerConceptName("Heavy bleeding per vaginum");

    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Mastitis")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Any breast problems").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Breast hardness", "Nipple hardness", "Cracked Nipple");

    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Post Operative Infection")
        .when.valueInEncounter("How is the Cesarean incision area").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Looks red", "Indurated", "Filled with pus");

    const existingComplications = complicationsBuilder.getComplications().value;
    const existingComplicationsThatCanResultInHighTemperature = ["Post-Partum Haemorrhage", "Urinary Tract Infection", "Genital Tract Infection", "Mastitis", "Post Operative Infection"];
    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Infection")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Temperature").is.greaterThanOrEqualTo(99)
        .and.whenItem(existingComplications).matchesFn((existingComplications) => {
        return _.intersection(existingComplicationsThatCanResultInHighTemperature, existingComplications).length === 0;
    });

    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Post-Partum Depression")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Post-Partum Depression Symptoms").containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Insomnia", "Loss of appetite", "Weakness", "Irritability");

    complicationsBuilder.addComplication("Post-Partum Eclampsia")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Convulsions").containsAnswerConceptName("Present")
        .and.valueInEncounter("Systolic").is.greaterThanOrEqualTo(140)
        .or.valueInEncounter("Diastolic").is.greaterThanOrEqualTo(90);

  
    decisions.encounterDecisions.push(complicationsBuilder.getComplications());

const treatmentBuilder = new imports.rulesConfig.complicationsBuilder({
        programEncounter: programEncounter,
        complicationsConcept: "Treatment"
    });
    
    treatmentBuilder.addComplication("Calcium 1g/day");
    
    treatmentBuilder.addComplication("Ferrous Sulphate (100mg) 1 OD")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Hb % Level").greaterThanOrEqualTo(11);

    treatmentBuilder.addComplication("Ferrous Sulphate (200mg) 1 OD")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Hb % Level").lessThan(11);

    decisions.encounterDecisions.push(treatmentBuilder.getComplications());

    const referralAdvice = new imports.rulesConfig.complicationsBuilder({
        programEncounter: programEncounter,
        complicationsConcept: "Refer to the hospital immediately for"
    });
    
    referralAdvice.addComplication("Hypertension")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Systolic").greaterThanOrEqualTo(140)
        .or.when.valueInEncounter("Diastolic").greaterThanOrEqualTo(90);

    referralAdvice.addComplication("Fever")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Temperature").greaterThan(99);

    referralAdvice.addComplication("Abnormal Hb")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Hb % Level").lessThan(8);

    referralAdvice.addComplication("Post-Partum Haemorrhage symptoms")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Post-Partum Haemorrhage symptoms")
        .containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Difficulty breathing", "Bad headache", "Blurred vision");

    referralAdvice.addComplication("Abdominal problems")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Any abdominal problems")
        .containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Uterus is soft or tender", "Abdominal pain");

    referralAdvice.addComplication("Vaginal problems")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Any vaginal problems")
        .containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Heavy bleeding per vaginum", "Bad-smelling lochia", "Infected perineum suture");

    referralAdvice.addComplication("Urination difficulties")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Any difficulties with urinating")
        .containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Difficulty passing urine", "Burning Urination");

    referralAdvice.addComplication("Breast-related problems")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Any breast problems")
        .containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Cracked Nipple", "Nipple hardness", "Breast hardness", "Agalactorrhea- no or insufficient lactation", "Breast engorgement", "Breast abcess");

    referralAdvice.addComplication("Cesarean incision problems")
        .when.valueInEncounter("How is the Cesarean incision area")
        .containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Indurated", "Looks red", "Filled with pus");

    referralAdvice.addComplication("Feeling hot or has chills")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Does feel hot or have the chills")
        .containsAnswerConceptName("Yes");

    referralAdvice.addComplication("Convulsions")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Convulsions")
        .containsAnswerConceptName("Present");
        
    decisions.encounterDecisions.push(referralAdvice.getComplications());

const referralHospital = new imports.rulesConfig.complicationsBuilder({
        programEncounter: programEncounter,
        complicationsConcept: "Refer to the hospital for"
    });
    
    referralHospital.addComplication("Post-Partum Depression Symptoms")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Post-Partum Depression Symptoms")
        .containsAnyAnswerConceptName("Insomnia", "Irritability", "Loss of appetite", "Weakness");

    referralHospital.addComplication("Low Pulse")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Pulse").is.lessThan(60);

    referralHospital.addComplication("High Pulse")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Pulse").is.greaterThan(100);

    referralHospital.addComplication("Low Respiratory Rate")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Respiratory Rate").is.lessThan(12);

    referralHospital.addComplication("High Respiratory Rate")
        .when.valueInEncounter("Respiratory Rate").is.greaterThan(20);

    referralHospital.addComplication("Not using contraceptives")
        .valueInEncounter("Is the mother using any contraceptive method?")
        .containsAnswerConceptName("No");
    
    decisions.encounterDecisions.push(referralHospital.getComplications());



       return decisions;
};
```

### `MLD Trust` · `form[ANC Encounter]`  (7055 bytes)

```javascript
"use strict";
({params, imports}) => {
    const programEncounter = params.entity;
    const moment = imports.moment;
    const decisions = params.decisions;
    const enrolmentDecisions = [];
    const encounterDecisions = [];
    const registrationDecisions = [];
    const highRiskList = [];
    const referToDoctorList = [];
    const adviceList = [];
    
    let ancs = programEncounter.programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('ANC');
    
    let ancType = programEncounter.getObservationReadableValue('38581f86-92f4-4777-9c57-781f9ace43ab');
    
    let seq = ancType.split(" ")[1];
    
    let age = programEncounter.programEnrolment.individual.getAgeInYears(moment(), true);
    
    if(age < 18 || age > 35){
        highRiskList.push("Age less than 18 or more than 35");
    }
    
    const addDecision = (field, message, condition, anyTimeInANC, ...lists) => {
        if(anyTimeInANC){
          const prevEncounterWithValue = programEncounter.programEnrolment.findLatestPreviousEncounterWithValueForConcept(programEncounter, field, 'Yes');
          if (prevEncounterWithValue != null) {
            lists.forEach(list => list.push(message));
          } else {
            const value = programEncounter.getObservationReadableValue(field);
            if (value != null && condition(value)) {
              lists.forEach(list => list.push(message));
            }
          } 
        } else {
          const value = programEncounter.getObservationReadableValue(field);
          if (value != null && condition(value)) {
            lists.forEach(list => list.push(message));
          }
        }
    };

    const decisionRules = [
        { field: "Problem", message: "Bleeding", condition: v => v.includes("Bleeding"), lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: false},
        { field: "Problem", message: "Convulsions", condition: v => v.includes("Convulsions"), lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: false },
        { field: "Problem", message: "Severe Pain in Abdomen", condition: v => v.includes("Severe Pain in Abdomen"), lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: false },
        { field: "Problem", message: "Headache", condition: v => v.includes("Headache"), lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: false },
        { field: "Problem", message: "Blurred Vision", condition: v => v.includes("Blurred Vision"), lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: false },
        { field: "Problem", message: "Vomiting", condition: v => v.includes("Vomiting"), lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: false },
        
        
        { field: "General appearance", message: "Unconscious", condition: v => v === "Unconscious", lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: false },
        { field: "General appearance", message: "Looking Severely ill", condition: v => v === 'Looking Severely ill', lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: false },


        { field: "Respiratory Rate", message: "Breathing Difficulty", condition: v => v < 15 || v > 25 , lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: false },

        { field: "Body Temperature", message: "Body Temperature >=100", condition: v => v >= 100, lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: false },

        { field: "Blood Pressure: Systolic", message: "Blood Pressure: Systolic (<=90 or >=140)", condition: v => v <= 90 || v >= 140, lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: false },

        { field: "Blood Pressure: Diastolic", message: "Blood Pressure: Diastolic (<60 or >=90)", condition: v => v < 60 || v>=90, lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: false },

        { field: "Hb", message: "Hb (<9)", condition: v => v < 9, lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: false },

        { field: "Swelling in Legs", message: "Swelling in Legs", condition: v => v === 'Yes', lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: false },

        { field: "Pulse Rate", message: "Pulse (>100)", condition: v => v >100, lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: false },

        { field: "Height for women", message: "Height less than 145 cm", condition: v => v < 145, lists: [highRiskList], anyTimeInANC: false },
        
        { field: "Number of foetal heartbeats", message: "Abnormal Foetal Heartbeats", condition: v => v < 110 || v > 160, lists: [highRiskList], anyTimeInANC: false },
        
        { field: "Position of foetus", message: "Abnormal foetal position", condition: v => v.includes("Breech") || v.includes("Transverse"), lists: [highRiskList], anyTimeInANC: false },

        { field: "SickleCell", message: "Sickle Cell", condition: v => v === 'Yes', lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: true },
        { field: "Thalassemia Condition", message: "Thalassemia", condition: v => v === 'Yes', lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: true },
        { field: "IronDeficiency", message: "Iron Deficiency", condition: v => v === 'Yes', lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: false },
        { field: "HeartDisease", message: "Heart Disease", condition: v => v === 'Yes', lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: true },

        { field: "HIVQ", message: "HIV", condition: v => v === 'Yes', lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: true },
        { field: "TuberculosisQ", message: "Tuberculosis", condition: v => v === 'Yes', lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: true },
        { field: "SyphilisQ", message: "Syphilis", condition: v => v === 'Yes', lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: true },
        { field: "LeprosyQ", message: "Leprosy", condition: v => v === 'Yes', lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: true },
        { field: "HepatitisB", message: "Hepatitis B", condition: v => v === 'Yes', lists: [highRiskList, referToDoctorList], anyTimeInANC: true },

        { field: "Hb", message: "Schedule her for Iron-sucrose injections at the centre.", condition: v => (seq === '4') && (v <= 9 && v >= 7), lists: [adviceList], anyTimeInANC: false },
        
        { field: "Hb", message: "Blood Transfusion", condition: v => (seq === '4') && (v < 7), lists: [adviceList], anyTimeInANC: false },

    ];

    decisionRules.forEach(({ field, message, condition, lists, anyTimeInANC }) => {
        addDecision(field, message, condition, anyTimeInANC, ...lists);
    });

    encounterDecisions.push({ name: "Sequence", value: seq });
    encounterDecisions.push({ name: "ANC: High Risk Conditions", value: highRiskList });
    encounterDecisions.push({ name: "Refer to Doctor", value: referToDoctorList });
    encounterDecisions.push({ name: "Advice-ANC", value: adviceList });
  
    decisions.enrolmentDecisions.push(...enrolmentDecisions);
    decisions.encounterDecisions.push(...encounterDecisions);
    decisions.registrationDecisions.push(...registrationDecisions);
    return decisions;
};
```

### `Goonj` · `form[Village Assessment Encounter Form]`  (2365 bytes)

```javascript
"use strict";
({params, imports}) => {
    const {entity, form, services, entityContext, myUserGroups, user, db} = params;
    const moment = imports.moment;
    
    // Reload the encounter from the database to get all properties
    const encounterReloaded = db.objects('Encounter').filtered('uuid = $0', entity.uuid)[0];
    console.log('EditFormRule - Info:', JSON.stringify({
        entityUUID: entity.uuid,
        encounterFound: encounterReloaded ? true : false,
        createdByUUID: encounterReloaded ? encounterReloaded.createdByUUID : null,
        userUUID: user.userUUID
    }));
    
    const userGroups = myUserGroups
      .map((grp) => grp.groupName);
    
    const isUserFieldUser = userGroups.includes('Field Users');
    const isUserFieldSupervisor = userGroups.includes('Field Supervisor');
    
    // Get createdByUUID from the reloaded encounter
    const createdBy = encounterReloaded ? encounterReloaded.createdByUUID : null;
    
    const sameUser = createdBy === user.userUUID;
    
    // Allow Field Supervisors to edit any record, but Field Users can only edit their own
    const userCanEdit = (isUserFieldSupervisor || isUserFieldUser) && sameUser;
    
    // Parse encounterDateTime properly and check if it's within 3 days
    const currentDate = moment();
    const encounterDate = moment(entity.encounterDateTime);
    const thresholdDate = encounterDate.clone().add(3, 'days');
    const isEncounterDateWithinThreeDays = thresholdDate.isSameOrAfter(currentDate, 'day');
    
    const editableValue = userCanEdit && isEncounterDateWithinThreeDays;
    
    console.log('EditFormRule - Decision:', JSON.stringify({
        groups: userGroups,
        isFieldUser: isUserFieldUser,
        isFieldSupervisor: isUserFieldSupervisor,
        sameUser: sameUser,
        withinTimeLimit: isEncounterDateWithinThreeDays,
        editable: editableValue,
        reason: !editableValue ? 
            (!userCanEdit ? 'Not authorized to edit this record' : 
             !isEncounterDateWithinThreeDays ? 'Encounter date is past 3 days' : 'Unknown reason') : 
            'Editable'
    }));
    
    const output = {
      editable: {
        value: editableValue,
        messageKey: 'Edit access denied:Encounter date is past 3 days or You are not allowed to edit other users data'
      } 
    }; 
    
    return output;
};
```

### `Maitrayana` · `form[YPI Pragati Lifeskill Session Attendance Encounter]`  (577 bytes)

```javascript
//SAMPLE EDIT FORM RULE
  "use strict";
({params, imports}) => {
    const {entity, form, services, entityContext, myUserGroups, userInfo} = params;
    const _ = imports.lodash;
    const moment = imports.moment;
    const userGroupExists = _.find(myUserGroups, userGroup => userGroup.groupName === 'Users');
    const hasBeenThreeDays = moment(params.entity.encounterDateTime).isBefore(moment().subtract(3, 'days'))
    return {
      editable: {
        value: !!userGroupExists && !hasBeenThreeDays,
          messageKey: "Cannot edit after 3 days"        
      }
    }
};
```

### `APF Odisha` · `form[ANC Encounter Cancellation]`  (527 bytes)

```javascript
//SAMPLE EDIT FORM RULE
"use strict";
({ params, imports }) => {
  const { entity } = params; // entity = encounter
  const moment = imports.moment;

  const isCancelledPreviousMonth =
    entity.cancelDateTime &&
    moment(entity.earliestVisitDateTime).isBefore(moment(), 'month');

  if (isCancelledPreviousMonth) {
    return {
      eligible: {
        value: false,
        message: "Cancelled visits from a previous month cannot be edited."
      }
    };
  }


  return {
    eligible: {
      value: true
    }
  };
};
```

```javascript
    const userGroups = params.myUserGroups
      .map((grp) => grp.groupName);
    const sameUser = params.entity.createdByUUID === params.user.userUUID;
    
    const isUserFieldUser = userGroups.includes('Field Users');
    const isUserFieldSupervisor = userGroups.includes('Field Supervisor');
    
    const isRegistrationDateWithinThreeDays = moment(entity.registrationDate).add(3, 'days').isSameOrAfter(moment(), 'day');
    
    const output = {
      editable : {
        value: (isUserFieldUser || isUserFieldSupervisor) && isRegistrationDateWithinThreeDays && sameUser,
        messageKey: 'Edit access denied:Registration date is past 3 days or You are not allowed to edit other users data'
      } 
    }; 
    return output;
};
```
