## `eligibility-enrolment` — 39 extracted, showing 15 representatives

Sources: APF Odisha, Animedh Charitable Trust DNH, Ashwini uat, Centre for Social Justice (CSJ), IPH Sickle Cell, JK Lakshmi Cement, JNPCT, MLD Trust, …

### `atul_uat` · `program[Community Screening]`  (2340 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({ params, imports }) => {
    const individual = params.entity;
    const moment = imports.moment;

    let eligibility = false;

    const dob = individual.dateOfBirth ? moment(individual.dateOfBirth) : null;
    if (!dob || !dob.isValid()) return false;

    const today = moment();
    const fiveYearsAgo = today.clone().subtract(5, 'years');
    const nineteenYearsAgo = today.clone().subtract(19, 'years');
    const fortyNineYearsAgo = today.clone().subtract(49, 'years');

    const gender = individual.gender ? individual.gender.name : null;

    let isEligibleByAge = false;
    if (gender === 'Male') {
        isEligibleByAge = dob.isBetween(nineteenYearsAgo, fiveYearsAgo, null, '[)');
    } else if (gender === 'Female' || gender === 'Other') {
        isEligibleByAge = dob.isBetween(fortyNineYearsAgo, fiveYearsAgo, null, '[)');
    }
    
    if (!isEligibleByAge) return false;

    const schoolTypeUUID = '79f9fc96-7817-491c-a202-9e98ead1574c';    
    const isPartOfSchool = false;
    
    if(individual.lowestAddressLevel.type == 'School' || individual.lowestAddressLevel.typeUuid == schoolTypeUUID){
      isPartOfSchool = true;
    }

    if (isPartOfSchool) {
        const encounters = Array.isArray(individual.encounters) ? individual.encounters : [];

        const foundEligibleCancellation = encounters.some(enc =>
            enc &&
            enc.encounterType &&
            enc.encounterType.name === 'School Health - Screening Follow up' &&
            enc.cancelDateTime &&
            Array.isArray(enc.cancelObservations) &&
            enc.cancelObservations.some(obs => {
                if (
                    obs &&
                    obs.concept &&
                    obs.concept.uuid === 'f52e6c46-f982-4331-95c4-26bb36c48e72' &&
                    typeof obs.valueJSON === 'string'
                ) {
                    try {
                        const parsed = JSON.parse(obs.valueJSON);
                        return parsed.answer === '6250a8db-2bfc-4bc2-9f26-ca9e5ac41619';
                    } catch (e) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
                return false;
            })
        );

        eligibility = foundEligibleCancellation;
    } else {
        eligibility = true;
    }

    return eligibility;
};
```

### `Animedh Charitable Trust DNH` · `program[NK Home-based/PTOT intervention]`  (2270 bytes)

```javascript
//SAMPLE RULE EXAMPLE
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
    const individual = params.entity;
    const moment = imports.moment;
    let eligibility = true;
    const conceptNameUuid = [
        // {
        //     "uuid": "1658b9d7-91d9-4f4a-9d99-ddf7a5d9ece4",
        //     "name": "3 (single delay)"
        // },
        {
            "uuid": "99410a7c-ff27-48e4-802a-faa33bd388f5",
            "name": "4 (disability or multiple delays)"
        },
        // {
        //     "uuid": "09a8d981-a415-45ba-87c2-24f0434f5036",
        //     "name": "3a (single delay)"
        // },
        {
            "uuid": "06cf5f53-1305-43ff-8f00-7f3d18f70a96",
            "name": "4a (disability or multiple delays)"
        }
    ]

    const options = conceptNameUuid.map((option) => option.uuid)
    
    const isAlreadyEnrolledInProgram = individual.enrolments.some(enrolment => enrolment.program.name == "NK Home-based/PTOT intervention");
    if(isAlreadyEnrolledInProgram) return false;
    
    const age = individual.getAgeInYears(moment(), true);
    const ageISLessThan8Years = age <= 8;

    const enrolments = individual.enrolments
        .filter(enrl => enrl.program.name == 'Child Development Categorisation' && enrl.programExitDateTime == null)
    
    if(ageISLessThan8Years && enrolments && enrolments.length > 0) {
        const enrolment = enrolments[0];

        const mannualCategorization = enrolment.getEncountersOfType('Manual categorization')
            .filter(enc => enc.encounterDateTime);
        
        if(mannualCategorization && mannualCategorization.length > 0){
            const latestEncounter = mannualCategorization[0];

            const selectedChildCategoryACT = latestEncounter.getObservationValue('3752c3dc-8067-48ea-b96d-55cc34320e94') || [];
            const selectedChildCategoryACT2 = latestEncounter.getObservationValue('9df88162-f16c-463c-b919-187c55512ba4') || [];

            const selectedOptions = [...(selectedChildCategoryACT || []), ...(selectedChildCategoryACT2 || [])];

            const isSelectedInOptions = selectedOptions.some(option => options.includes(option));
            
            eligibility = isSelectedInOptions;

            return eligibility;
        } 
    }

    return false;
};
```

### `MLD Trust` · `program[Malnutrition Program]`  (1106 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
  const individual = params.entity;
  const moment = imports.moment;
  let eligibility = true;

  const childEnrollments = individual.enrolments
        .filter((enrl) => enrl.program.name == "Child" && enrl.voided == false && enrl.enrolmentDateTime);
  
  let ageInMonths = individual.getAgeInMonths(); 
  if (ageInMonths < 6 || ageInMonths > 60) {
      eligibility = false; 
  }
  
  if (childEnrollments.length === 0) {
      eligibility = false;
  } else {
      const childEnrollment = childEnrollments[0];
      let gradationScreeningEnc = childEnrollment.getEncountersOfType('Child Gradation Screening');
      if(gradationScreeningEnc.length > 0){
          let malnutritionScreeningRequired = gradationScreeningEnc[0].getObservationReadableValue('e57e0d12-3ccb-4d21-a6a5-a76aeeec2647');
          if (ageInMonths < 6 || ageInMonths > 60) {
            eligibility = false; 
          } else {
            eligibility = (malnutritionScreeningRequired === 'Yes');
          }
      } else {
        eligibility = false;
      }
  }
  
  return eligibility;
};
```

## `eligibility-member` — 4 extracted, showing 4 representatives

### `Goonj` · `subjectType[Village]`  (3978 bytes)

```javascript
"use strict";

({params, imports}) => {
    const member = params.member;
    const group = params.group;
    
    if (!member || !group) {
        return {
            eligible: {
                value: false,
                message: "Member or group information is missing"
            }
        };
    }

    const memberAddress = member.lowestAddressLevel;
    const groupAddress = group.lowestAddressLevel;

    if (!memberAddress || !groupAddress) {
        return {
            eligible: {
                value: false,
                message: "Address information is missing for either member or group"
            }
        };
    }

    // Check if either address is "Other"
    const isMemberOther = memberAddress && memberAddress.name === 'Other';
    const isGroupOther = groupAddress && groupAddress.name === 'Other';

    // If neither is "Other", compare UUIDs
    if (!isMemberOther && !isGroupOther) {
        if (memberAddress.uuid !== groupAddress.uuid) {
            return {
                eligible: {
                    value: false,
                    message: "Address levels do not match"
                }
            };
        }
        return { eligible: { value: true } };
    }

    // Handle cases where at least one address is "Other"
    const getBlockFromAddress = (address) => {
        if (!address) return undefined;
        
        // Check locationMappings path
        if (address.locationMappings && 
            Array.isArray(address.locationMappings) && 
            address.locationMappings[0] && 
            address.locationMappings[0].parent && 
            address.locationMappings[0].parent.name) {
            return address.locationMappings[0].parent.name;
        }
        
        // Fallback to titleLineage
        if (address.titleLineage) {
            const titleParts = address.titleLineage.split(', ');
            return titleParts.length > 2 ? titleParts[2] : undefined;
        }
        
        return undefined;
    };

    const getActualAddress = (entity, address) => {
        if (!entity || !address) return address;
        
        if (address.name === 'Other') {
            const otherVillageUuid = "16b4db7c-e0a8-41f1-ac67-07470a762d9f";
            const observationValue = entity.getObservationValue ? entity.getObservationValue(otherVillageUuid) : null;
            const observationReadable = entity.getObservationReadableValue ? entity.getObservationReadableValue(otherVillageUuid) : null;
            
            return {
                name: observationReadable || address.name,
                uuid: observationValue || address.uuid,
                locationMappings: address.locationMappings,
                titleLineage: address.titleLineage
            };
        }
        return address;
    };

    const actualMemberAddress = getActualAddress(member, memberAddress);
    const actualGroupAddress = getActualAddress(group, groupAddress);

    // Compare villages
    if (!actualMemberAddress || !actualGroupAddress || !actualMemberAddress.name || !actualGroupAddress.name) {
        return {
            eligible: {
                value: false,
                message: "Missing village information for comparison"
            }
        };
    }

    if (actualMemberAddress.name !== actualGroupAddress.name) {
        return {
            eligible: {
                value: false,
                message: `Village mismatch: ${actualMemberAddress.name} vs ${actualGroupAddress.name}`
            }
        };
    }

    // Compare blocks if available
    const memberBlock = getBlockFromAddress(actualMemberAddress);
    const groupBlock = getBlockFromAddress(actualGroupAddress);
    
    if (memberBlock && groupBlock && memberBlock !== groupBlock) {
        return {
            eligible: {
                value: false,
                message: `Block mismatch: ${memberBlock} vs ${groupBlock}`
            }
        };
    }

    return { eligible: { value: true } };
};
```

### `atul_uat` · `subjectType[Household]`  (1334 bytes)

```javascript
"use strict";
({params, imports}) => {
  const member = params.member;
  const group = params.group;
  
  const memberAddess = member.individual.lowestAddressLevel.name;
  const grpAddess = group.lowestAddressLevel.name;
   
  const faliyaUUID = '4c23ae7d-6f0c-4924-93f2-7296bfe96fef';
  const schoolUUID = '79f9fc96-7817-491c-a202-9e98ead1574c';
  
  const memberAddessType = member.individual.lowestAddressLevel.type;
  const memberAddessTypeUUID = member.individual.lowestAddressLevel.typeUuid;
  
  if(memberAddessType == 'Faliya' || memberAddessTypeUUID == faliyaUUID){
    if (memberAddess != grpAddess) {
      return {
        eligible: {
          value: false,
          message: "Individual address should be same as Household address"
        }
      };
    }
  }else if(memberAddessType == 'School' || memberAddessTypeUUID == schoolUUID){
    const individualVillageSddress = member.individual.lowestAddressLevel.locationMappings[0].parent.uuid;
    const grpVillageAddress = group.lowestAddressLevel.locationMappings[0].parent.uuid;

    if (individualVillageSddress != grpVillageAddress) {
      return {
        eligible: {
          value: false,
          message: "Individual village should be same as Household village"
        }
      };
    }
  }

  
  
  return {
    eligible: {
      value: true
    }
  };
};
```

## `enrolmentSummary` — 26 extracted, showing 15 representatives

Sources: APF Odisha, Animedh Charitable Trust DNH, Ashwini uat, Centre for Social Justice (CSJ), JNPCT, JSS, MLD Trust, atul_uat, …

### `MLD Trust` · `program[Pregnancy]`  (9773 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) =>  {
    const summaries = [];
    const programEnrolment = params.programEnrolment;
    const moment = imports.moment;
    
    const lmp = programEnrolment.findObservationInEntireEnrolment('LMP');
    if(lmp){
      var lmpDate = imports.moment(lmp.getValue()).toDate();
      summaries.push({name: 'LMP', value: lmpDate});
    }
    
    const edd = programEnrolment.findObservationInEntireEnrolment('EDD');
    if(edd) {
      var eddDate = imports.moment(edd.getValue()).toDate();
      summaries.push({name: 'EDD', value: eddDate});
    }
    
    let highRisk = programEnrolment.getObservationValue('cccfc848-b816-485a-86f7-e9d8369dda5d');
    if(highRisk && highRisk.length) {
      summaries.push({name: 'History of High Risk Factors', value: [...highRisk]});
    }
    
    let allANC = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('ANC');
    const ANCSeq = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < allANC.length; i++){
      const enc = allANC[i];
      let cancelQuestionValueUuid = enc.getObservationReadableValue("b18abde6-b61d-489a-b16d-9e77b1a23caf");
      if(cancelQuestionValueUuid === 'No'){
        let ancType = enc.getObservationReadableValue("38581f86-92f4-4777-9c57-781f9ace43ab");
        let seq = ancType.split(" ")[1];
        ANCSeq[i] = seq;
      }
    }
    const sortedArr = [...ANCSeq].sort((a, b) => b - a);
    const firstValue = sortedArr[0];
    const latestANC = ANCSeq.indexOf(firstValue);
  
    if(allANC[latestANC]) {
      let ancHighRisk = allANC[latestANC].getObservationValue('69b35743-2b4f-47fd-8a64-5218fd1d89ef');
      if(ancHighRisk && ancHighRisk.length) {
        summaries.push({name: 'ANC: High Risk Conditions', value:[...ancHighRisk]});
      }
    }

    let allPNC = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('PNC');
    const PNCSeq = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < allPNC.length; i++){
      const enc = allPNC[i];
      let cancelQuestionValueUuid = enc.getObservationReadableValue("bfc0a835-f5e9-42b3-8208-41d5e4df5761");
      if(cancelQuestionValueUuid === 'No'){
        let pncType = enc.getObservationReadableValue("6877fa7e-d224-47ff-8213-54fb982afad6");
        let seq = pncType.split(" ")[1];
        PNCSeq[i] = seq;
      }
    }
    const sortedArr = [...PNCSeq].sort((a, b) => b - a);
    const firstValue = sortedArr[0];
    const latestPNC = PNCSeq.indexOf(firstValue);
    
    if(allPNC[latestPNC]) {
      let pncHighRisk = allPNC[latestPNC].getObservationValue('cef32768-7388-4588-820d-82b65af1a0b8');
      if(pncHighRisk && pncHighRisk.length) {
        summaries.push({name: 'PNC: High Risk Conditions', value:[...pncHighRisk]});
      }
    }

    let ancEncounters = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('ANC');
    let deliveryEncounters = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('Delivery');
  
    let allANCs = ['ANC 1', 'ANC 2', 'ANC 3', 'ANC 4', 'ANC 5', 'ANC 6', 'ANC 7', 'ANC 8', 'ANC 9'];
    let compeletedOrCancelledANCs = [];
    let compeletedANCs = [];
    let cancelledANCs = [];
    
    const today = moment().startOf('day');
    const daysSinceLMP = today.diff(lmpDate, 'days');
    const overDueANC = [];
    
    const schedule = [
        {min:   0, max:  90, sequence: 1},
        {min:  90, max: 120, sequence: 2},
        {min: 120, max: 180, sequence: 3},
        {min: 180, max: 210, sequence: 4},
        {min: 210, max: 240, sequence: 5},
        {min: 240, max: 247, sequence: 6},
        {min: 247, max: 254, sequence: 7},
        {min: 254, max: 261, sequence: 8},
        {min: 261, max: 268, sequence: 9}
    ];
    
    if(deliveryEncounters.length == 0 && daysSinceLMP < 270){
      let completedSequences = ancEncounters
          .map(enc => parseInt(enc.getObservationReadableValue("5ee51584-6d54-496c-8a5f-bb7958662bb5")))
          .filter(seq => !isNaN(seq));
      
      let currentANC = schedule.find(s => daysSinceLMP >= s.min && daysSinceLMP < s.max);
      let nextANC = currentANC ? `ANC ${currentANC.sequence}` : null;
      
      let overdueANCs = schedule
          .filter(s => daysSinceLMP >= s.max && !completedSequences.includes(s.sequence))
          .map(s => `ANC ${s.sequence}`);
      
      let isAncCancel = ancEncounters.length > 0 
          ? (ancEncounters[0].getObservationReadableValue('b18abde6-b61d-489a-b16d-9e77b1a23caf') || '').trim()
          : '';
      
      if (nextANC && !completedSequences.includes(currentANC.sequence) && isAncCancel !== 'This ANC and all remaining ANC') {
          summaries.push({ name: 'ANCs to be done', value: [nextANC] });
      }
      if (overdueANCs.length > 0 && isAncCancel !== 'This ANC and all remaining ANC') {
          summaries.push({ name: 'Overdue ANC(s)', value: overdueANCs });
      }
    }
    
    for (let i = 0; i < ancEncounters.length; i++){
      const enc = ancEncounters[i];
      let ancType = ancEncounters[i].getObservationReadableValue("38581f86-92f4-4777-9c57-781f9ace43ab");
      let cancelQuestionValueUuid = enc.getObservationReadableValue("b18abde6-b61d-489a-b16d-9e77b1a23caf");
      if(cancelQuestionValueUuid === 'No'){
        const index = allANCs.indexOf(ancType);
        compeletedANCs.push(ancType);
        compeletedOrCancelledANCs.push(ancType);
      } else {
        const index = allANCs.indexOf(ancType);
        cancelledANCs.push(ancType);
        compeletedOrCancelledANCs.push(ancType);
      }
    }
    
    if(compeletedANCs.length > 0) {
      compeletedANCs.sort((a, b) => {
        const matchA = a.match(/\d+/); 
        const matchB = b.match(/\d+/);
        const numA = matchA ? parseInt(matchA[0], 10) : 0; 
        const numB = matchB ? parseInt(matchB[0], 10) : 0;
        return numA - numB; 
      });
      summaries.push({name: 'ANCs Done', value: compeletedANCs});
    }
    
    if(cancelledANCs.length > 0) {
      cancelledANCs.sort((a, b) => {
        const matchA = a.match(/\d+/); 
        const matchB = b.match(/\d+/);
        const numA = matchA ? parseInt(matchA[0], 10) : 0; 
        const numB = matchB ? parseInt(matchB[0], 10) : 0;
        return numA - numB; 
      });
      summaries.push({name: 'ANCs Cancelled', value: cancelledANCs});
    }
    
  
    let pncEncounters = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('PNC');
  
    let allPNCs = ['PNC 1', 'PNC 2', 'PNC 3', 'PNC 4', 'PNC 5', 'PNC 6', 'PNC 7'];
    let compeletedOrCancelledPNCs = [];
    let compeletedPNCs = [];
    let cancelledPNCs = [];
    const overDuePNC = [];
    
    let daysSinceDelivery = 0;
    if(deliveryEncounters.length){
      const dateOfDeliveryUUID = "76d5d509-ca1b-45b7-863f-536d5f65d06f";
      let deliveryDate = moment(deliveryEncounters[0].getObservationValue(dateOfDeliveryUUID)).startOf('day');
      const today = moment().startOf('day');
      daysSinceDelivery = today.diff(deliveryDate, 'days');
    }

    const schedulePNC = [
        {min:  0, max:  1, sequence:1},
        {min:  3, max:  4, sequence:2},
        {min:  7, max:  8, sequence:3},
        {min: 14, max: 15, sequence:4},
        {min: 21, max: 22, sequence:5},
        {min: 28, max: 29, sequence:6},
        {min: 42, max: 43, sequence:7}
    ];
  
    if(deliveryEncounters.length > 0 && daysSinceDelivery < 50){
      let completedSequences = pncEncounters
          .map(enc => parseInt(enc.getObservationReadableValue("5ee51584-6d54-496c-8a5f-bb7958662bb5")))
          .filter(seq => !isNaN(seq));
    
      let currentPNC = schedulePNC.find(s => daysSinceDelivery >= s.min && daysSinceDelivery < s.max);
      let nextPNC = currentPNC ? `PNC ${currentPNC.sequence}` : null;
      
      let overduePNCs = schedulePNC
          .filter(s => daysSinceDelivery >= s.max && !completedSequences.includes(s.sequence))
          .map(s => `PNC ${s.sequence}`);
      
      let isPncCancel = pncEncounters.length > 0 
          ? (pncEncounters[0].getObservationReadableValue('bfc0a835-f5e9-42b3-8208-41d5e4df5761') || '').trim()
          : '';
      
      if (nextPNC && !completedSequences.includes(currentPNC.sequence) && isPncCancel !== 'This PNC and all remaining PNC') {
          summaries.push({ name: 'Mother PNC to be done', value: [nextPNC] });
      }
      if (overduePNCs.length > 0 && isPncCancel !== 'This PNC and all remaining PNC') {
          summaries.push({ name: 'Overdue PNC(s)', value: overduePNCs });
      }
    }
    
    for (let i = 0; i < pncEncounters.length; i++){
      const enc = pncEncounters[i];
      let pncType = pncEncounters[i].getObservationReadableValue("6877fa7e-d224-47ff-8213-54fb982afad6");
      let cancelQuestionValueUuid = enc.getObservationReadableValue("bfc0a835-f5e9-42b3-8208-41d5e4df5761");
      
      if(cancelQuestionValueUuid === 'No'){
        const index = allPNCs.indexOf(pncType);
        compeletedPNCs.push(pncType);
        compeletedOrCancelledPNCs.push(pncType);
      } else {
        const index = allPNCs.indexOf(pncType);
        cancelledPNCs.push(pncType);
        compeletedOrCancelledPNCs.push(pncType);
      }
    }
    
    if(compeletedPNCs.length > 0) {
      compeletedPNCs.sort((a, b) => {
        const matchA = a.match(/\d+/); 
        const matchB = b.match(/\d+/);
        const numA = matchA ? parseInt(matchA[0], 10) : 0; 
        const numB = matchB ? parseInt(matchB[0], 10) : 0;
        return numA - numB; 
      });
      summaries.push({name: 'Mother PNCs Done', value: compeletedPNCs});
    }
    
    if(cancelledPNCs.length > 0) {
      cancelledPNCs.sort((a, b) => {
        const matchA = a.match(/\d+/); 
        const matchB = b.match(/\d+/);
        const numA = matchA ? parseInt(matchA[0], 10) : 0; 
        const numB = matchB ? parseInt(matchB[0], 10) : 0;
        return numA - numB; 
      });
      summaries.push({name: 'Mother PNCs Cancelled', value: cancelledPNCs});
    }  
    return summaries;
};
```

### `Animedh Charitable Trust DNH` · `program[NK Home-based/PTOT intervention]`  (6559 bytes)

```javascript
//SAMPLE RULE EXAMPLE
'use strict';
({params, imports}) =>  {
    const summaries = [];
    const moment = imports.moment;
    const programEnrolment = params.programEnrolment;
    const _ = imports.lodash;

    const goalsSetting = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType("Goal setting");
    if (goalsSetting && goalsSetting.length > 0) {
        const latestGoalSetting = goalsSetting[0]; // Assuming the first encounter is the latest
        let latestOnGoingShortTermGoal = [];
        let latestOnGoingLongTermGoal = [];
        const allGoals = [];
        const shortTermGoal = latestGoalSetting.getObservationValue("4279fd3d-95e8-4f53-b714-1c21f97605ae");
        
        if (shortTermGoal && shortTermGoal.length > 0) {
            for (let i = 0; i < shortTermGoal.length; i++) {
                const questionGroup = shortTermGoal[i];
                const typeOfGoal = questionGroup.findObservationByConceptUUID('7060522e-e88b-470a-aaa6-ceba986e4169');
                const status = questionGroup.findObservationByConceptUUID('cd5304c5-870f-420b-8929-1fc8b3dd9be3');
                const descriptions = questionGroup.findObservationByConceptUUID('29700074-36f5-4245-8aba-3c57ffa839f8');
                if (typeOfGoal && status && status.getReadableValue() == 'Ongoing' && descriptions) {
                    latestOnGoingShortTermGoal.push(descriptions.getReadableValue());
                }
            }
        }

        const longTermGoal = latestGoalSetting.getObservationValue("7d06a63c-4f78-4519-acec-9b8bc1a85fce");
        
        if (longTermGoal && longTermGoal.length > 0) {
            for (let i = 0; i < longTermGoal.length; i++) {
                const questionGroup = longTermGoal[i];
                const typeOfGoal = questionGroup.findObservationByConceptUUID('7060522e-e88b-470a-aaa6-ceba986e4169');
                const status = questionGroup.findObservationByConceptUUID('cd5304c5-870f-420b-8929-1fc8b3dd9be3');
                const descriptions = questionGroup.findObservationByConceptUUID('29700074-36f5-4245-8aba-3c57ffa839f8');
                if (typeOfGoal && status && status.getReadableValue() == 'Ongoing' && descriptions) {
                    latestOnGoingLongTermGoal.push(descriptions.getReadableValue());
                }
            }
        }
        
        const uniqueShortTermGoals = _.uniq(latestOnGoingShortTermGoal);
        if (uniqueShortTermGoals.length > 0) {
            const goalsText = uniqueShortTermGoals.join(' || ');
            summaries.push({name: "Latest on-going Short Term Goals", value: goalsText});
        }
        const uniqueLongTermGoals = _.uniq(latestOnGoingLongTermGoal);
        if (uniqueLongTermGoals.length > 0) {
            const goalsText = uniqueLongTermGoals.join(' || ');
            summaries.push({name: "Latest on-going Long Term Goals", value: goalsText});
        }
    }

    const recommendedActivities = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType("Recommended Activities");
    if (recommendedActivities && recommendedActivities.length > 0) {
        const latestRecommendedActivity = recommendedActivities[0];
        const activitiesAndStatus = latestRecommendedActivity.getObservationValue("Activity and their status");
        if (activitiesAndStatus && activitiesAndStatus.length > 0) {
            const activityNamePTOP = [];
            const activityNameCDA = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < activitiesAndStatus.length; i++) {
                const item = activitiesAndStatus[i];
                const activity = item.findObservationByConceptUUID('37dbc501-19ae-406d-b1de-0bab5c3743d4');
                const recommendedBy = item.findObservationByConceptUUID('b8571b8b-7185-4b3b-bd6a-20c6ab6efaa5');
                if (activity && recommendedBy && recommendedBy.getReadableValue() == 'PTOT') {
                    activityNamePTOP.push(activity.getReadableValue());
                } 
                if (activity && recommendedBy && recommendedBy.getReadableValue() == 'CDA') {
                    activityNameCDA.push(activity.getReadableValue());
                }
            }

            if (activityNamePTOP.length > 0) {
                const activityTextPTOP = activityNamePTOP.join(' || ');
                summaries.push({name: "Latest on-going activity recommended (PTOP)", value: activityTextPTOP});
            }
            
            if (activityNameCDA.length > 0) {
                const activityTextCDA = activityNameCDA.join(' || ');
                summaries.push({name: "Latest on-going activity recommended (CDA)", value: activityTextCDA});
            }
        }
    }
    
    const ptotTherapyVisit = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType("PTOT therapy visit")
        .filter(encounter => encounter.encounterDateTime != null);
    let latestPTOTEncounterDateTime = moment().startOf('day');
    let secondLatestPTOTEncounterDateTime = moment(programEnrolment.enrolmentDateTime).startOf('day');

    if(ptotTherapyVisit && ptotTherapyVisit.length > 0) {
        latestPTOTEncounterDateTime = moment(ptotTherapyVisit[0].encounterDateTime).startOf('day');
        if(ptotTherapyVisit.length > 1) {

            secondLatestPTOTEncounterDateTime = moment(ptotTherapyVisit[1].encounterDateTime).startOf('day');
        }
    }
    
    const homeBasedTherapyVisit = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('Home-based therapy visit')
        .filter(encounter => {
            if (!encounter.encounterDateTime) return false;
            const currentEncounterDateTime = moment(encounter.encounterDateTime).startOf('day');
            return currentEncounterDateTime.isSameOrAfter(secondLatestPTOTEncounterDateTime, 'day') 
                    && currentEncounterDateTime.isSameOrBefore(latestPTOTEncounterDateTime, 'day');
        });

    
    if(homeBasedTherapyVisit && homeBasedTherapyVisit.length > 0) {
        let observations = [];
        for(let i = homeBasedTherapyVisit.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const encounter = homeBasedTherapyVisit[i];
            const encounterDateTime = moment(encounter.encounterDateTime).format('YYYY-MM-DD');
            const observation = encounter.getObservationReadableValue("4d469b3c-6a16-4458-8cbf-167438b02067");
            if(observation) {
                observations.push(`${encounterDateTime} - ${observation}`);
            }
        }
        if(observations.length > 0) {
            summaries.push({name: "Prior observations of CDA from home-based therapy visits", value: observations.join(' || ')});
        }
    }
    
    return summaries;
};
```

### `atul_uat` · `program[Pregnancy]`  (4817 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) =>  {
    const summaries = [];
    
    const programEnrolment = params.programEnrolment;
   
    const edd =  programEnrolment.findObservationInEntireEnrolment('Estimated Date of Delivery');
    if (edd) {
        summaries.push({name: 'Estimated Date of Delivery', value: edd.getValue()});
    }
        
    const lmp =  programEnrolment.findObservationInEntireEnrolment('Last menstrual period');
    if (lmp) {
        summaries.push({name: 'Last menstrual period', value: lmp.getValue()});
    }
    
    const healthFacility =  programEnrolment.findObservationInEntireEnrolment('If registered at health facility');
    if (healthFacility) {
        summaries.push({name: 'If registered at health facility', value: [healthFacility.getValue()]});
    }
  
    const ancFollowups = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('ANC - Follow Up', true);
    if (ancFollowups && ancFollowups.length > 0){
        const latestancFollowups = ancFollowups[0];

        const getObs = conceptName => latestancFollowups.getObservationValue(conceptName);

        const height = getObs('Height');
        if (height) {
            summaries.push({name: 'Height', value: height});
        }
        
        const weight = getObs('Weight');
        if (weight) {
            summaries.push({name: 'Weight', value: weight});
        }
        
        const haemoglobin = getObs('Haemoglobin');
        if (haemoglobin) {
            summaries.push({name: 'Haemoglobin', value: haemoglobin});
        }
        
        const dbp = getObs('Diastolic Blood pressure');
        if (dbp) {
            summaries.push({name: 'Diastolic Blood pressure', value: dbp});
        }
        
        const sbp = getObs('Systolic Blood pressure');
        if (sbp) {
            summaries.push({name: 'Systolic Blood pressure', value: sbp});
        }
        
        const complication = getObs('Any complications in current pregnancy');
        if (complication) {
            summaries.push({name: 'Any complications in current pregnancy', value: complication});
        }
        
        const healthFacility = getObs('If registered at health facility');
        if (healthFacility) {
            summaries.push({name: 'If registered at health facility', value: [healthFacility]});
        }
        
        const cPointsPreg = getObs('Counselling done on which of the following topic - pregnancy');
        if (cPointsPreg) {
            summaries.push({name: 'Counselling done on which of the following topic - pregnancy', value: cPointsPreg});
        }
    }

    //-- DELIVERY 
    let delivery = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('Delivery', true);
    if (delivery && delivery.length > 0){
        delivery = delivery[0];
        const getObs = conceptName => delivery.getObservationValue(conceptName);

        const deliveryDate = getObs('Date of Delivery');
        if (deliveryDate) {
            summaries.push({name: 'Date of Delivery', value: deliveryDate});
        } 
        
        const timeOfDelivery = getObs('Time of Delivery');
        if (timeOfDelivery) {
            summaries.push({name: 'Time of Delivery', value: timeOfDelivery});
        }   
            
        const placeOfDelivery = getObs('Place of delivery (facility type)');
        if (placeOfDelivery) {
            summaries.push({name: 'Place of delivery (facility type)', value: [placeOfDelivery]});
        }
        
        const birthWeight = getObs('Birth Weight');
        if (birthWeight) {
            summaries.push({name: 'Birth Weight', value: birthWeight});
        } 
        
        const birthDose = getObs('Which of the following birth dose vaccine was given');
        if (birthDose) {
            summaries.push({name: 'Which of the following birth dose vaccine was given', value: birthDose});
        }    
           
    }
    //-- PNC 

    const pnc = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('PNC', true);
    if (pnc && pnc.length > 0){
        const latestpnc = pnc[0];
    
        const getObs = conceptName => latestpnc.getObservationValue(conceptName);
    
        const breastFeed = getObs('Do you breastfeed your child');
        if (breastFeed) {
            summaries.push({name: 'Do you breastfeed your child', value: [breastFeed]});
        }
        
        const method = getObs('Which of the following Family Planning method is practiced');
        if (method) {
            summaries.push({name: 'Which of the following Family Planning method is practiced', value: method});
        }
        
        const counsellingP = getObs('Counselling done on which of the following topic');
        if (counsellingP) {
            summaries.push({name: 'Counselling done on which of the following topic', value: counsellingP});
        }
    }
    
    return summaries;
};
```

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) =>  {
    const summaries = [];
    const programEnrolment = params.programEnrolment;
    
    const lmp = programEnrolment.findLatestObservationInEntireEnrolment("Last menstrual period").getReadableValue();

       // var lmpDate = moment(lmp);
        var currentDate = imports.moment();
               
        var months = imports.moment(currentDate).diff(lmp, 'months');
        if (!_.isNil(months)) {
            summaries.push({name: 'Pregnancy Month', value: months});
        }

    const highRiskEnrolment  = programEnrolment.findLatestObservationFromEncounters('High Risk Conditions') ||           
          programEnrolment.findObservationInEntireEnrolment('High Risk Conditions');
  if (highRiskEnrolment) {
      summaries.push({name: 'High Risk Conditions', value: highRiskEnrolment.getValue()});
    }

    const ironTab = programEnrolment
    .getObservationsForConceptName('IF YES THEN WRITE NUMBER OF TABLET SWALLOWED');
        if (!_.isEmpty(ironTab)) {
          const value = ironTab.map(({encounterDateTime, obs}) =>
           (`${imports.moment(encounterDateTime).format("DD-MM-YYYY")}: ${obs}`)).join(", ");
            summaries.push({name: "Iron Tablet Consumed", value: value})
        }

        const calciumTab = programEnrolment
        .getObservationsForConceptName('IF YES THEN WRITE NUMBER OF CALCIUM TABLET SWALLOWED');
        if (!_.isEmpty(calciumTab)) {
            const value = calciumTab.map(({encounterDateTime, obs}) => 
              (`${imports.moment(encounterDateTime).format("DD-MM-YYYY")}: ${obs}`)).join(", ");
            summaries.push({name: "Calcium Tablet Consumed", value: value})
        }

        const sickleCellTest = programEnrolment.
        findLatestObservationFromEncounters("IF YES, result of sickle cell test");
        const sickleCellTestResult = sickleCellTest && sickleCellTest.getReadableValue();
        if (!_.isNil(sickleCellTestResult)) {
            summaries.push({name: 'Sickle Cell Result', value: sickleCellTestResult});
        }

        const hb = programEnrolment.getObservationsForConceptName('H.B');
        if (!_.isEmpty(hb)) {
            const value = hb.map(({encounterDateTime, obs}) => 
            (`${imports.moment(encounterDateTime).format("DD-MM-YYYY")}: ${obs}`)).join(", ");
            summaries.push({name: "HB", value: value})
        }

        const bpSystolic = programEnrolment.getObservationsForConceptName("B.P - Systolic");
        const bpDiastolic = programEnrolment.getObservationsForConceptName("B.P - Diastolic"); 
        
        if (!_.isEmpty(bpSystolic) && !_.isEmpty(bpDiastolic)) {            
            var value = [];
            bpSystolic.forEach(element => {
                let display = imports.moment(element.encounterDateTime).format("DD-MM-YYYY") + ': ' + element.obs; 
                value.push(display);
            });
            bpDiastolic.forEach(element => {
                for (var i = 0; i < bpSystolic.length ; i++) {                    
                    if(_.isEqual(bpSystolic[i].encounterDateTime, element.encounterDateTime)){
                        let display =  value[i] + '|' + element.obs;
                        value[i] = display;
                    } 
                }               
            });

            summaries.push({name: "Blood Pressure", value: value})
        }


    const muacCount = programEnrolment.getObservationsForConceptName('MUAC (in cms)');
        if (!_.isEmpty(muacCount)) {
          const value = muacCount.map(({encounterDateTime, obs}) => 
        (`${imports.moment(encounterDateTime).format("DD-MM-YYYY")}: ${obs}`)).join(", ");
        summaries.push({name: "MUAC", value: value})
      }

    return summaries;
};
```

```javascript
({ params }) => {
    const summaries = [];
    const { programEnrolment } = params;
  const { individual } = programEnrolment;

    const actOffenceRights = [
        ['05865e79-c454-47c2-af03-fc719213b576', 'Theme of case', true],
        ['57df7482-20dc-4af0-87ce-c4110df7fdf3', 'Act / Offence (womens right)', true],
        ['3db5dae3-5b17-4d0d-9a4d-f1878d4c2243', 'Act / offence (land rights)', true],
        ['30df70d5-96c0-4147-95a3-d6196b1e7924', 'Act / offence (labour rights)', true],
        ['2897555a-ba05-4dfe-9e34-7e98395c0792', 'Act / offence (consumer)', true],
        ['8ffdedc8-d8e4-4808-aa60-7b074a1a5958', 'Act / offence (prisoners rights)', true],
        ['ed44e193-f685-4911-b997-ba04b007e8ad', 'Act/ offence (child rights)',true],
        ['2c886435-bb26-4206-9fd4-7811083a5871', 'Act / offence (Rights of fisherfolk)', true],
        ['197b8fef-49a9-428d-adda-f8c9f1f800da', 'Act / offence (minority rights)', true],
        ['74056c05-df3f-4e9e-aa2f-ffcedc98cd16', 'Rights of SC/ST community', true],
        ['0122fe96-a79b-4f6f-8ed2-c050998cb642', 'Act / offence (financial fraud)', true],
        ['0127d408-91fb-4eb1-906f-febbc882f69b', 'Act / offence (Social Boycott)', true],
        ['6779b8e0-f60d-446d-a371-e5d05931c69c', 'Primary Theme', false],
    ];
       
    const caseStatusStages = [
        ['252f4c55-d113-4951-8ebb-eed01b9ccbb9', 'Nature of Case', false],
        ['b1927aeb-ab4c-46a6-8eff-6ccdf38f5478', 'Status of case', false],
        ['c8bafd78-6fdf-48ac-bd54-f180b00ce46f', 'Stage of Active (pre-litigation)', false],
        ['065e005a-3203-4192-91ad-37e55480a695', 'Stage of Active (litigation) status', false],
        ['e1945ebb-19df-4055-b9c2-9fbcda959671', 'Stage of Active (pre-litigation) - Criminal Prosecution/ Defence fresh filing', false],
        ['ee698620-9b14-4379-a7de-6098c9f009b0', 'Stage of Active (litigation) status - Criminal Prosecution/ Defence fresh filing', false],
        ['c4d9571c-1f78-45d7-a415-83589595ae9d', 'Stage of Active (pre-litigation) - Civil / revenue / labour / consumer / family etc. fresh filing', false],
        ['aa7cfb95-21f2-48fb-9c28-e6a46c3d88a8', 'Stage of Active (litigation) status - Civil / revenue / labour / consumer / family etc. fresh filing', false]
    ];

    function addSummary(entity, id, name, isMultiSelect) {
        let observation = entity.findObservation(id);
        if (observation) {
            observation = observation.getValue();
            if (!isMultiSelect) {
                observation = [observation];
            }
            summaries.push({ name, value: observation });
        }
    }

    actOffenceRights.forEach(([id, name, isMultiSelect]) => addSummary(individual, id, name, isMultiSelect));
    
     const otherThemeOfCase = individual.findObservation('Others theme of case, please specify'); 
     if(otherThemeOfCase) {
      summaries.push({name: 'Others theme of case, please specify', value: otherThemeOfCase.getValue()});
     }
     
     let caseStatusDetails = individual.enrolments.filter((enr) => 
    enr.program.name === "Case Status" && 
    enr.programExitDateTime == null && 
    !enr.voided 
  );
  

    if (caseStatusDetails.length>0) {
        caseStatusDetails = caseStatusDetails[0];
        caseStatusStages.forEach(([id, name, isMultiSelect]) => addSummary(caseStatusDetails, id, name, isMultiSelect));
    }

    return summaries;
};
```

### `JSS` · `program[Phulwari]`  (1140 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) =>  {
    const summary = [];
    const _ = imports.lodash;
    const summaryForObservation = function (conceptName, programEnrolment, summary) {
        let observationValue = programEnrolment.findLatestObservationInEntireEnrolment(conceptName);
        if (!_.isNil(observationValue)) {
            summary.push({name: conceptName, value: observationValue.getValue()});
        }
    };
    const programEnrolment = params.programEnrolment;
    summaryForObservation("Weight for age z-score", programEnrolment, summary);
    summaryForObservation("Weight for age Grade", programEnrolment, summary);
    summaryForObservation("Weight for age Status", programEnrolment, summary);

    summaryForObservation("Height for age z-score", programEnrolment, summary);
    summaryForObservation("Height for age Grade", programEnrolment, summary);
    summaryForObservation("Height for age Status", programEnrolment, summary);

    summaryForObservation("Weight for height z-score", programEnrolment, summary);
    summaryForObservation("Weight for Height Status", programEnrolment, summary);
    return summary;
};
```

### `MLD Trust` · `program[Child]`  (8748 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) =>  {
    const summaries = [];
    const programEnrolment = params.programEnrolment;
    const moment = imports.moment;
    
    let currentWeight = null; 
    let currentHeight = null;
    let birthWeight = null;
    let birthHeight = null;

    const motherName = programEnrolment.individual.getObservationValue('314aa736-2663-4303-b309-6ee7f7cc5801');
    const selectedMotherName = programEnrolment.individual.getObservationReadableValue('670f4d67-851d-4c6e-acd4-7c537715c908');   
    if(motherName){
        summaries.push({name: "Name of Mother", value: motherName});
    } else if(selectedMotherName){
        summaries.push({name: "Mother's Name", value: selectedMotherName});
    }
  
    let highRisk = programEnrolment.getObservationValue('c3aa61b5-7010-4790-bc57-fea57b5d1129');
    if(highRisk && highRisk.length) {
        summaries.push({name: 'High risk condition for child', value: [...highRisk]});
    }
    
    let programWeight = programEnrolment.getObservationValue('148fb61a-9d85-4876-b20f-50235c0d1d6e');
    let programHeight = programEnrolment.getObservationValue('f9319bd3-de50-48df-873b-bdce97793855');
    
    let highRiskFromMother = programEnrolment.getObservationValue('c665033d-b172-4c1d-be72-c4161afea912');
    if(highRiskFromMother && highRiskFromMother.length) {
        summaries.push({name: "Child at High Risk due to mother's high risk condition", value: [...highRiskFromMother]});
    }
    
    let allPNC = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('Child PNC');
    let lastPNC = allPNC[0];
    if(lastPNC) {
        let pncHighRisk = lastPNC.getObservationValue('abea4537-be00-4135-b664-4922939db5cd');
        if(pncHighRisk && pncHighRisk.length) {
            summaries.push({name: 'High Risk Conditions Child PNC', value:[...pncHighRisk]});
        }
    }
    
    let birthFormEnc = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('Birth form');

    if (birthFormEnc.length > 0) {
        birthWeight = birthFormEnc[0].getObservationReadableValue('148fb61a-9d85-4876-b20f-50235c0d1d6e');
        birthHeight = birthFormEnc[0].getObservationReadableValue('f9319bd3-de50-48df-873b-bdce97793855');

    
        if (birthWeight< 2.5) {
            summaries.push({ name: "Low Birth Weight", value: birthWeight });
        } else {
            summaries.push({ name: "Birth Weight of Child", value:birthWeight});
        }


        
        let highRiskFromMother = birthFormEnc[0].getObservationValue('c665033d-b172-4c1d-be72-c4161afea912');
        if(highRiskFromMother && highRiskFromMother.length) {
            summaries.push({name: "Child at High Risk due to mother's high risk condition", value: [...highRiskFromMother]});
        }
    }
    
    let monthGradationFormEnc = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('Child Monthly Gradation Form');
    if(monthGradationFormEnc.length > 0) {
    
        currentWeight = monthGradationFormEnc[0].getObservationReadableValue('148fb61a-9d85-4876-b20f-50235c0d1d6e');

        let weightPercentile = monthGradationFormEnc[0].getObservationReadableValue('3b03765e-6abf-421e-b2f2-e233bbf4989c');
        if(weightPercentile) {
            summaries.push({name: "Weight percentile", value: weightPercentile});
        }
        
        currentHeight = monthGradationFormEnc[0].getObservationReadableValue('f9319bd3-de50-48df-873b-bdce97793855');
        
        let heightPercentile = monthGradationFormEnc[0].getObservationReadableValue('45eaf53d-670a-4a70-afec-522ee410911f');
        if(heightPercentile) {
            summaries.push({name: "Height percentile", value: heightPercentile});
        }  
    }
    
    let gradationScreeningEnc = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('Child Gradation Screening');
    if(gradationScreeningEnc.length > 0) {
        let malnutritionScreeningRequired = gradationScreeningEnc[0].getObservationValue('e57e0d12-3ccb-4d21-a6a5-a76aeeec2647');
        if(malnutritionScreeningRequired) {
            summaries.push({name: "Malnutrition Screening Required", value: malnutritionScreeningRequired});
        } 
    }
    
    if(currentWeight) {
        summaries.push({name: "Weight", value: currentWeight});
    } else if (birthWeight){
        summaries.push({name: "Weight", value: birthWeight});
    } else if(programWeight) {
        summaries.push({name: "Weight", value: programWeight});
    }
    
    if(currentHeight) {
        summaries.push({name: "Height", value: currentHeight});
    } else if(birthHeight) {
        summaries.push({name: "Height", value: birthHeight});
    } else if(programHeight) {
        summaries.push({name: "Height", value: programHeight});
    }
    
    let pncEncounters = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('Child PNC');

    let birthDate = moment(programEnrolment.individual.dateOfBirth).startOf('day');
    const today = moment().startOf('day');
    const daysSinceBirth = today.diff(birthDate, 'days');

    
    function hasOverDue(schedule, daysBetween, pncType){
      const isOverDue = schedule.some((obj) => obj.name == pncType && obj.max < daysBetween)
      return isOverDue;
    }
    
    function hasDue(schedule, dateOfBirth) {
        const daysBetween = moment().diff(dateOfBirth, 'days');
    
        const currentSchedule = schedule.filter((obj) => daysBetween >= obj.min && daysBetween <= obj.max);
        
        return currentSchedule.length > 0 ? currentSchedule[0].name : null;
    }
    

    let allPNCs = ['PNC 1(Day1)', 'PNC 2(Day 3)', 'PNC 3(Day 7)', 'PNC 4(Day 14)', 'PNC 5(Day 21)', 'PNC 6(Day 28)', 'PNC 7(Day 42/45)'];
    if(true){
        const pncsDone = pncEncounters
            .filter((enc) => enc.getObservationValue('e963732c-cba1-4528-9e69-9123e34651e1') == '81b675d6-277b-46c8-9e9d-703d940c7f69')
            .map((enc) => enc.getObservationReadableValue('44787f0e-609d-45df-b9da-a405183d6f13'));

        const pncsCancelled = pncEncounters
            .filter((enc) => enc.getObservationValue('e963732c-cba1-4528-9e69-9123e34651e1') == '6f9b17e0-d94f-4378-a38c-cdef509d3804')
            .map((enc) => enc.getObservationReadableValue('44787f0e-609d-45df-b9da-a405183d6f13'));
        
        const schedule = [
            {name: 'PNC 1(Day1)', min: 0, max: 1},
            {name: 'PNC 2(Day 3)', min: 3, max: 4},
            {name: 'PNC 3(Day 7)', min: 7, max: 8},
            {name: 'PNC 4(Day 14)', min: 14, max: 15},
            {name: 'PNC 5(Day 21)', min: 21, max: 22},
            {name: 'PNC 6(Day 28)', min: 28, max: 29},
            {name: 'PNC 7(Day 42/45)', min: 42, max: 43}
        ];

        let childPNCsDone = [];
        let childPNCsCancelled = [];
        let toBeDone= [];
        let childPNCsOverDue = [];

        for(let i = 0; i < allPNCs.length; i++){
            const pncType = allPNCs[i]
            if(pncsDone.includes(pncType)) childPNCsDone.push(pncType);
            if(pncsCancelled.includes(pncType)) childPNCsCancelled.push(pncType);
        }
        
        const allUpcomingVisitsAreCancelled = pncEncounters.some((enc) => enc.getObservationValue('e963732c-cba1-4528-9e69-9123e34651e1') == '346eca56-4a9f-43f7-9f5e-0616cd6b22aa');

        if(allUpcomingVisitsAreCancelled){
            childPNCsCancelled = allPNCs.filter(pnc => !childPNCsDone.includes(pnc));
        }
        else{

            let dateOfBirth = moment(programEnrolment.individual.dateOfBirth).startOf('day');
            const daysBetween = moment().diff(dateOfBirth, 'days');
            
            const duePNC = hasDue(schedule, dateOfBirth);
            if (duePNC) {
              if (!childPNCsDone.includes(duePNC) && !childPNCsCancelled.includes(duePNC)) {
                toBeDone = [duePNC];
              }
              
              let dueIndex = allPNCs.indexOf(duePNC);
              let previousPNCs = allPNCs.slice(0, dueIndex);
            }
            let remainingPNCs = allPNCs.filter(pnc => !childPNCsDone.includes(pnc) && !childPNCsCancelled.includes(pnc) && !toBeDone.includes(pnc));
            childPNCsOverDue = remainingPNCs.filter(pncType => hasOverDue(schedule, daysBetween, pncType))
        }

        if(childPNCsDone.length > 0) {
            summaries.push({name: "Child PNCs Done", value: childPNCsDone.join(', ')});
        }
        if(childPNCsCancelled.length > 0) {
            summaries.push({name: "Child PNCs Cancelled", value: childPNCsCancelled.join(', ')});
        }
        if(toBeDone.length > 0 && daysSinceBirth <= 50) {
            summaries.push({ name: "Child PNCs to be done", value: toBeDone });
        }
        if(childPNCsOverDue.length > 0 && daysSinceBirth <= 50) {
            summaries.push({name: "Overdue Child PNC(s)", value: childPNCsOverDue.join(', ')});``
        }
    }
    
    return summaries;
};
```

```javascript
    const motherName = programEnrolment.individual.getObservationValue('314aa736-2663-4303-b309-6ee7f7cc5801');
    const selectedMotherName = programEnrolment.individual.getObservationReadableValue('670f4d67-851d-4c6e-acd4-7c537715c908');

    } else if(selectedMotherName){
      summaries.push({name: "Mother's Name", value: selectedMotherName});
    }
    
    const childEnrollments = programEnrolment.individual.nonVoidedEnrolments().filter((enrl) => enrl.program.name == "Child" && enrl.voided == false && enrl.enrolmentDateTime);
    
    if(childEnrollments.length > 0){
      const childEnrollment = childEnrollments[0];
      
      let highRisk = childEnrollment.getObservationValue('c3aa61b5-7010-4790-bc57-fea57b5d1129');
      console.log('highRisk',highRisk);
      if(highRisk && highRisk.length) {
        summaries.push({name: 'High risk condition for child', value: [...highRisk]});
      }
      
      let birhFormEnc = childEnrollment.getEncountersOfType('Birth form');
      
      if(birhFormEnc.length > 0) {
        let highRiskFromMother = birhFormEnc[0].getObservationValue('c665033d-b172-4c1d-be72-c4161afea912');
        if(highRiskFromMother && highRiskFromMother.length) {
          summaries.push({name: "Child at High Risk due to mother's high risk condition", value: [...highRiskFromMother]});
        }
      }
      
      let allPNC = childEnrollment.getEncountersOfType('Child PNC');
      let lastPNC = allPNC[0];
      if(lastPNC) {
        let pncHighRisk = lastPNC.getObservationValue('abea4537-be00-4135-b664-4922939db5cd');
        console.log('pncHighRisk',pncHighRisk)
        if(pncHighRisk && pncHighRisk.length) {
          summaries.push({name: 'High Risk Conditions Child PNC', value:[...pncHighRisk]});
        }
      }
      
      if(birhFormEnc.length > 0) {
        let birthWeight = birhFormEnc[0].getObservationReadableValue('148fb61a-9d85-4876-b20f-50235c0d1d6e');
        let birthHeight = birhFormEnc[0].getObservationReadableValue('f9319bd3-de50-48df-873b-bdce97793855');
        if(birthWeight) {
          summaries.push({name: "Birth Weight of Child", value: birthWeight});
        }
      }
      
      let monthGradationFormEnc = childEnrollment.getEncountersOfType('Child Monthly Gradation Form');
      
      if(monthGradationFormEnc.length > 0) {
        let gradationWeight = monthGradationFormEnc[0].getObservationValue('148fb61a-9d85-4876-b20f-50235c0d1d6e');
        let gradationHeight = monthGradationFormEnc[0].getObservationValue('f9319bd3-de50-48df-873b-bdce97793855');
      }
    }
    
    let homeoTreatmentEnc = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('Homeo Treatment Form: For Malnourished Children');
    if(homeoTreatmentEnc.length > 0) {
    
      let currentWeight = homeoTreatmentEnc[0].getObservationReadableValue('148fb61a-9d85-4876-b20f-50235c0d1d6e');
      
      if(currentWeight) {
        summaries.push({name: "Weight", value: currentWeight});
      } else if (gradationWeight){
        summaries.push({name: "Weight", value: birthWeight});
      }
      
      let weightExpected = homeoTreatmentEnc[0].getObservationReadableValue('d7892158-9c00-4006-ac9f-cef6af262488');
      if(weightExpected) {
        summaries.push({name: "Expected weight for that age", value: weightExpected});
      }

      let weightPercentile = homeoTreatmentEnc[0].getObservationReadableValue('8466a400-88d7-48f8-927d-137a5b5be494');
      if(weightPercentile) {
        summaries.push({name: "Weight percentile", value: weightPercentile});
      }
      
      let currentHeight = homeoTreatmentEnc[0].getObservationReadableValue('f9319bd3-de50-48df-873b-bdce97793855');
      if(currentHeight) {
        summaries.push({name: "Height", value: currentHeight});
      } else if (gradationHeight){
        summaries.push({name: "Height", value: birthHeight});
      }
      
      let heightExpected = homeoTreatmentEnc[0].getObservationReadableValue('98733409-3192-44cc-b00b-a097fb843434');
      if(heightExpected) {
        summaries.push({name: "Expected Height for that age", value: heightExpected});
      }
      
      let heightPercentile = homeoTreatmentEnc[0].getObservationReadableValue('1094b385-2f28-4ef4-bf07-417cef5c6dcc');
      if(heightPercentile) {
        summaries.push({name: "Height percentile", value: heightPercentile});
      }  
          
      let armCircumference = homeoTreatmentEnc[0].getObservationReadableValue('506884c5-b725-4c35-9507-3be4da7826c5');
      if(armCircumference) {
        summaries.push({name: "Mid-Upper arm Circumference", value: armCircumference});
      }
      
      let headCircumference = homeoTreatmentEnc[0].getObservationReadableValue('50e119a7-73e1-4111-ba4d-03d8b136125b');
      if(headCircumference) {
        summaries.push({name: "Head Circumference", value: headCircumference});
      }
      
      let chestCircumference = homeoTreatmentEnc[0].getObservationReadableValue('111c7184-543a-4c8a-8e7c-4fae01f10706');
      if(chestCircumference) {
        summaries.push({name: "Chest Circumference", value: chestCircumference});
      }
      
      let gradeOfMalnutrition = homeoTreatmentEnc[0].getObservationValue('0e8a4b19-9e99-4fbf-8b70-b7cc2f12855d');
      if(gradeOfMalnutrition) {
        summaries.push({name: "Grade of Malnutrition", value: gradeOfMalnutrition});
      }
    }
    return summaries;
};
```

### `atul_uat` · `program[Child]`  (3001 bytes)

```javascript
//SAMPLE RULE EXAMPLE
'use strict';
({params, imports}) =>  {
    const summaries = [];
    const programEnrolment = params.programEnrolment;

    const dateOfDelivery = programEnrolment.getObservationReadableValue("Date of Birth");
    if(dateOfDelivery){
        summaries.push({name: "Date of Delivery", value: dateOfDelivery});
    }
    
    const birthWeight = programEnrolment.getObservationReadableValue("Birth Weight");
    if(birthWeight){
        summaries.push({name: "Birth Weight", value: birthWeight});
    }

    const typeOfDelivery = programEnrolment.getObservationValue("Type of Delivery");
    if(typeOfDelivery){
        summaries.push({name: "Type of Delivery", value: [typeOfDelivery]});
    }

    const doesTheChildHaveAnyBirthDefect = programEnrolment.getObservationValue("Does the child have any birth defect");
    if(doesTheChildHaveAnyBirthDefect && doesTheChildHaveAnyBirthDefect.length > 0){
        summaries.push({name: "Does the child have any birth defect", value: doesTheChildHaveAnyBirthDefect});
    }   

    const doesTheChildReferForAnyConditionAtTheTimeOfBirth = programEnrolment.getObservationValue("Does the child refer for any condition at the time of birth");
    if(doesTheChildReferForAnyConditionAtTheTimeOfBirth){
        summaries.push({name: "Does the child refer for any condition at the time of birth", value: [doesTheChildReferForAnyConditionAtTheTimeOfBirth]});
    }   

    const currentHeightOfTheChild = programEnrolment.findLatestObservationInEntireEnrolment("Height");
    if(currentHeightOfTheChild){
        summaries.push({name: "Current Height of a child", value: currentHeightOfTheChild.getValue()});
    }

    const currentWeightOfTheChild = programEnrolment.findLatestObservationInEntireEnrolment("Weight");
    if(currentWeightOfTheChild){
        summaries.push({name: "Current Weight of a child", value: currentWeightOfTheChild.getValue()});
    }

    const growthChartWeightForLengthHeightZScore = programEnrolment.findLatestObservationInEntireEnrolment("Growth Chart - Weight for length/height (Z score)");
    if(growthChartWeightForLengthHeightZScore){
        summaries.push({name: "Growth Chart - Weight for length/height (Z score)", value: growthChartWeightForLengthHeightZScore.getValue()});
    }

    const doesTheChildShowedAppropriateWeightGain = programEnrolment.findLatestObservationInEntireEnrolment("Does the child showed appropriate weight gain");
    if(doesTheChildShowedAppropriateWeightGain){
        summaries.push({name: "Does the child showed appropriate weight gain", value: [doesTheChildShowedAppropriateWeightGain.getValue()]});
    }

    const doesTheChildStillReceivingBreastmilk = programEnrolment.findLatestObservationInEntireEnrolment("Does the child still receiving breastmilk");
    if(doesTheChildStillReceivingBreastmilk){
        summaries.push({name: "Does the child still receiving breastmilk", value: [doesTheChildStillReceivingBreastmilk.getValue()]});
    }

    return summaries;
};
```

### `JNPCT` · `program[NCD Program]`  (2799 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) =>  {
    const summaries = [];
    
    const programEnrolment = params.programEnrolment;


    const ncdFollowups = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('NCD Followup', true);
    if (!ncdFollowups || ncdFollowups.length == 0) return summaries;
    const latestNcdFollowup = ncdFollowups[0];

    const getObs = conceptName => latestNcdFollowup.getObservationValue(conceptName);

    const diabetesMedicineGiven = getObs('Have You Take Any Medicine for Sugar?');
    if (diabetesMedicineGiven) {
        summaries.push({name: 'Have You Take Any Medicine for Sugar?', value: [diabetesMedicineGiven]});
    }
    const diabetesMedicine = getObs('Which Medicine for Sugar?');
    if (diabetesMedicine) {
        summaries.push({name: 'Which Medicine for Sugar?', value: diabetesMedicine});
    }
    const diabetesMedicineRegular = getObs('Are You Taking Sugar Medicine Regularly ?');
    if (diabetesMedicineRegular) {
        summaries.push({name: 'Are You Taking Sugar Medicine Regularly ?', value: [diabetesMedicineRegular]});
    }
    const diabetesSymptoms = getObs('Which Type of Symptoms for DM?');
    if (diabetesSymptoms) {
        summaries.push({name: 'Which Type of Symptoms for DM?', value: diabetesSymptoms});
    }
    const diabetesRBS = getObs("Today's Random Blood Sugar");
    if (diabetesRBS) {
        summaries.push({name: "Today's Random Blood Sugar", value: [diabetesRBS]});
    }
  
  //HYPERTENSION SUMMARIES
  
    
     const BPMedicineGiven = getObs('Have You Take Any Medicine for BP?');
    if (BPMedicineGiven) {
        summaries.push({name: 'Have You Take Any Medicine for BP?', value: [BPMedicineGiven]});
    }
    
    const BPMedicineList = getObs('Which Medicine for BP?');
    if (BPMedicineList) {
        summaries.push({name: 'Which Medicine for BP?', value: BPMedicineList});
    }
    
    const BPSymptoms = getObs('Which Type of Symptoms for BP?');
    if (BPSymptoms) {
        summaries.push({name: 'Which Type of Symptoms for BP?', value: BPSymptoms});
    }
    
    const HyperTension = getObs('Hypertension Grade');
    if (HyperTension) {
        summaries.push({name: 'Hypertension Grade', value: HyperTension});
    }
    
    
  // Referral Details
  
   const referralrequired = getObs('Referral Required');
    if (referralrequired) {
        summaries.push({name: 'Referral Required', value: [referralrequired]});
    }
    
    const dateOfReferral = getObs('Date of Referral');
    if (dateOfReferral) {
        summaries.push({name: 'Date of Referral', value: dateOfReferral});
    }
    
    const patientRefer = getObs('Which Hospital Patient Refer?');
    if (patientRefer) {
        summaries.push({name: 'Which Hospital Patient Refer?', value: [patientRefer]});
    }
  
    return summaries;
};
```

### `Ashwini uat` · `program[Mental Health]`  (2712 bytes)

```javascript
//SAMPLE RULE EXAMPLE
'use strict';
({params, imports}) =>  {
    const summaries = [];
    const programEnrolment = params.programEnrolment;
    //value can be fetched from entire enrolment using programEnrolment.findObservationInEntireEnrolment(CONCEPT_NAME);
    //value can be fetched from entire enrolment using 
    const Diagnosis= programEnrolment.findObservationInEntireEnrolment("Diagnosis_MH");
    if (Diagnosis) {
     summaries.push({name: 'Diagnosis_MH', value: Diagnosis.getValue()});
    }    
        const Medicines1 = programEnrolment.findObservationInEntireEnrolment("Medicines are being given");
    if (Medicines1) {
     summaries.push({name: 'Medicines are being given', value: Medicines1.getValue()});
    }  
    const Medicines = programEnrolment.findLatestObservationInEntireEnrolment("Medicines which are administered now");
    if (Medicines) {
     summaries.push({name: 'Medicines which are administered now', value: Medicines.getValue()});
    }  
    const color = programEnrolment.findObservationInEntireEnrolment("Patient colour coding");
    if (color) {
     summaries.push({name: 'Patient colour coding - Enrolment', value: color.getValue()});
    }
    
  //
    const encounters = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('Mental Health followup');
   // console.log('encounters---->',encounters);

    let enc1 = _.chain(encounters)
    	.filter((enc) => enc.encounterDateTime)
    	.orderBy((enc) => enc.encounterDateTime, 'desc')
    	.nth(0)
    	.value();
    	
    
    let enc2 = _.chain(encounters)
    	.filter((enc) => enc.encounterDateTime)
    	.orderBy((enc) => enc.encounterDateTime, 'desc')
    	.filter((enc) => !_.isNil(enc.encounterDateTime))
    	.nth(1)
    	.value();
    	
    let enc3 = _.chain(encounters)
    	.filter((enc) => enc.encounterDateTime)
    	.orderBy((enc) => enc.encounterDateTime, 'desc')
    	.filter((enc) => !_.isNil(enc.encounterDateTime))
    	.nth(2)
    	.value();
    
    let enc1Value = '',enc2Value = '',enc3Value = '';
    if (enc1) {    	
    	enc1Value = enc1.getObservationReadableValue('Patient colour coding');
    }
    if (!_.isEmpty(enc1Value)) {
      summaries.push({name: 'Color code for last first visit', value: enc1Value});
    }    
    if (enc2) {
    		enc2Value = enc2.getObservationReadableValue('Patient colour coding');
    }
     if (!_.isEmpty(enc2Value)) {
      summaries.push({name: 'Color code for last second visit', value: enc2Value});
    }
    if (enc3) {
    		enc3Value = enc3.getObservationReadableValue('Patient colour coding');
    }
     if (!_.isEmpty(enc3Value)) {
      summaries.push({name: 'Color code for last third visit', value: enc3Value});
    }
    
    return summaries;
};
```

```javascript
//SAMPLE RULE EXAMPLE
'use strict';
({params, imports}) =>  {
    const summaries = [];
    const programEnrolment = params.programEnrolment;

    const encounterUUID = programEnrolment.getObservationValue("0d1c7661-4d1a-4229-87c1-d45fc170dede");

    if(encounterUUID) {
        const outReachEncounter = programEnrolment.individual.getEncounters().filter((enc) => enc.uuid == encounterUUID)[0];
        const whereDidOutReachHappen = outReachEncounter.getObservationValue('f619854c-9ed3-44ec-944e-ad9e51a768e0');
      
        if (whereDidOutReachHappen) {
            summaries.push({name:"Where did the Outreach Activity happen - village or other space?", value: [whereDidOutReachHappen]});
        }

        const whereInVillageDidYouGo = outReachEncounter.getObservationValue('0f22d77f-3b4d-44f6-a587-bc90d3855b98');
        const whereInOtherSpaceDidYouGo = outReachEncounter.getObservationValue('6188571c-711c-4466-be71-18362ce555de');

        if(whereInVillageDidYouGo){
            summaries.push({name:"Where did you go?", value: [whereInVillageDidYouGo]});
        }
        else if(whereInOtherSpaceDidYouGo){
            summaries.push({name:"Where did you go?", value: [whereInOtherSpaceDidYouGo]});
        }


        const whatInVillageDidYouDo = outReachEncounter.getObservationValue('2b465290-f555-4d3b-b418-b0b9a483d963');
        const whatInOtherSpaceDidYouDo = outReachEncounter.getObservationValue('5ccfa48e-8d07-44c3-9f58-9f52c7327ba0');

        if(whatInVillageDidYouDo){
            summaries.push({name:"What did you do?", value: whatInVillageDidYouDo});
        }
        else if(whatInOtherSpaceDidYouDo){
            summaries.push({name:"What did you do?", value: whatInOtherSpaceDidYouDo});
        }
    }
    
    const clientName = programEnrolment.getObservationValue('2dd148df-6fd0-43ba-a777-ceaf5dd9fca9');

    if(clientName){
        summaries.push({name:"Name of client", value: clientName});
    }

    const issueIdentified = programEnrolment.getObservationValue('08e8417e-85ab-4e92-a729-e04f10c48f9c');
    if(issueIdentified){
        summaries.push({name:"Issue identified", value: issueIdentified});
    }
    
    const clientFollowUpStatus = programEnrolment.findLatestObservationInEntireEnrolment("e26a61db-d7bf-4fbe-b5e2-ed60294e8f62");
    if(clientFollowUpStatus){
        summaries.push({name:"Client Follow up Status", value: [clientFollowUpStatus.getValue()]});
    }
    
    return summaries;
};
```

```javascript
        district = listOfLocations[1].trim();

    else

    let partnersToBeShown = partners[district];

    if(!_.isArray(partnersToBeShown)) {
        partnersToBeShown = _.castArray(partnersToBeShown);
    }

    const statusBuilder = new imports.rulesConfig.FormElementStatusBuilder({encounter, formElement});
    statusBuilder.show();
    
    partnersToBeShown.push("Goonj");
    const answersToSkip = _.difference(allPartnerOrgs, partnersToBeShown);
    console.log("answersToSkip:::", answersToSkip);
    statusBuilder.skipAnswers(...answersToSkip);
    return statusBuilder.build();
};
```

```javascript
        .when.valueInEncounter(anyDiseaseConceptUUID)
        .containsAnswerConceptName(diseases["Infertility"])
        .matches();

    let advicesToShow = [];
    
    if (hasHypertension) {
        advicesToShow = _.union(advicesToShow, diseaseAdviceMap[diseases["Hypertension"]]);
    }
    
    if (hasDiabetes) {
        advicesToShow = _.union(advicesToShow, diseaseAdviceMap[diseases["Diabetes"]]);
    }
    
    if (hasHeartDisease) {
        advicesToShow = _.union(advicesToShow, diseaseAdviceMap[diseases["Heart Disease"]]);
    }
    
    if (hasSickleCell) {

    if (hasMentalHealth) {
        advicesToShow = _.union(advicesToShow, diseaseAdviceMap[diseases["Mental Health"]]);
    }
    
    if (hasCOPD) {
        advicesToShow = _.union(advicesToShow, diseaseAdviceMap[diseases["COPD"]]);
    }
    
    if (hasSeizureEpilepsy) {
        advicesToShow = _.union(advicesToShow, diseaseAdviceMap[diseases["Seizure/Epilepsy"]]);
    }
    
    if (hasStroke) {
        advicesToShow = _.union(advicesToShow, diseaseAdviceMap[diseases["Stroke"]]);
    }
    
    if (hasHypertensionSuspect) {
        advicesToShow = _.union(advicesToShow, diseaseAdviceMap[diseases["Hypertension Suspect"]]);
    }
    
    if (hasDiabetesSuspect) {
        advicesToShow = _.union(advicesToShow, diseaseAdviceMap[diseases["Diabetes Suspect"]]);
    }
    
    if (hasBedriddenPatient) {
        advicesToShow = _.union(advicesToShow, diseaseAdviceMap[diseases["Bedridden patient"]]);
    }
    
    if (hasNeedOfOperation) {
        advicesToShow = _.union(advicesToShow, diseaseAdviceMap[diseases["Need of Operation"]]);
    }
    
    if (hasIntellectuallyDisabled) {
        advicesToShow = _.union(advicesToShow, diseaseAdviceMap[diseases["Intellectually disabled"]]);
    }
    
    if (hasHandicap) {
        advicesToShow = _.union(advicesToShow, diseaseAdviceMap[diseases["Handicap"]]);
    }
    
    if (hasInfertility) {
        advicesToShow = _.union(advicesToShow, diseaseAdviceMap[diseases["Infertility"]]);
    }

    const alwaysShowAdvices = [
        advices["Other"]
    ];

    const allAdviceUUIDs = Object.values(advices);

    if (advicesToShow.length === 0) {
        advicesToShow = allAdviceUUIDs;
    } else {
        advicesToShow = _.union(advicesToShow, alwaysShowAdvices);
    }

    const adviceUUIDsToSkip = allAdviceUUIDs.filter(uuid => !advicesToShow.includes(uuid));

    _.forEach(adviceUUIDsToSkip, (adviceUUID) => {
        const answerToSkip = formElement.getAnswerWithConceptUuid(adviceUUID);
        if (answerToSkip) {
            answersToSkip.push(answerToSkip);
        }
    });

    return new imports.rulesConfig.FormElementStatus(formElement.uuid, visibility, value, answersToSkip, validationErrors);
};
```

### `Animedh Charitable Trust DNH` · `form[Goal setting Encounter].fe[Status]`  (4483 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
    const programEncounter = params.entity;
    const moment = imports.moment;
    const formElement = params.formElement;
    const _ = imports.lodash;
    let visibility = true;
    let value = null;
    let answersToSkip = [];
    let validationErrors = [];
    let questionGroupIndex = params.questionGroupIndex;
    const encounterType = programEncounter.encounterType.name;
    let currentTypeOfGoal = null;
    let currentDescription = null;
    let currentStatus = null;
    const programEnrolment = programEncounter.programEnrolment;

    const currentEncounterQuestionGroup = programEncounter.getObservationValue('7d06a63c-4f78-4519-acec-9b8bc1a85fce') || [];

    if(currentEncounterQuestionGroup && currentEncounterQuestionGroup.length > questionGroupIndex) {
        const currentGroup = currentEncounterQuestionGroup[questionGroupIndex];
        currentTypeOfGoal = currentGroup.findObservationByConceptUUID('7060522e-e88b-470a-aaa6-ceba986e4169');
        currentDescription = currentGroup.findObservationByConceptUUID('29700074-36f5-4245-8aba-3c57ffa839f8');
        currentStatus = currentGroup.findObservationByConceptUUID('cd5304c5-870f-420b-8929-1fc8b3dd9be3');
        if(currentTypeOfGoal && currentStatus && currentDescription) {
            currentTypeOfGoal = currentTypeOfGoal.getValue();
            currentDescription = currentDescription.getValue();
            currentStatus = currentStatus.getValue();
        }
    }
    
    let allOptions = {
        '333874ea-c391-41c2-aff1-b249c44cf235': {name: 'New Goal', level: 1},
        '9f22d112-45dc-4045-a8d0-c54c8e4304d4': {name: 'Ongoing', level: 2},
        '2a43890e-a1e8-4289-bb5e-80a239a14d1e': {name: 'Achieved', level: 3}
    }

    function levelSIsSameOrGreater(level1, level2) {

        const level1Value = allOptions[level1];
        const level2Value = allOptions[level2];
        if(level1Value && level2Value) {
            if(level2Value.level >= level1Value.level) {
                return {
                    value: true,
                    message: 'Level is same or greater'
                };
            }
            else if(level1Value.name == 'Ongoing'){
                return {
                    value: false,
                    message: 'This goal is already ongoing; are you sure you want to edit it?'
                };
            }
            else if(level1Value.name == 'Achieved') {
                return {
                    value: false,
                    message: 'This goal is already achieved; are you sure you want to edit it?'
                };
            }
        }

        return {
            value: true,
            message: 'Don\'t know'
        };
    }

    const encounters = programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType(encounterType) || [];
    const previousEncounters = encounters.filter(enc => enc.uuid != programEncounter.uuid);
    
    if(previousEncounters.length > 0 && currentEncounterQuestionGroup.length > 0 && currentTypeOfGoal && currentStatus && currentDescription) {
        const latestPreviousEncoutner = previousEncounters[0];
        const questionGroup = latestPreviousEncoutner.getObservationValue('7d06a63c-4f78-4519-acec-9b8bc1a85fce') || [];

        for (let i = 0; i < questionGroup.length; i++) {
            const currentGroup = questionGroup[i];
            let previousTypeOfgoal = currentGroup.findObservationByConceptUUID('7060522e-e88b-470a-aaa6-ceba986e4169');
            let previousStatus = currentGroup.findObservationByConceptUUID('cd5304c5-870f-420b-8929-1fc8b3dd9be3');
            let previousDescription = currentGroup.findObservationByConceptUUID('29700074-36f5-4245-8aba-3c57ffa839f8');
            if (previousTypeOfgoal && previousStatus && previousDescription) {
                previousTypeOfgoal = previousTypeOfgoal.getValue();
                previousStatus = previousStatus.getValue();
                previousDescription = previousDescription.getValue();
                if(previousTypeOfgoal == currentTypeOfGoal && previousDescription == currentDescription) {
                    const result = levelSIsSameOrGreater(previousStatus, currentStatus);
                    if(!result.value) {
                        validationErrors.push(result.message);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    return new imports.rulesConfig.FormElementStatus(formElement.uuid, visibility, value, answersToSkip, validationErrors);
};
```
