```javascript
  let validationErrors = [];

  const RuleCondition = imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition;

  /* ---------------- SAFE HELPERS ---------------- */

  function getParentLocation(location) {
    if (
      location &&
      location.locationMappings &&
      location.locationMappings.length > 0 &&
      location.locationMappings[0] &&
      location.locationMappings[0].parent
    ) {
      return location.locationMappings[0].parent;
    }
    return null;
  }

  /* ---------------- CHECK ADDRESS ---------------- */

  const hasIndividualAddress = new RuleCondition({
    individual: individual,
    formElement: formElement
  }).when.lowestAddressLevel.defined.matches();

  if (!hasIndividualAddress) {
    return new imports.rulesConfig.FormElementStatus(
      formElement.uuid,
      visibility,
      value,
      answersToSkip,
      validationErrors
    );
  }

  /* ---------------- INDIVIDUAL DATA ---------------- */

  const individualLowestAddress = individual.lowestAddressLevel.uuid;
  const individualLowestAddressName = individual.lowestAddressLevel.name;
  const individualLowestAddressType = individual.lowestAddressLevel.typeUuid;

  const individualParent = getParentLocation(individual.lowestAddressLevel);
  const individualSecondLowestAddress =
    individualParent ? individualParent.uuid : null;

  /* ---------------- CONSTANTS ---------------- */

  const FALIYA_UUID = '4c23ae7d-6f0c-4924-93f2-7296bfe96fef';
  const SCHOOL_UUID = '79f9fc96-7817-491c-a202-9e98ead1574c';

  /* ---------------- GROUP CONTEXT (SAFE) ---------------- */

  const groupSubject =
    params.entityContext && params.entityContext.group
      ? params.entityContext.group
      : null;

  /* -------- CASE 1: GROUP CONTEXT -------- */

  if (groupSubject && groupSubject.lowestAddressLevel) {
    const householdLowestAddress = groupSubject.lowestAddressLevel.uuid;

    const householdParent = getParentLocation(groupSubject.lowestAddressLevel);
    const householdSecondLowestAddress =
      householdParent ? householdParent.uuid : null;

    if (
      individualLowestAddressType === FALIYA_UUID &&
      householdLowestAddress &&
      individualLowestAddress !== householdLowestAddress
    ) {
      validationErrors.push(
        'Address selected for individual is not same as Household address.'
      );
    }

    if (
      individualLowestAddressType === SCHOOL_UUID &&
      householdSecondLowestAddress &&
      individualSecondLowestAddress &&
      individualSecondLowestAddress !== householdSecondLowestAddress
    ) {
      validationErrors.push(
        'Selected village for individual is not same as Household village.'
      );
    }
  }

  /* -------- CASE 2: FALLBACK VIA INDIVIDUAL GROUPS -------- */

  else if (
    individual.groups &&
    individual.groups.length > 0 &&
    individual.groups[0].memberSubject &&
    individual.groups[0].memberSubject.lowestAddressLevel
  ) {
    const householdSubject = individual.groups[0].memberSubject;

    const householdLowestAddress =
      householdSubject.lowestAddressLevel.uuid;

    const householdParent = getParentLocation(
      householdSubject.lowestAddressLevel
    );
    const householdSecondLowestAddress =
      householdParent ? householdParent.uuid : null;

    if (
      individualLowestAddressType === FALIYA_UUID &&
      householdLowestAddress &&
      individualLowestAddress !== householdLowestAddress
    ) {
      validationErrors.push(
        'Address selected for individual is not same as Household address.'
      );
    }

    if (
      individualLowestAddressType === SCHOOL_UUID &&
      householdSecondLowestAddress &&
      individualSecondLowestAddress &&
      individualSecondLowestAddress !== householdSecondLowestAddress
    ) {
      validationErrors.push(
        'Selected village for individual is not same as Household village.'
      );
    }
  }

  /* ---------------- DISPLAY ---------------- */

  value = individualLowestAddressName;

  return new imports.rulesConfig.FormElementStatus(
    formElement.uuid,
    visibility,
    value,
    answersToSkip,
    validationErrors
  );
};
```

### `APF Odisha` · `form[Growth Monitoring].fe[What is the treatment advise for the SAM/MAM/GF2 child?]`  (3881 bytes)

```javascript
//SAMPLE RULE EXAMPLE
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
    const programEncounter = params.entity;
    const moment = imports.moment;
    const formElement = params.formElement;
    const _ = imports.lodash;
    let visibility = true;
    let value = null;
    let answersToSkip = [];
    let validationErrors = [];
  
    const height = programEncounter.getObservationValue("Height");
    const weight = programEncounter.getObservationValue("Weight");
    const asOnDate = programEncounter.encounterDateTime;
    const individual = programEncounter.programEnrolment.individual;
    const age = programEncounter.programEnrolment.individual.getAgeInMonths();

    const addIfRequired = (decisions, name, value) => {
        if (value === -0) value = 0;
        if (value !== undefined) decisions.push({name: name, value: value});
    };

    const getGradeforZscore = (zScore) => {
        let grade;
        if (zScore <= -3) {
            grade = 3;
        } else if (zScore > -3 && zScore < -2) {
            grade = 2;
        } else if (zScore >= -2) {
            grade = 1;
        }
        return grade;
    };
    const zScoresForChild = ruleServiceLibraryInterfaceForSharingModules.common.getZScore(individual, asOnDate, weight, height);

    const zScoreGradeStatusMappingWeightForHeight = [
        ["SAM", -3],
        ["MAM", -2],
        ["Normal", 1],
        ["Normal", 2],
        ["Normal", 3],
        ["Normal", Infinity],
    ];

    const weightForHeightStatus = function (zScore) {
        let found = _.find(zScoreGradeStatusMappingWeightForHeight, function (currentStatus) {
            return zScore <= currentStatus[1];
        });
        return found && found[0];
    };

    const wfhStatus = weightForHeightStatus(zScoresForChild.wfh);
    const wfhGrade = getGradeforZscore(zScoresForChild.wfh);
    
    let nutritionalStatus = wfhStatus;
    let growthFalteringStatus = '';

    const encounters = programEncounter.programEnrolment.getEncountersOfType('Growth Monitoring');
    let enc1 = _.chain(encounters)
        .filter((enc) => !_.isNil(enc.encounterDateTime) && !_.isNil(enc.earliestVisitDateTime) && _.isNil(enc.cancelDateTime))
        .filter((enc) => enc.uuid !== programEncounter.uuid)
        .filter((enc) => enc.encounterDateTime < programEncounter.encounterDateTime)
        .sortBy((enc) => -imports.moment(enc.earliestVisitDateTime)) 
        .nth(0)
        .value();

    let enc2 = _.chain(encounters)
        .filter((enc) => !_.isNil(enc.encounterDateTime) && !_.isNil(enc.earliestVisitDateTime) && _.isNil(enc.cancelDateTime))
        .filter((enc) => enc.uuid !== programEncounter.uuid)
        .filter((enc) => enc.encounterDateTime < programEncounter.encounterDateTime)
        .sortBy((enc) => -imports.moment(enc.earliestVisitDateTime))
        .nth(1)
        .value();

    let enc1Weight = null, enc2Weight = null;
    let currentWeight = programEncounter.getObservationReadableValue('Weight');
    if (enc1) {
        enc1Weight = enc1.getObservationReadableValue('Weight');
        
    }
    if (enc2) {
        enc2Weight = enc2.getObservationReadableValue('Weight');
        
    }
     
    if(enc1Weight && enc2Weight && (currentWeight <= enc1Weight) && (enc1Weight <= enc2Weight)){
        growthFalteringStatus = "GF2"; // If current weight is less than the second last 2 weights, GF status is GF2 
    } else if(enc1Weight && (currentWeight <= enc1Weight)){
        growthFalteringStatus = "GF1"; // If current weight is less than the last weight, GF status is GF1
    } else {
        growthFalteringStatus = "None";
    }

    visibility = (nutritionalStatus && ['SAM', 'MAM'].includes(nutritionalStatus)) || (growthFalteringStatus && growthFalteringStatus == 'GF2');

    return new imports.rulesConfig.FormElementStatus(formElement.uuid, visibility, value, answersToSkip, validationErrors);
};
```

### `Gubbachi` · `form[Daily Activity Update & Reflections Encounter].fe[Activity Id]`  (3499 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
  const programEncounter = params.entity;
  const formElement = params.formElement;
  const statusBuilder = new imports.rulesConfig.FormElementStatusBuilder({programEncounter, formElement});
  const individualService = params.services.individualService;
  const _ = imports.lodash;
  
  const classSubject = programEncounter.programEnrolment.individual;
  const classGrade = classSubject.getObservationValue('c2b3a4d5-e6f7-8901-bcde-f12345678904');
  
  const selectedSubject = programEncounter.getObservationValue('e4d5c6b7-a8f9-0123-def0-123456789007');
  
  if(!classGrade) {
    statusBuilder.show().whenItem(false).is.truthy;
    return statusBuilder.build();
  }
  
  const completedActivityUuids = [];
  const allEncounters = programEncounter.programEnrolment.encounters;
  
  const previousEncounters = allEncounters.filter(enc => 
    enc.encounterType.name === 'Daily Activity Update & Reflection' && 
    enc.encounterDateTime < programEncounter.encounterDateTime &&
    !enc.voided &&
    enc.uuid !== programEncounter.uuid
  );
  
  previousEncounters.forEach(enc => {
    const activityCompletionDetails = enc.findGroupedObservation('9df11836-e83b-4dae-b0e0-36b9b568fc80');
    
    if(activityCompletionDetails && activityCompletionDetails.length > 0) {
      activityCompletionDetails.forEach((group, idx) => {
        const activityObs = group.groupObservations.find(obs => obs.concept.uuid === '058464dc-1711-480e-afb3-6a72e362e64a');
        const statusObs = group.groupObservations.find(obs => obs.concept.uuid === 'e0d1c2b3-a4f5-6789-3456-789012345005');
        
        if(activityObs && statusObs && statusObs.valueJSON && statusObs.valueJSON.answer === 'e0d1c2b3-a4f5-6789-3456-789012345002') {
          const activityUuid = activityObs.valueJSON && activityObs.valueJSON.answer;
          
          if(activityUuid) {
            if(Array.isArray(activityUuid)) {
              completedActivityUuids.push(...activityUuid);
            } else {
              completedActivityUuids.push(activityUuid);
            }
          }
        }
      });
    }
  });
  
  const activities = individualService.getSubjects('Activity');
  
  const filteredActivities = activities.filter(activity => {
    const activityStatus = activity.getObservationValue('d9c0b1a2-f3e4-5678-2345-678901234004');
    const activityGrades = activity.getObservationValue('c2b3a4d5-e6f7-8901-bcde-f12345678904');
    const activitySubject = activity.getObservationValue('d9c0b1a2-f3e4-5678-2345-678901234005');
    
    const isActive = activityStatus && activityStatus === '4d058de2-1b1b-4a09-9e60-592246a6130a';
    const notCompleted = !completedActivityUuids.includes(activity.uuid);
    
    let gradeMatches = false;
    if(activityGrades) {
      if(Array.isArray(activityGrades)) {
        gradeMatches = activityGrades.includes(classGrade);
      } else {
        gradeMatches = activityGrades === classGrade;
      }
    }
    
    let subjectMatches = true;
    if(selectedSubject && selectedSubject.length > 0) {
      subjectMatches = activitySubject && selectedSubject.includes(activitySubject);
    }
    
    return isActive && gradeMatches && subjectMatches && notCompleted;
  });
  
  const activityUuids = _.map(filteredActivities, ({uuid}) => uuid);
  
  if(activityUuids.length > 0) {
    statusBuilder.showAnswers(...activityUuids);
  } else {
    statusBuilder.show().whenItem(false).is.truthy;
  }
  
  return statusBuilder.build();
};
```

### `JNPCT` · `form[NCD Followup Encounter].fe[Scheduled Followup For]`  (2996 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
  const programEncounter = params.entity;
  const formElement = params.formElement;
  let visibility = true;
  let value = null;
  let answersToSkip = [];
  let validationErrors = [];
  const HYPERTENSION_CONCEPT = '08a08802-97b2-43d3-8757-16a3b4ecf3c8';
  const BLOOD_SUGAR_CONCEPT = '509542f2-b76d-4339-af57-add590b8d536';
  const SEVERE_HYPERTENSION_GRADES = ['Grade Two', 'Grade Three', 'Grade Four', 'Grade Five', 'Grade Six'];
let randomBloodSugar;
let hypertensionGrade;
const webapp = programEncounter.programEnrolment.individual.lowestAddressLevel.titleLineage;

if(webapp){
value = 'Both';
  return new imports.rulesConfig.FormElementStatus(formElement.uuid, visibility, value, answersToSkip, validationErrors);
}

// Get initial observations from individual's encounters
const hypertensionObs = programEncounter.programEnrolment.individual.findLatestObservationFromEncounters(HYPERTENSION_CONCEPT);
const sugarObs = programEncounter.programEnrolment.individual.findLatestObservationFromEncounters('5666b393-d450-4865-8127-205559a6b1be');

if (hypertensionObs) {
    hypertensionGrade = hypertensionObs.getReadableValue();
}
if (sugarObs) {
    randomBloodSugar = sugarObs.getReadableValue();
}

// Check for previous followup encounters
const followupEncounters = programEncounter.programEnrolment.hasCompletedEncounterOfType(programEncounter.encounterType.name);

// If there are previous encounters, get observations from them
if (followupEncounters) {
    const prevSugarObs = programEncounter.programEnrolment.findLatestObservationFromPreviousEncounters(BLOOD_SUGAR_CONCEPT, programEncounter);
    const prevHypertensionObs = programEncounter.programEnrolment.findLatestObservationFromPreviousEncounters(HYPERTENSION_CONCEPT, programEncounter);
    
    if (prevSugarObs) randomBloodSugar = prevSugarObs.getReadableValue();
    if (prevHypertensionObs) hypertensionGrade = prevHypertensionObs.getReadableValue();
}

// Set value based on which observations exist
if (hypertensionGrade !== 'Unknown' && !randomBloodSugar) {
    value = 'Hypertension';
} else if (randomBloodSugar && !hypertensionGrade) {
    value = 'Diabetes';
} else if (hypertensionGrade && randomBloodSugar) {
    value = 'Both';
}else if(hypertensionGrade === 'Unknown' && !randomBloodSugar){
value ='Both';
}
  
// Evaluate conditions
const hasHighSugar = !isNaN(randomBloodSugar) && randomBloodSugar >= 150;
const isSevereHypertension = SEVERE_HYPERTENSION_GRADES.includes(hypertensionGrade);

// Determine the value based on conditions
if (hasHighSugar && isSevereHypertension) {
    value = 'Both';
} 
else if (hasHighSugar) {
    value = 'Diabetes';
}
else if (hypertensionGrade === 'Grade One' && (!hasHighSugar || randomBloodSugar < 150)) {
    value = 'Both';
}
else if (hypertensionGrade !== 'Unknown') {
    value = 'Hypertension';
}
  
  return new imports.rulesConfig.FormElementStatus(formElement.uuid, visibility, value, answersToSkip, validationErrors);
};
```

### `Nala Kholikaran 26-27` · `form[Farmer Endline].fe[Updated Category of Farmer]`  (2397 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
    const encounter = params.entity;
    const moment = imports.moment;
    const formElement = params.formElement;
    const _ = imports.lodash;
    let visibility = true;
    let value = null;
    let answersToSkip = [];
    let validationErrors = [];
    
    const wrongInfoOfLandHolding = new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({encounter, formElement}).when.valueInEncounter("180be136-7173-4fd2-9c80-2909af98de4c").containsAnswerConceptName("ad0cc7f5-53fd-4298-8ff2-06d306e242ba").matches();
    
    visibility = wrongInfoOfLandHolding;

    const hasUpdatedFarmerCategotyDefined = new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({encounter, formElement}).when.valueInEncounter("49844fa0-2fe2-4cbb-a605-183916e4034c").defined.matches();
    
    if(hasUpdatedFarmerCategotyDefined) {

        const land_val = encounter.getObservationValue("ce1dbebb-47f4-4c99-b86f-86d474edda64");
        const cat_val = encounter.getObservationValue("49844fa0-2fe2-4cbb-a605-183916e4034c");
        const rangeConditions = [
            { answer: "d788c182-e7de-48ea-a23a-cef038c588c2", range: {min: 0, max: 2.49}, error_message: "For Marginal: Land holding should be in the range of 0 - 2.49" },
            { answer: "003458ff-be84-4937-9c44-636819ec3738", range: {min: 2.5, max: 4.99}, error_message: "For Small: Land holding should be in the range of 2.5 - 4.99" },
            { answer: "34cfaa23-8c27-4ca1-899c-9f1106788c19", range: {min: 5, max: 9.99}, error_message: "For Semi Medium: Land holding should be in the range of 5 - 9.55" },
            { answer: "e67585a0-0a7d-4976-aabe-04e38069149f", range: {min: 10, max: 24.99}, error_message: "For Medium: Land holding should be in the range of 10 - 24.99" },
            { answer: "3f88926b-0bbd-4dc3-aca8-b8ad5ad30ab0", range: {min: 25, max: Infinity}, error_message: "For Large: Land holding should be greater than 25" }
        ];

        const selectedRangeCondition = rangeConditions.find(condition => condition.answer === cat_val);
        if (selectedRangeCondition && land_val && !(land_val >= selectedRangeCondition.range.min && land_val <= selectedRangeCondition.range.max)
        ) {
            validationErrors.push(selectedRangeCondition.error_message);
        }
    }
    
    return new imports.rulesConfig.FormElementStatus(formElement.uuid, visibility, value, answersToSkip, validationErrors);
};
```

### `IPH Sickle Cell` · `form[Confirmation Form].fe[12. HPLC (Hb Variants)]`  (2159 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
  const encounter = params.entity;
  const moment = imports.moment;
  const formElement = params.formElement;
  const _ = imports.lodash;
  let visibility = true;
  let value = null;
  let answersToSkip = [];
  let validationErrors = [];
  const individual = encounter.individual;
  
  const isScreeningDone = individual.getEncounters().filter((enc) => enc.encounterType.name == 'Screening' && enc.encounterDateTime != null && enc.cancelDateTime == null).length != 0;
  
  let prevEncounters = individual.getEncounters(true).filter((enc) => enc.encounterType.name == 'Confirmation' && enc.encounterDateTime != null && enc.uuid != encounter.uuid);
  
  if(prevEncounters.length != 0){
    prevEncounters = prevEncounters[0];
    const sampleFailedInPreviousEncounter = prevEncounters.getObservationReadableValue('18d212b1-0b7e-4e82-822f-39d557c3d018') === 'Sample failed' || prevEncounters.getObservationReadableValue('14cb8d22-49c8-4b9c-ad8a-bbb4bd18e3c9') === 'Sample failed';
    visibility = sampleFailedInPreviousEncounter;
  }
  else if(isScreeningDone){
    const thalIsPositiveInScreening = new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({encounter, formElement}).when.latestValueInPreviousEncounters("0c97ea86-c0ef-4ec3-aadc-7cd5309bf78f").containsAnswerConceptName("7ecda14e-6d43-4328-8724-548a23e9f096").matches();
    const sickelCellIsPositiveInScreening = new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({encounter, formElement}).when.latestValueInPreviousEncounters("cec6bd53-8f11-4b8d-91d5-d12c469292af").containsAnswerConceptName("7ecda14e-6d43-4328-8724-548a23e9f096").matches();
    visibility = thalIsPositiveInScreening || sickelCellIsPositiveInScreening;
  }
  else if (!isScreeningDone) {
      const edtaBloodSampleCollectedIsYes = new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({encounter, formElement}).when.valueInEncounter("fe83aac2-1373-4031-9f39-137dcfea0dba").containsAnswerConceptName("5415e207-542c-44b0-a624-5bf14b32ffcc").matches();
      visibility = edtaBloodSampleCollectedIsYes;
  }
  
  return new imports.rulesConfig.FormElementStatus(formElement.uuid, visibility, value, answersToSkip, validationErrors);
};
```

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
    const programEncounter = params.entity;
    const moment = imports.moment;
    const formElement = params.formElement;
    const _ = imports.lodash;
    let visibility = true;
    let value = null;
    let answersToSkip = [];
    let validationErrors = [];
    let weightPercentile = null;
    
    const weight = programEncounter.getObservationValue('148fb61a-9d85-4876-b20f-50235c0d1d6e');

    let expectedWeight = null;
    if (weight) {
        const ageInYears = programEncounter.programEnrolment.individual.getAgeInYears(moment(programEncounter.encounterDateTime), true);
        const ageInMonths = programEncounter.programEnrolment.individual.getAgeInMonths(moment(programEncounter.encounterDateTime), true);

        if (ageInYears >= 1 && ageInYears <= 6) {
            expectedWeight = ageInYears * 2 + 8;
            expectedWeight = expectedWeight.toFixed(2);
        } else if (ageInYears < 1) { 
            expectedWeight = 0.5 * (ageInYears * 12) + 4;  
            expectedWeight = expectedWeight.toFixed(2);
        } else if (ageInYears > 6) {
            expectedWeight = ageInYears * 2 + 8;
            expectedWeight = expectedWeight.toFixed(2);
        }

        if (expectedWeight) {
            weightPercentile = (weight / expectedWeight) * 100;
            weightPercentile = weightPercentile.toFixed(2);
        }
    }

    if (weightPercentile) {
        if (weightPercentile > 80) {
            value = "Normal";
        } else if (weightPercentile > 70 && weightPercentile <= 80) {
            value = "Grade 1 Malnutrition";
        } else if (weightPercentile > 60 && weightPercentile <= 70) {
            value = "Grade 2 Malnutrition";
        } else if (weightPercentile > 50 && weightPercentile <= 60) {
            value = "Grade 3 Malnutrition";
        } else if (weightPercentile <= 50) {
            value = "Grade 4 Malnutrition";
        }
    }
    
    return new imports.rulesConfig.FormElementStatus(formElement.uuid, visibility, value, answersToSkip, validationErrors);
};
```

### `GDGSGOM` · `form[Farmer Registration].fe[Category of farmer]`  (1922 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
  const individual = params.entity;
  const moment = imports.moment;
  const formElement = params.formElement;
  const _ = imports.lodash;
  let visibility = true;
  let value = null;
  let answersToSkip = [];
  let validationErrors = [];
  
  const condition11 = new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({individual, formElement}).when.valueInRegistration("19778a2e-3397-4e79-8a31-9341dc50f336").defined.matches();

  if(condition11){
  
    const land_val = individual.getObservationValue("19778a2e-3397-4e79-8a31-9341dc50f336");
    
    const cat_val = individual.getObservationValue("afc7de39-650e-442d-9818-ae12448041d7");
    
    const rangeConditions = [
      { answer: "d788c182-e7de-48ea-a23a-cef038c588c2", range: [0, 2.49], error_message: "For Marginal: Land holding should be in the range of 0 - 2.49" },
      { answer: "003458ff-be84-4937-9c44-636819ec3738", range: [2.5, 4.99], error_message: "For Small: Land holding should be in the range of 2.5 - 4.99" },
      { answer: "34cfaa23-8c27-4ca1-899c-9f1106788c19", range: [5, 9.99], error_message: "For Semi Medium: Land holding should be in the range of 5 - 9.55" },
      { answer: "e67585a0-0a7d-4976-aabe-04e38069149f", range: [10, 24.99], error_message: "For Medium: Land holding should be in the range of 10 - 24.99" },
      { answer: "3f88926b-0bbd-4dc3-aca8-b8ad5ad30ab0", range: [25, Infinity], error_message: "For Large: Land holding should be greater than 25" }
  ];

    const selectedRangeCondition = rangeConditions.find(condition => condition.answer === cat_val);

    if (selectedRangeCondition && !(land_val >= selectedRangeCondition.range[0] && land_val <= selectedRangeCondition.range[1])) {
        validationErrors.push(selectedRangeCondition.error_message);
    }
  }
  
  return new imports.rulesConfig.FormElementStatus(formElement.uuid, visibility, value, answersToSkip, validationErrors);
};
```

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
  const programEncounter = params.entity;
  const moment = imports.moment;
  const formElement = params.formElement;
  const _ = imports.lodash;
  let visibility = true;
  let value = null;
  let answersToSkip = [];
  let validationErrors = [];
  
  const condition11 = new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({programEncounter, formElement}).when.valueInEncounter("cd77cb7a-842e-418a-8dec-c4f7030e594f").greaterThan(0).matches();
  
  visibility = condition11 ;
  
  if(visibility){
    const noOfChildDelivered = parseFloat(programEncounter.getObservationReadableValue('cd77cb7a-842e-418a-8dec-c4f7030e594f'));
    
    console.log('noOfChildDelivered--->',noOfChildDelivered);
    
    const childrenDetails = programEncounter.getObservationValue('8449a749-a977-4f74-af99-ccfc14749693');
    
    console.log('childrenDetails--->',childrenDetails);
    
    let activeGroupCount = 0;
    
    if (Array.isArray(childrenDetails)) {
      console.log('in array if');
      activeGroupCount = childrenDetails.filter(child => {
        if (!child || typeof child !== 'object') return false;
  
        return Object.values(child).some(value => value !== undefined && value !== null && value !== '');
      }).length;
    }
    console.log('activeGroupCount--->',activeGroupCount);
    
    if (!isNaN(noOfChildDelivered) && activeGroupCount > noOfChildDelivered) {
      validationErrors.push("Repeatable Question Groups should not exceed the total number of Delivered child/children.");
    } else if (!isNaN(noOfChildDelivered) && activeGroupCount < noOfChildDelivered) {
      validationErrors.push("Repeatable Question Groups should not less than the total number of Delivered child/children.");
    }
  }
  
  return new imports.rulesConfig.FormElementStatus(formElement.uuid, visibility, value, answersToSkip, validationErrors);
};
```

### `Ashwini uat` · `form[Covid Encounter].fe[Color code]`  (1697 bytes)

```javascript
({params, imports}) => {
    const programEncounter = params.entity;
    const formElement = params.formElement;
    

    const colors = ['Green', 'Orange', 'Red'];
    let finalColor = 'Green';
    
    let changeColor = (color) => {
        if (colors.indexOf(color) > colors.indexOf(finalColor)) {
            finalColor = color;
        }
    }
    let temperature = programEncounter.getObservationReadableValue('Fever?');
    const temperatures = programEncounter.programEnrolment.getObservationsForConceptName('Fever?');
    let feverDays = temperatures.filter(temperature => temperature.obs === '04bb1773-c353-44a1-a68c-9b448e07ff70')
    if (temperature === 'Yes' && feverDays.length > 3) {
        changeColor('Red');
    }
    let cough = programEncounter.getObservationReadableValue('Cough?');
    const coughh = programEncounter.programEnrolment.getObservationsForConceptName('Cough?');
    let coughDays = coughh.filter(cough => cough.obs === '04bb1773-c353-44a1-a68c-9b448e07ff70')
    if (cough === 'Yes' && coughDays.length > 3) {
        changeColor('Red');
    }
    let saturation = programEncounter.getObservationReadableValue('Saturation value');
       if  (saturation < 94) { 
            changeColor('Red');
            }
       if  (saturation == 94) { 
            changeColor('Orange');
            }
    let Respiratory = programEncounter.getObservationReadableValue('Respiratory rate value');
       if  (Respiratory > 26) { 
            changeColor('Red');
            }
       if  (Respiratory >= 24 && Respiratory <= 26) { 
            changeColor('Orange');
            }
    return new imports.rulesConfig.FormElementStatus(formElement.uuid, true, finalColor);
}
```

### `Nala Kholikaran 26-27` · `form[Farmer Registration]`  (2857 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
    const individual = params.entity;
    const individualUUID = individual.uuid;
    const fullName = individual.firstName.toLowerCase() + ' ' + individual.lastName.toLowerCase();
    const lowestAddressLevel = individual.lowestAddressLevel;
    const individualService = params.services.individualService;
    let validationResults = [];
    let allUUIDs = [];

    const mobileNumber = individual.getObservationReadableValue('660f6741-3ab2-4300-8404-397c4eb82d9e');
    
    const farmerSubjectUUID = '73271784-512d-4435-8dc8-0f102b99d682'
    //const farmers = individualService.getAllBySubjectTypeUUID(farmerSubjectUUID);
    
    allUUIDs = individualService.findAllSubjectsWithMobileNumberForType(mobileNumber, farmerSubjectUUID)
        .map((farmer) => ({
            uuid: farmer.uuid, 
            full_name: farmer.firstName.toLowerCase() + ' ' + farmer.lastName.toLowerCase()
        }));
    
    if (mobileNumber) {
        const payload = {
            "name": "duplicate_mobile_nos",
            "queryParams": {
                "mobile_number": mobileNumber
            }
        };

        const headers = {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "Accept": "application/json",
            "auth-token": params.authToken
        };

        const request = {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: headers,
            body: JSON.stringify(payload),
            credentials: "include"
        };

        return fetch("https://app.rwb.avniproject.org/executeQuery", request)
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                const uniqueNames = new Set();

                data.data.forEach(farmer => {
                    allUUIDs.push({uuid: farmer[0], full_name: farmer[1]});
                });

                allUUIDs = allUUIDs.filter(obj => {
                    if (!obj.full_name || uniqueNames.has(obj.full_name) || individualUUID == obj.uuid || obj.full_name == fullName) {
                        return false;
                    }
                    uniqueNames.add(obj.full_name);
                    return true;
                });

                if (allUUIDs.length >= 5) {
                    validationResults.push(imports.common.createValidationError(
                        'This number has already been used 5 times for registration. Kindly register with another number.'
                    ));
                }
                return validationResults;
            })
            .catch(error => {
                validationResults.push(imports.common.createValidationError(
                    'Kindly switch on the internet connection to proceed further'
                ));
                
                return validationResults;
            });
    }
    
    return validationResults;
};
```

### `Gubbachi` · `form[Daily Activity Update & Reflections Encounter]`  (2061 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
  const programEncounter = params.entity;
  const _ = imports.lodash;
  const validationResults = [];
  
  if(programEncounter.programEnrolment.individual && programEncounter.programEnrolment.individual.groupSubjects) {
    let membersubjects = programEncounter.programEnrolment.individual.groupSubjects.filter(gs => !gs.voided);
    const totalStudents = membersubjects.length;
    
    let studentsCompletedActivities = programEncounter.getObservationValue('e0d1c2b3-a4f5-6789-3456-789012345007') || [];
    const completedCount = Array.isArray(studentsCompletedActivities) ? studentsCompletedActivities.length : 0;
    
    let groupedObservation = programEncounter.findGroupedObservation('c0d1e2f3-a4b5-6789-3456-789012345003');
    
    let remedialStudentsCount = 0;
    
    if(groupedObservation && groupedObservation.length > 0) {
      groupedObservation.forEach((group) => {
        let studentObs = group.groupObservations.find(obs => obs.concept.uuid === '8fb8200c-3ec8-408c-a67d-fda6e2a41f1a');
        let reasonObs = group.groupObservations.find(obs => obs.concept.uuid === 'f1e2d3c4-b5a6-7890-4567-890123456005');
        
        if(studentObs && studentObs.valueJSON && studentObs.valueJSON.answer && 
           reasonObs && reasonObs.valueJSON && reasonObs.valueJSON.answer) {
          
          const studentsInGroup = studentObs.valueJSON.answer;
          const studentCount = Array.isArray(studentsInGroup) ? studentsInGroup.length : 1;
          remedialStudentsCount += studentCount;
        }
      });
    }
    
    const accountedStudents = completedCount + remedialStudentsCount;
    
    if(accountedStudents < totalStudents) {
      const missingCount = totalStudents - accountedStudents;
      
      let validationError = imports.common.createValidationError(
        `Please account for all students. ${missingCount} student(s) not accounted for in completed or remedial activities.`
      );
      validationResults.push(validationError);
    }
  }
  
  return validationResults;
};
```

### `Centre for Social Justice (CSJ)` · `form[Letter Impact Encounter]`  (1993 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
  const programEncounter = params.entity;
  const validationResults = [];
  const formElement = params.formElement;
  const _ = imports.lodash;
  let validationErrors = [];
  const moment = imports.moment;
  
  
  let documentDetails = programEncounter.findGroupedObservation("6a66028e-e11c-4b47-a94d-695d1b232577");
  if(documentDetails && documentDetails.length > 0){

    for(let i = 0;i<documentDetails.length;i++){
      let currentQuestionGroup = documentDetails[i]
      let impactDescription= currentQuestionGroup.findObservationByConceptUUID("bed28fd5-e245-42d5-b547-6b100a90c1d9");
      let monetaryImpact=  currentQuestionGroup.findObservationByConceptUUID("35d197f1-e61c-4efe-a8a9-7d65f741edad");


      if((impactDescription=== null || impactDescription=== undefined) && (monetaryImpact=== null || monetaryImpact=== undefined)){
        let validationError = imports.common.createValidationError('Please answer either Impact Description OR Monetary Impact Question');
        validationResults.push(validationError);
      }
    }
  }  
  
  const encounterDateForEditCase = programEncounter.getObservationValue("1f4bc226-9e3f-42c9-a332-2d81cd8e64f7");
  const encounterDate = programEncounter.encounterDateTime;

  if (!encounterDateForEditCase) {
    if (encounterDate) {
      const today = moment().startOf('day');
      const encDate = moment(encounterDate).startOf('day');

      if (!encDate.isSame(today, 'day')) {
        validationResults.push(
          imports.common.createValidationError("Encounter Date should be today's date.")
        );
      }
    }
  } else {
    const encDateEdit = moment(encounterDate).startOf('day');
    const encDateOriginal = moment(encounterDateForEditCase).startOf('day');

    if (!encDateEdit.isSame(encDateOriginal, 'day')) {
      validationResults.push(
        imports.common.createValidationError("Encounter Date cannot be changed.")
      );
    }
  }
  
  return validationResults;
};
```

### `Goonj` · `form[Village Registration]`  (1989 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
  const individual = params.entity;
  const moment = imports.moment;
  const _ = imports.lodash;
  const validationResults = [];
  const individualService = params.services.individualService;

  function toStartCase(str) {
    return str
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase()
    .split(/[\s]+/)
    .map(word => word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.slice(1))
    .join(' ');
  }  

  const isLocationMatch = (e1,e2,location) => {
    const {uuid,name} = location;
    const loc1 = e1.getObservationReadableValue(uuid) || "";
    const loc2 = e2.getObservationReadableValue(uuid) || "";
    console.log(`value of ${name} ${loc1} ${loc2}`);
    return toStartCase(loc1) === toStartCase(loc2)
  }

  const OtherLocations = [
    {name:"Other Block",uuid:"e2d35dee-c34f-4f54-a68b-f32ee81835b6"},
    {name:"Other Village",uuid:"16b4db7c-e0a8-41f1-ac67-07470a762d9f"}
  ]  


  
  let villages = individualService.getSubjectsInLocation(individual.lowestAddressLevel, 'Village');




  if(villages && villages.length > 0){
    villages =  villages.filter(({voided,uuid})=>!voided && (uuid!=individual.uuid));
    if(villages.length > 0){
      let isPresent = true;
      const otherBlock = individual.getObservationReadableValue("e2d35dee-c34f-4f54-a68b-f32ee81835b6");
      const otherVillage =  individual.getObservationReadableValue("16b4db7c-e0a8-41f1-ac67-07470a762d9f");
      if(otherBlock || otherVillage){
        villages = villages.filter(village=> isLocationMatch(village,individual,OtherLocations[0]) &&  isLocationMatch(village,individual,OtherLocations[1]))
        if(villages.length > 0){
          isPresent = true;
        }else{
          isPresent = false;
        }
      }
      if(isPresent){
        validationResults.push(imports.common.createValidationError("Village for specified geographical location already exists."));
      }
    }
  }                                                              

  return validationResults;
};
```

### `Animedh Charitable Trust DNH` · `form[Child Home Visit Anthropometry Encounter]`  (1391 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
  const programEncounter = params.entity;
  const individual = programEncounter.individual;
  const moment = imports.moment;
  const validationResults = [];

  const encounterDateTime = moment(programEncounter.encounterDateTime).format('YYYY-MM-DD');
  const maxVisitDateTime = moment(programEncounter.maxVisitDateTime).format('YYYY-MM-DD');

  // Fetch child's growth status
  const growthStatus = individual.getObservationValue("Weight for Height Status");

  // Set earliest visit date based on growth status
  let daysBeforeSchedule;
  if (growthStatus === "Normal") {
    daysBeforeSchedule = 5;
  } else if (["SAM", "MAM", "SUW", "MUW"].includes(growthStatus)) {
    daysBeforeSchedule = 3;
  } else {
    daysBeforeSchedule = 0; // Default if status is missing
  }

  const earliestVisitDateTime = moment(programEncounter.earliestVisitDateTime).subtract(daysBeforeSchedule, 'days').format('YYYY-MM-DD');

  // Validation checks
  if (!(encounterDateTime >= earliestVisitDateTime)) {
    validationResults.push(imports.common.createValidationError("This visit cannot be completed sooner"));
  }

  if (encounterDateTime >= maxVisitDateTime) {
    validationResults.push(imports.common.createValidationError("Overdue forms cannot be filled, please cancel this form so that the next due form can be scheduled"));
  }

  return validationResults;
};
```

### `Centre for Social Justice (CSJ)` · `form[RTI Impact Encounter]`  (1992 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
  const programEncounter = params.entity;
  const validationResults = [];
  const formElement = params.formElement;
  const _ = imports.lodash;
  let validationErrors = [];
  const moment = imports.moment;
  
  let documentDetails = programEncounter.findGroupedObservation("dd15d326-ae66-478e-996f-aab226a4b1ce");
  if(documentDetails && documentDetails.length > 0){

    for(let i = 0;i<documentDetails.length;i++){
      let currentQuestionGroup = documentDetails[i]
      let impactDescription= currentQuestionGroup.findObservationByConceptUUID("66ed83a5-9311-44d9-a696-697de6e57ac1");
      let monetaryImpact=  currentQuestionGroup.findObservationByConceptUUID("c7de0f07-5fad-4e9a-b276-28f3580f6ac8");

      if((impactDescription === null || impactDescription === undefined) && (monetaryImpact === null || monetaryImpact=== undefined)){
        let validationError = imports.common.createValidationError('Please answer either Impact Description OR Monetary Impact Question');
        validationResults.push(validationError);
      }
    }
  }
  
  const encounterDateForEditCase = programEncounter.getObservationValue("1f4bc226-9e3f-42c9-a332-2d81cd8e64f7");
  const encounterDate = programEncounter.encounterDateTime;

  if (!encounterDateForEditCase) {
    if (encounterDate) {
      const today = moment().startOf('day');
      const encDate = moment(encounterDate).startOf('day');

      if (!encDate.isSame(today, 'day')) {
        validationResults.push(
          imports.common.createValidationError("Encounter Date should be today's date.")
        );
      }
    }
  } else {
    const encDateEdit = moment(encounterDate).startOf('day');
    const encDateOriginal = moment(encounterDateForEditCase).startOf('day');

    if (!encDateEdit.isSame(encDateOriginal, 'day')) {
      validationResults.push(
        imports.common.createValidationError("Encounter Date cannot be changed.")
      );
    }
  }
    
  return validationResults;
};
```

### `Purna Clinic` · `subjectType[Individual]`  (7664 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({ params, imports }) => {
    const { summaries, individual } = params;
    const AFFECTED_DISEASE_OBSERVATION_UUID = "d56abf12-b1d7-4e00-8fe3-19d8aa5fea3c";
    let supportNeeded = [];

    const households = individual.groups
        .filter((grp) => grp.membershipEndDate == null && grp.voided == false && grp.groupSubject.voided == false)
        .map((grp) => grp.groupSubject);

    const enrolments = (individual.enrolments || [])
        .filter(e => e.program && e.program.name === 'Chronic Disease' && e.programExitDateTime == null);

    const firstEnrolment = enrolments?.[0] || null;
    const encounters = firstEnrolment?.getEncountersOfType('Services Delivered') || null;
    const latestEncounter = encounters?.[0] || null;
    
    // Function to check if household observation has answer
    function observationHasAnswer(observation, conceptUUID, answerUUID) {
        return observation.some((obs) => {
            let parsed;
            try {
                parsed = JSON.parse(obs.valueJSON);
            } catch (e) {
                return false;
            }

            const answer = parsed.answer;
            if (!answer) return false;

            return obs.concept.uuid == conceptUUID && answer == answerUUID;
        });
    }

    // Document Support Neeeded
    function hasDocumentSupportNeeded() {
        const isDocumentationSupportNeeded = new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({ individual })
            .when.valueInRegistration("8b32818c-944e-475b-9837-3133606846f4").containsAnswerConceptName("84032a63-eb48-4ad0-9b9a-bac3d810d204")
            .or.when.valueInRegistration("49466c1b-f6ce-4ede-8e58-910429fed242").containsAnswerConceptName("84032a63-eb48-4ad0-9b9a-bac3d810d204")
            .or.when.valueInRegistration("43df6269-2ea6-4a93-8af7-46553eae1345").containsAnswerConceptName("84032a63-eb48-4ad0-9b9a-bac3d810d204")
            .or.when.valueInRegistration("5c82d1a5-eec3-4be7-b972-19ec772afb0a").containsAnswerConceptName("84032a63-eb48-4ad0-9b9a-bac3d810d204")
            .or.when.valueInRegistration("99248c27-346c-4af7-8380-0cbd64d03e77").containsAnswerConceptName("84032a63-eb48-4ad0-9b9a-bac3d810d204")
            .or.when.valueInRegistration("4ae04fb5-cc23-4803-abfb-3272fbff0ae6").containsAnswerConceptName("84032a63-eb48-4ad0-9b9a-bac3d810d204")
            .matches();

        const rationCardNotAvailable = households.some((grp) => observationHasAnswer(grp.observations, '7014a9df-1cdd-4d62-bcfc-ba99b9938c93', 'ed9555a1-3861-4892-834f-3e4fcb5b485e'));

        const incomeCertificateNotAvailable = households.some((grp) => observationHasAnswer(grp.observations, '14bf5a2d-15db-496a-8720-3322fa2e17ec', '84032a63-eb48-4ad0-9b9a-bac3d810d204'));

        let documentSupportNeeded = isDocumentationSupportNeeded || rationCardNotAvailable || incomeCertificateNotAvailable;

        if(latestEncounter) {
            let stillDocumentSupportNeeded = false;

            const noAnswerUUID = '84032a63-eb48-4ad0-9b9a-bac3d810d204';
            const notAvailableUUID = 'ed9555a1-3861-4892-834f-3e4fcb5b485e';

            const conceptUUIDs = {
                "8b32818c-944e-475b-9837-3133606846f4": "Name in Ration Card",
                "14bf5a2d-15db-496a-8720-3322fa2e17ec": "Income Certificate available?",
                "49466c1b-f6ce-4ede-8e58-910429fed242": "Ayushman Card Available",
                "99248c27-346c-4af7-8380-0cbd64d03e77": "Bank Account Available",
                "43df6269-2ea6-4a93-8af7-46553eae1345": "Aadhaar Card Available",
                "4ae04fb5-cc23-4803-abfb-3272fbff0ae6": "Job card Available",
                "5c82d1a5-eec3-4be7-b972-19ec772afb0a":	"Voter ID Available"
            }

            for (let uuid of Object.keys(conceptUUIDs)) {
                const value = latestEncounter.getObservationValue(uuid);

                if (value == noAnswerUUID) {
                    stillDocumentSupportNeeded = true;
                    break;
                }
            }

            let stillRationCardNotAvailable = latestEncounter.getObservationValue('7014a9df-1cdd-4d62-bcfc-ba99b9938c93') == notAvailableUUID;  

            documentSupportNeeded = documentSupportNeeded && (stillDocumentSupportNeeded || stillRationCardNotAvailable);
        }

        return documentSupportNeeded;
    }

    if(hasDocumentSupportNeeded()) {
        supportNeeded.push('8545f51c-8640-4893-b234-40d43506d294');
    }

    // Govt Scheme Benefit Needed
    function hasGovtSchemeBenefitNeeded() {
        const ELIGIBILITY_OBS = "e41f8f28-a14b-4a4e-9508-18b180de261b";
        const BENEFIT_OBS = "a3804659-d0ae-4ea7-9284-a2adab0c04d8";
        const NONE_CONCEPT = "e1c86f27-0b67-440e-b9d2-4e9abc56b991";
    
        const notEligible = new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({ individual })
            .when.valueInRegistration(ELIGIBILITY_OBS)
            .containsAnswerConceptName(NONE_CONCEPT)
            .matches();
        
        if (notEligible) return false;
    
        const eligibleSchemes = individual.getObservationValue(ELIGIBILITY_OBS) || [];
        let benefitSchemes = individual.getObservationValue(BENEFIT_OBS) || [];
    
        const allEligibleInBenefit = (extra = []) => {
            const combined = [...benefitSchemes, ...extra];
            return eligibleSchemes.every(val => combined.includes(val));
        };
    
        if (allEligibleInBenefit()) return false;
    
        if (latestEncounter) {
            const newlyBenefited = latestEncounter.getObservationValue(BENEFIT_OBS) || [];
            if (allEligibleInBenefit(newlyBenefited)) return false;
        }
    
        return true;
    }

    if (hasGovtSchemeBenefitNeeded()) {
        supportNeeded.push('aeb17df0-a5cc-45f9-ae07-cf462b44c790');
    }

    // Medical Support Needed
    const isMedicalSupportNeeded = new imports.rulesConfig.RuleCondition({ individual })
        .when.valueInRegistration("7e2ab8eb-adaa-49f3-8f98-64acac49e539").containsAnswerConceptNameOtherThan("644aea1f-a768-4894-948c-73a8654a0000").matches();

    if (isMedicalSupportNeeded) {
        supportNeeded.push('1477eafb-a3e6-41bc-ae82-3518640d4af1');
    }

    // Manavrahat Support Needed
    function hasManavrahatNotRecieved() {
        let manavrahatNotRecieved = households.some((grp) =>
            observationHasAnswer(grp.observations, '818aec71-ed27-4a21-9929-a2d81fec2ffd', '84032a63-eb48-4ad0-9b9a-bac3d810d204')
        );

        if(latestEncounter) {
            const noAnswerUUID = '84032a63-eb48-4ad0-9b9a-bac3d810d204';
            const stillManavrahatNotRecieved = latestEncounter.getObservationValue('6da53ac6-4679-4f77-aa74-457aeef8e068') == noAnswerUUID;
            manavrahatNotRecieved = manavrahatNotRecieved && stillManavrahatNotRecieved;            
        }
        
        return manavrahatNotRecieved;
    } 

    if (hasManavrahatNotRecieved()) {
        supportNeeded.push('a36eb9d1-9f13-43f6-ba61-3e3a74a61937');
    }

    if (supportNeeded.length > 0) {
        summaries.push({ name: "Support Needed", value: supportNeeded });
    }

    // Function to Get the Affected Diseases
    function getAffectedDiseases() {
        if(firstEnrolment) {
            let affectedDiseases = firstEnrolment.findLatestObservationInEntireEnrolment(AFFECTED_DISEASE_OBSERVATION_UUID);
            if(!affectedDiseases) return false;
          
            return affectedDiseases.getValue();
        }
        return false;
    }
    
    const affectedDiseases = getAffectedDiseases();
    if(affectedDiseases) {
        summaries.push({ name: "Affected Diseases", value: affectedDiseases});
    }

    return summaries;
};
```

### `Nala Kholikaran 26-27` · `subjectType[Nala Work Order]`  (6793 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) =>  {
    const {summaries, individual} = params;
    
    const _ = imports.lodash;
    
    const ngoName = individual.findObservation('NGO Name');
    
    summaries.push({name: 'WaterBody', value: individual.lowestAddressLevel.name});

    function getTheLatestEncounter(encounterTypeName) {
        const encounters = individual.getEncounters()
            .filter((enc) => enc.encounterType.name === encounterTypeName && enc.voided == false && enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus &&  enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus.approvalStatus.status === 'Approved') || []
        if(encounters.length == 0) return null
        return encounters[0];
    }

    const latestDailyMachineRecording = getTheLatestEncounter('Excavating Machine Daily Recording');

    if(latestDailyMachineRecording) {
        const encounterDate = latestDailyMachineRecording.encounterDateTime;
        if(encounterDate) {
            summaries.push({name: 'Last Work order daily Recording - Machine Visit Date', value: encounterDate});
        }
    }
    
    const calculateSumOfObservationNumberValues = function(encounterTypeName, observationName) {
        return individual.getEncounters()
            .filter((enc) => enc.encounterType.name === encounterTypeName )
            .filter((enc) => enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus &&  enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus.approvalStatus.status === 'Approved' )
            .map((enc) => enc.getObservationReadableValue(observationName))
            .reduce((total, val) => total + parseFloat(val), 0);
    };

    const calculateSumOfObservationValues = function(encounterTypeName, observationName) {
        return individual.getEncounters()
            .filter((enc) => enc.encounterType.name === encounterTypeName )
            .filter((enc) =>  enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus && enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus.approvalStatus.status === 'Approved' )
            .map((enc) => enc.getObservationReadableValue(observationName) || 0)
            .reduce((total, val) => total + val, 0);
    }
    
    const calculateSumOfObservationValuesMachineWise = function(encounterTypeName, observationName,typeOfMachine) {
        const subjects = params.services.individualService.getSubjectsInLocation(individual.lowestAddressLevel,'Excavating Machine');
        const machineTypesubjects = subjects
            .filter((e) => e.getObservationReadableValue('Type of Machine') === typeOfMachine);
        
        const uuids = _.map(machineTypesubjects, ({uuid}) => uuid);
        
        return individual.getEncounters()
            .filter((enc) => enc.encounterType.name === encounterTypeName )
            .filter((enc) =>  enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus && enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus.approvalStatus.status === 'Approved' )
            .filter((enc) => _.includes(uuids,enc.getObservationReadableValue('Excavating Machine')) )
            .map((enc) => enc.getObservationReadableValue(observationName) || 0)
            .reduce((total, val) => total + val, 0);
    }

    const getTotalMachineType = function(encounterTypeName, observationName, typeOfMachine) {
        const encounters = individual.getEncounters()
            .filter((enc) => enc.encounterType.name === encounterTypeName)
            .filter((enc) =>  enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus && enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus.approvalStatus.status === 'Approved'); 
                
        const subjects = params.services.individualService.getSubjectsInLocation(individual.lowestAddressLevel,'Excavating Machine');           
        const uniqueSubjects = _.uniqBy(subjects, 'uuid');
            
        let obsSubjects = [];
        const temp  = _.forEach(encounters, (enc) => { 
        obsSubjects.push(_.find(uniqueSubjects,
            (subject) => subject.uuid === enc.getObservationReadableValue(observationName)));
        });
        const uniqueObsSubjects = _.uniqBy(obsSubjects, 'uuid');
            
        if(uniqueObsSubjects.length > 0){
        return uniqueObsSubjects.filter((e) => !_.isEqual(e,undefined))
            .filter((e) => e.getObservationReadableValue('Type of Machine') === typeOfMachine).length;
        } else 
        return 0;
    }
    
    const getTotalBeneficiaries = function(encounterTypeName, observationName) {
        const encounters = individual.getEncounters()
            .filter((enc) => enc.encounterType.name === encounterTypeName)
            .filter((enc) =>  enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus && enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus.approvalStatus.status === 'Approved');      
        const distinctNames = new Set(encounters.map(enc =>`${enc.getObservationReadableValue(observationName)}`));
        return distinctNames.size;
    }
    
    function getTotalSiltExcavated(encounterTypeName, observationName) {
        const encounters = individual.getEncounters()
        .filter(enc => 
            enc.encounterType.name == encounterTypeName &&
            enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus &&
            enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus.approvalStatus.status == 'Approved'
        );
    
        let total = 0;
        encounters.forEach(enc => {
        const value = enc.getObservationValue(observationName);
        if (value && typeof value == 'number') {
            total += value;
        }
        });
    
        return total;
    }
    
    summaries.push({name: 'Number of machine', value: getTotalBeneficiaries('Excavating Machine Daily Recording','Excavating Machine')});
    
    summaries.push({name: 'Total number of JCBs', value: getTotalMachineType('Excavating Machine Daily Recording','Excavating Machine','JCB')});
    
    summaries.push({name: 'Total number of Poclains', value: getTotalMachineType('Excavating Machine Daily Recording','Excavating Machine','Poclain')});
    
    summaries.push({name: 'Total working hours of machine', value: calculateSumOfObservationValues('Excavating Machine Daily Recording','Working Hours as per time')});
    
    summaries.push({name: 'Total working hours of JCBs', value: calculateSumOfObservationValuesMachineWise('Excavating Machine Daily Recording','Working Hours as per time','JCB')});
    
    summaries.push({name: 'Total working hours of Poclains', value: calculateSumOfObservationValuesMachineWise('Excavating Machine Daily Recording','Working Hours as per time','Poclain')});

    if(ngoName) {
        summaries.push({name: 'NGO Name', value: ngoName.getValue()});
    }

    const siltAsPerPlan = individual.getObservationReadableValue('Silt to be excavated as per plan');
    summaries.push({name: 'Silt to be excavated as per plan', value: siltAsPerPlan});
    
    summaries.push({name: 'Cumulative Silt Excavated by the machines', value: getTotalSiltExcavated('Excavating Machine Daily Recording','Silt Excavated')})
        
    return summaries;
};
```

```javascript
      .filter(enc => 
        enc.encounterType.name == encounterTypeName &&
        enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus &&
        enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus.approvalStatus.status == 'Approved'
      );
  
    let total = 0;
    encounters.forEach(enc => {
      const value = enc.getObservationValue(observationName);
      if (value && typeof value == 'number') {
        total += value;
      }
    });
  
    return total;
  }
  
  summaries.push({name: 'Number of machine', value: getTotalBeneficiaries('Excavating Machine Daily Recording','Excavating Machine')});
  
  summaries.push({name: 'Total number of JCBs', value: getTotalMachineType('Excavating Machine Daily Recording','Excavating Machine','JCB')});
  
  summaries.push({name: 'Total number of Poclains', value: getTotalMachineType('Excavating Machine Daily Recording','Excavating Machine','Poclain')});
   
  summaries.push({name: 'Total working hours of machine', value: calculateSumOfObservationValues('Excavating Machine Daily Recording','Working Hours as per time')});
  
  summaries.push({name: 'Total working hours of JCBs', value: calculateSumOfObservationValuesMachineWise('Excavating Machine Daily Recording','Working Hours as per time','JCB')});
  
  summaries.push({name: 'Total working hours of Poclains', value: calculateSumOfObservationValuesMachineWise('Excavating Machine Daily Recording','Working Hours as per time','Poclain')});

  if(ngoName) {
    summaries.push({name: 'NGO Name', value: ngoName.getValue()});
  }

  const siltAsPerPlan = individual.getObservationReadableValue('Silt to be excavated as per plan');
  summaries.push({name: 'Silt to be excavated as per plan', value: siltAsPerPlan});
  
  summaries.push({name: 'Cumulative Silt Excavated by the machines', value: getTotalSiltExcavated('Excavating Machine Daily Recording','Silt Excavated')})
     
  return summaries;
};
```

### `GDGSGOM` · `subjectType[Work Order]`  (5565 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
    const {summaries, individual} = params;
    const _ = imports.lodash;

    summaries.push({name: 'WaterBody', value: individual.lowestAddressLevel.name});

    const calculateSumOfObservationNumberValues = function (encounterTypeName, observationName) {
        return individual.getEncounters()
            .filter((enc) => enc.encounterType.name === encounterTypeName)
            .filter((enc) => enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus && enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus.approvalStatus.status === 'Approved')
            .map((enc) => enc.getObservationReadableValue(observationName))
            .reduce((total, val) => total + parseFloat(val), 0);
    };

    const calculateSumOfObservationValues = function (encounterTypeName, observationName) {
        return individual.getEncounters()
            .filter((enc) => enc.encounterType.name === encounterTypeName)
            .filter((enc) => enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus && enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus.approvalStatus.status === 'Approved')
            .map((enc) => enc.getObservationReadableValue(observationName) || 0)
            .reduce((total, val) => total + val, 0);
    };

    const calculateSumOfObservationValuesMachineWise = function (encounterTypeName, observationName, typeOfMachine) {
        const subjects = params.services.individualService.getSubjectsInLocation(individual.lowestAddressLevel, 'Excavating Machine');
        const machineTypesubjects = subjects.filter((e) => e.getObservationReadableValue('Type of Machine') === typeOfMachine);
        const uuids = _.map(machineTypesubjects, ({uuid}) => uuid);
        return individual.getEncounters()
            .filter((enc) => enc.encounterType.name === encounterTypeName)
            .filter((enc) => enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus && enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus.approvalStatus.status === 'Approved')
            .filter((enc) => _.includes(uuids, enc.getObservationReadableValue('Excavating Machine')))
            .map((enc) => enc.getObservationReadableValue(observationName) || 0)
            .reduce((total, val) => total + val, 0);
    };

    const getTotalMachineType = function (encounterTypeName, observationName, typeOfMachine) {
        const encounters = individual.getEncounters()
            .filter((enc) => enc.encounterType.name === encounterTypeName)
            .filter((enc) => enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus && enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus.approvalStatus.status === 'Approved');
        const subjects = params.services.individualService.getSubjectsInLocation(individual.lowestAddressLevel, 'Excavating Machine');
        const uniqueSubjects = _.uniqBy(subjects, 'uuid');
        let obsSubjects = [];
        _.forEach(encounters, (enc) => {
            obsSubjects.push(_.find(uniqueSubjects, (subject) => subject.uuid === enc.getObservationReadableValue(observationName)));
        });
        const uniqueObsSubjects = _.uniqBy(obsSubjects, 'uuid');
        if (uniqueObsSubjects.length > 0) {
            return uniqueObsSubjects
                .filter((e) => !_.isEqual(e, undefined))
                .filter((e) => e.getObservationReadableValue('Type of Machine') === typeOfMachine).length;
        } else {
            return 0;
        }
    };

    const getTotalBeneficiaries = function (encounterTypeName, observationName) {
        const encounters = individual.getEncounters()
            .filter((enc) => enc.encounterType.name === encounterTypeName)
            .filter((enc) => enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus && enc.latestEntityApprovalStatus.approvalStatus.status === 'Approved');
        const distinctNames = new Set(encounters.map((enc) => `${enc.getObservationReadableValue(observationName)}`));
        return distinctNames.size;
    };

    summaries.push({
        name: 'Total Silt carted',
        value: calculateSumOfObservationNumberValues('Work order daily Recording - Farmer', 'Total Silt carted').toFixed(2)
    });
    summaries.push({
        name: 'Number of farmers',
        value: getTotalBeneficiaries('Work order daily Recording - Farmer', 'Farmer/Beneficiary')
    });

    const totalNumOfJCBs = getTotalMachineType('Work order daily Recording - Machine', 'Excavating Machine', 'JCB');
    const totalNumOfPoclains = getTotalMachineType('Work order daily Recording - Machine', 'Excavating Machine', 'Poclain');

    summaries.push({
        name: 'Total number of JCBs',
        value: totalNumOfJCBs
    });
    summaries.push({
        name: 'Total number of Poclains',
        value: totalNumOfPoclains
    });

    summaries.push({
        name: 'Number of machine',
        value: totalNumOfJCBs + totalNumOfPoclains
    });

    const workingHoursJCB = calculateSumOfObservationValuesMachineWise('Work order daily Recording - Machine', 'Working Hours as per time', 'JCB');
    const workingHoursPoclain = calculateSumOfObservationValuesMachineWise('Work order daily Recording - Machine', 'Working Hours as per time', 'Poclain');

    summaries.push({
        name: 'Total working hours of JCBs',
        value: workingHoursJCB
    });
    summaries.push({
        name: 'Total working hours of Poclains',
        value: workingHoursPoclain
    });

    summaries.push({
        name: 'Total working hours of machine',
        value: workingHoursJCB + workingHoursPoclain
    });

    const siltAsPerPlan = individual.getObservationReadableValue('Silt to be excavated as per plan');
    summaries.push({
        name: 'Silt to be excavated as per plan',
        value: siltAsPerPlan
    });

    return summaries;
};
```

### `Calcutta Kids` · `subjectType[Demand]`  (4731 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';
({params, imports}) => {
    const {summaries, individual} = params;

    const calculateSumOfObservationValuesRecieved = function (materialTypes) {
        return individual.getEncounters()
            .filter((enc) => enc.encounterType.name === 'Dispatch receipt')
            .flatMap((enc) => enc.getObservationValue('5dfb2f28-b866-4442-be01-0ed451c6aad9'))
            .filter(obs => !_.isNil(obs))
            .map((obs) =>obs.getReadableValue())
            .map((keyValues) => {
           // console.log('obs--1-->',obs);
            console.log('keyValues--1-->',keyValues);    
  const isQuantityMatching = _.get(_.find(keyValues, (kv) => kv['Quantity matching']), 'Quantity matching');
  const matchingQuantity = _.get(_.find(keyValues, (kv) => kv['Quantity (Dispatched)']), 'Quantity (Dispatched)') || 0;
                
                const notMatchingQuantity =
                    _.get(_.find(keyValues, (kv) => kv['Quantity']), 'Quantity') || 0;
                const typeOfMaterial = _.get(_.find(keyValues, (kv) => kv['Type Of Material']), 'Type Of Material');
                if (isQuantityMatching === 'Yes' && materialTypes.includes(typeOfMaterial))
                    return matchingQuantity;
                else if (isQuantityMatching === 'No' && materialTypes.includes(typeOfMaterial))
                    return notMatchingQuantity;
                else return 0;
            })
            
            .reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    };


    const calculateSumOfObservationValuesDistributed = function (materialTypes) {
        return individual.getEncounters()
            .filter((enc) => enc.encounterType.name === 'Distribution')
            .flatMap((enc) => enc.getObservationValue('3093c0f1-5e3f-47eb-92ed-0067b41b6f9c'))
            .map((obs) => obs.getReadableValue())
            .map((keyValues) => {
          //  console.log('keyValues--2-->',keyValues);
          //  console.log('obs--2-->',obs);
                const quantityNumber = _.get(_.find(keyValues, (kv) => kv['Quantity']), 'Quantity') || 0;
                const typeOfMaterial = _.get(_.find(keyValues, (kv) => kv['Type Of Material']), 'Type Of Material');
                return materialTypes.includes(typeOfMaterial) ? quantityNumber : 0;
            })
            .reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    };

    if (calculateSumOfObservationValuesRecieved(['Kit']) != 0) {
    summaries.push({
        name: 'Number of kits received',
        value: calculateSumOfObservationValuesRecieved(['Kit'])
    });

    summaries.push({
        name: 'Number of kits distributed',
        value: calculateSumOfObservationValuesDistributed(['Kit'])
    });
}; 

    if (calculateSumOfObservationValuesRecieved(['Contributed item']) != 0) {
    summaries.push({
        name: 'Number of contributed items received',
        value: calculateSumOfObservationValuesRecieved(['Contributed item'])
    });

    summaries.push({
        name: 'Number of contributed items distributed',
        value: calculateSumOfObservationValuesDistributed(['Contributed item'])
    });
};

    if (calculateSumOfObservationValuesRecieved(['Purchased item']) != 0) {
    summaries.push({
        name: 'Number of purchased items received',
        value: calculateSumOfObservationValuesRecieved(['Purchased item'])
    });

    summaries.push({
        name: 'Number of purchased items distributed',
        value: calculateSumOfObservationValuesDistributed(['Purchased item'])
    });
};
 
    if (calculateSumOfObservationValuesRecieved(['Contributed track']) != 0) {
    summaries.push({
        name: 'Number of contributed track items received',
        value: calculateSumOfObservationValuesRecieved(['Contributed track'])
    });

    summaries.push({
        name: 'Number of contributed track items distributed',
        value: calculateSumOfObservationValuesDistributed(['Contributed track'])
    });
};

    if (calculateSumOfObservationValuesRecieved(['Goonj product']) != 0) {
      summaries.push({
        name: 'Number of goonj products received',
        value: calculateSumOfObservationValuesRecieved(['Goonj product'])
    });
;
    summaries.push({
        name: 'Number of goonj products distributed',
        value: calculateSumOfObservationValuesDistributed(['Goonj product'])
    });  
};

    if (calculateSumOfObservationValuesRecieved('Bulk Material') != 0) {
      summaries.push({
        name: 'Number of bulk materials received',
        value: calculateSumOfObservationValuesRecieved(['Bulk Material'])
    });

    summaries.push({
        name: 'Number of bulk materials distributed',
        value: calculateSumOfObservationValuesDistributed(['Bulk Material'])
    });   
};

    return summaries;
};
```

### `Centre for Social Justice (CSJ)` · `subjectType[Training]`  (4362 bytes)

```javascript
'use strict';

({ params, imports }) => {
  const { summaries, individual } = params;

  // REGISTRATION SUMMARY
  const observations = {
    typeOfTraining: individual.findObservation("Type of training"),
    natureOfTraining: individual.findObservation("Nature of training"),
    trainingParticipants: individual.findObservation("Training Participants"),
    whetherPhased: individual.findObservation("Whether phased"),
  };
    
  const trainingTypeUUIDs = {
    internal: "3feb2dac-7f40-49e7-82c8-a5c55b3feb8a",
    external: "f882e2b6-52e1-43cf-a45b-215e76ff05eb"
  };
  
  const selectedTrainingType = individual.getObservationValue("c7afa395-baae-4434-b8f7-b938b78051bd");

  if (observations.typeOfTraining) {
    summaries.push({ name: "Type of training", value: observations.typeOfTraining.getValue() });
  }

  if (selectedTrainingType === trainingTypeUUIDs.internal && observations.natureOfTraining) {
    summaries.push({ name: "Nature of training", value: observations.natureOfTraining.getValue() });
  }

  if (selectedTrainingType === trainingTypeUUIDs.external && observations.trainingParticipants) {
    summaries.push({ name: "Training Participants", value: observations.trainingParticipants.getValue() });
  }

  if (observations.whetherPhased) {
    summaries.push({ name: "Whether phased", value: observations.whetherPhased.getValue() });
  }

  // IF INTERNAL ENCOUNTER SUMMARY
  const isInternalTraining = individual.getObservationReadableValue("c7afa395-baae-4434-b8f7-b938b78051bd") === 'Internal';
  const isExternalTraining = individual.getObservationReadableValue("c7afa395-baae-4434-b8f7-b938b78051bd") === 'External';

  if (isInternalTraining) {
    let internalTrainingEncounter = individual.encounters.filter((enc) => 
        enc.encounterType.name === 'Internal Training' &&
        enc.encounterDateTime != null &&
        enc.voided === false &&
        enc.cancelDateTime == null
    );

    if (internalTrainingEncounter.length > 0) {
        internalTrainingEncounter = internalTrainingEncounter[0];

        const whichPhase = internalTrainingEncounter.getObservationValue("1732f13a-26c0-4ef9-a92b-78d65519b024");
        if (whichPhase) {
            summaries.push({ name: 'Which phase', value: [whichPhase] });
        }

        const trainingStart = internalTrainingEncounter.getObservationValue("841e5c85-9626-433c-a7a9-e27073025b68");
        if (trainingStart) {
            summaries.push({ name: 'Date (start date)', value: trainingStart });
        }

        const trainingEnd = internalTrainingEncounter.getObservationValue("5d01a701-f8b1-4edf-ab48-3d9b36d28554");
        if (trainingEnd) {
            summaries.push({ name: 'Date (end date)', value: trainingEnd });
        }

        const topicsCovered = internalTrainingEncounter.getObservationValue("7e8c602d-3f11-4bec-a463-174a40b98ffd");
        if (topicsCovered) {
            summaries.push({ name: 'Topics Covered', value: topicsCovered });
        }
    }
  }
  //IF External Training 
  else if (isExternalTraining) {
    let externalTrainingEncounter = individual.encounters.filter((enc) => 
        enc.encounterType.name === 'External Training' &&
        enc.encounterDateTime != null &&
        enc.voided === false &&
        enc.cancelDateTime == null
    );

    if (externalTrainingEncounter.length > 0) {
        externalTrainingEncounter = externalTrainingEncounter[0];

        const whichPhase = externalTrainingEncounter.getObservationValue("1732f13a-26c0-4ef9-a92b-78d65519b024");
        if (whichPhase) {
            summaries.push({ name: 'Which phase', value: [whichPhase] });
        }

        const trainingStart = externalTrainingEncounter.getObservationValue("841e5c85-9626-433c-a7a9-e27073025b68");
        if (trainingStart) {
            summaries.push({ name: 'Date (start date)', value: trainingStart });
        }

        const trainingEnd = externalTrainingEncounter.getObservationValue("5d01a701-f8b1-4edf-ab48-3d9b36d28554");
        if (trainingEnd) {
            summaries.push({ name: 'Date (end date)', value: trainingEnd });
        }

        const topicsCovered = externalTrainingEncounter.getObservationValue("7e8c602d-3f11-4bec-a463-174a40b98ffd");
        if (topicsCovered) {
            summaries.push({ name: 'Topics Covered', value: topicsCovered });
        }
    }
  }

  return summaries;
};
```

### `IPH Sickle Cell` · `subjectType[Participant]`  (2770 bytes)

```javascript
//SAMPLE RULE EXAMPLE
'use strict';
({params, imports}) =>  {
  const {summaries, individual} = params;
  const moment = imports.moment;
  summaries.push({name: "Name of the participant", value: individual.name});
  summaries.push({name: "RID", value: individual.getObservationValue('3547c858-9622-4dda-ba18-1befb7d831a3')});
  summaries.push({name: "Participant Gender", value: individual.gender.name});
  summaries.push({name: "Age", value: individual.getAgeInYears()});
  
  const household = individual.getGroups().filter((grp)=> grp.groupSubject.subjectType.type === 'Household');
  
  if(household.length !== 0){
    summaries.push({name: "Household", value: household[0].groupSubject.name});
  }
  
  const address = individual.lowestAddressLevel;
  
  if(address.type === 'Podu (Village)'){
    const parentAddress = address.locationMappings;
    if(parentAddress.length !== 0){
      summaries.push({name: "PHC", value: parentAddress[0].parent.name});
    }
    summaries.push({name: "Podu", value: address.name});
  }
  else if(address.type === 'PHC'){
    summaries.push({name: "PHC", value: address.name});
  }
  
  const status = individual.encounters.filter((enc)=> enc.encounterType.name === 'Screening' && (enc.encounterDateTime !== null || enc.cancelDateTime !== null)).length;
  
  summaries.push({name: "Screening status", value: (status) ? "Yes": "No"});
  
  summaries.push({name: "Enrolment Type", value: individual.getObservationReadableValue('9fc31cd8-b36e-466f-9c5a-906a11172863')});

  const currentDate = moment();
  const oneYearAgo = currentDate.clone().subtract(1, 'year');
  
  const hospitalization = individual.encounters
    .filter((enc) => 
      enc.encounterType.name === 'Hospitalisation' &&
      enc.encounterDateTime != null &&
      moment(enc.getObservationValue('a5827935-cf31-4dfc-9faf-f2a78a5812bf'))
        .isBetween(oneYearAgo, currentDate, null, '[]')
    )
    .slice()
    .sort((a, b) => {
      const dateA = new Date(b.getObservationValue('a5827935-cf31-4dfc-9faf-f2a78a5812bf'));
      const dateB = new Date(a.getObservationValue('a5827935-cf31-4dfc-9faf-f2a78a5812bf'));
      
      return dateA - dateB;
    });
    
  if(hospitalization.length > 0){
    const hospitalizationDetails = hospitalization.map(encounter => {
      const dateOfAdmission = moment(encounter.getObservationValue('a5827935-cf31-4dfc-9faf-f2a78a5812bf')).format("DD-MM-YYYY");
      const dateOfDischarge = moment(encounter.getObservationValue('178d803f-f9bf-4acd-a210-baeb3120027f')).format("DD-MM-YYYY");
      
      return `${dateOfAdmission} : ${dateOfDischarge}`;
    }).join('\n');
    summaries.push({name: "Hospitalization Summary", value: "Admission  : Discharge\n" + hospitalizationDetails});
  }

  
  return summaries;
};
```

### `Ashwini uat` · `subjectType[Individual]`  (2016 bytes)

```javascript
({params, imports}) =>  {
    const summaries = [];
    const individual = params.individual;

    // Fetching 'Death Registration' encounter
    const encounter = _.find(individual.encounters, enc => enc.encounterType.name === 'Death Registration');
    const dateofdeath = encounter && encounter.getObservationReadableValue('Date of Death');
    console.log('dateofdeath----->', dateofdeath);
    if (dateofdeath) {
        summaries.push({name: 'Date of Death', value: dateofdeath});
    }

    // Fetching 'Place of death'
    const placeofdeath = encounter && encounter.findObservation('Place of death');
    console.log('placeofdeath------>', placeofdeath);
    if (placeofdeath) {
        const placeValue = placeofdeath.getValueWrapper() ? placeofdeath.getValueWrapper().getValue() : 'N/A';
        summaries.push({name: 'Place of death', value: placeValue});
    }

    // Fetching 'Aadhar card number [HP]'
    const bahmniRegistrationEncounter = _.find(individual.encounters, enc => enc.encounterType.name === 'Bahmni Patient Registration');
    const aadhaar = bahmniRegistrationEncounter && bahmniRegistrationEncounter.getObservationReadableValue('Aadhar card number [HP]');
    console.log('aadhaar----->', aadhaar);
    if (aadhaar) {
        summaries.push({name: 'Aadhaar Number', value: aadhaar});
    }

    // Fetching 'Lab Results (Hospital)' encounter
    const encounter1 = _.find(individual.encounters, enc => enc.encounterType.name === 'Lab Results (Hospital)');
    const hb = encounter1 && encounter1.getObservationReadableValue('Haemoglobin (blood) [H]');
    
    if (hb && !_.isEmpty(hb)) {
        // If hb is an array, map through it and format
        const value = Array.isArray(hb)
            ? hb.map(({encounterDateTime, obs}) => (`${imports.moment(encounterDateTime).format("DD-MM-YYYY")} : ${obs}`)).join(", ")
            : hb; // In case it's not an array, just use the value as is
        summaries.push({name: "Haemoglobin", value: value});
    }

    return summaries;
};
```

### `atul_uat` · `subjectType[School]`  (1813 bytes)

```javascript
//SAMPLE RULE EXAMPLE
'use strict';
({params, imports}) =>  {
    const {summaries, individual} = params;
    const encounters = individual.getEncounters();
    
    const nearestHealthFacilityToSchool = individual.getObservationReadableValue('Name of nearby SC / PHC / CHC / DH to the school');
    if(nearestHealthFacilityToSchool){
        summaries.push({name: "Name of nearby SC / PHC / CHC / DH to the school", value: nearestHealthFacilityToSchool})
    }

    function getObservationValue(entity, observationName){

        const observation = entity.getObservationReadableValue(observationName);
        if(observation){
            return observation;
        }
        return false;
    }

    const schoolProfilePostEncounter = encounters.find(encounter => encounter.encounterType.name === 'School Profile (Pre-Post)');
    if(schoolProfilePostEncounter){
        const schoolProfilePostEncounterObservations = [
            {name: "Name of the principal", type: 'text'},
            {name: "Principal contact number", type: 'text'},
            {name: "Total number of students in the school", type: 'numeric'},
            {name: "Total number of boys in the school of standard 5th to 12th", type: 'numeric'},
            {name: "Total number of girls in the school of standard 5th to 12th", type: 'numeric'},
            {name: "Total number of students in the school of standard 5th to 12th", type: 'numeric'}
        ]
        schoolProfilePostEncounterObservations.forEach(observation => {
            const observationValue = getObservationValue(schoolProfilePostEncounter, observation.name);
            if(observationValue || observationValue === 0){
                summaries.push({name: observation.name, value: observationValue})
            }
        })
    }
    
    return summaries;
};
```
